# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""单入口导航：页面由 st.navigation 统一管理，不再手工维护多页 URL。"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import streamlit as st

APP_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(APP_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(APP_DIR))

PAGES = [
    st.Page("pages/0_概览.py", title="概览", url_path="概览", default=True),
    st.Page("pages/1_基准核算.py", title="核算", url_path="基准核算"),
    st.Page("pages/2_高排放诊断.py", title="诊断", url_path="高排放诊断"),
    st.Page("pages/3_替代方案.py", title="方案", url_path="替代方案"),
    st.Page("pages/4_成本效益.py", title="成本", url_path="成本效益"),
    st.Page("pages/5_碳会计台账.py", title="台账", url_path="碳会计台账"),
    st.Page("pages/6_路线实验室.py", title="路线", url_path="路线实验室"),
    st.Page("pages/7_电动化测算.py", title="测算", url_path="电动化测算"),
    st.Page("pages/8_车队TCO沙盘.py", title="TCO", url_path="车队TCO沙盘"),
]

pg = st.navigation(PAGES, position="hidden")
pg.run()

def render() -> None:
    layout.setup("碳慧智算")
    src = services.source()
    src_name = services.source_name()
    gate = services.gate()
    params = layout.sidebar_params(src_name, gate, current="0")

    k = services.kpis()
    mock = services.is_mock()
    counts = services.flag_counts()

    total_t = (k.get("total_kg") / 1000.0) if k.get("total_kg") else None
    speed = k.get("avg_speed")
    heavy = k.get("heavy_share")
    ratio = k.get("od_ratio_median")
    ratio_txt = ("%.2f×" % float(ratio)) if ratio is not None else "—"
    speed_txt = services.fmt(speed, 2)

    theme.hero(
        "让每一吨碳，都有据可查。",
        "从 12,167 趟物理行程、33,214 条运单关联出发，把路线、车型、成本与排放，"
        "算成一张可审计、可决策、可披露的碳账。前端只读结果表，所有数字都能追到具体行程。",
        kicker="碳慧智算 / CARBON INTELLIGENCE",
        badges=[layout.source_badge(src_name), layout.gate_badge(gate), "8 个模块", "只读数据面"],
        highlight="有据可查。",
        actions=[
            {"label": "进入决策台", "href": "/基准核算", "primary": True},
            {"label": "打开路线实验室", "href": "/路线实验室", "primary": False},
        ],
    )
    if mock:
        layout.demo_note(str(src.get("reason", "")))

    theme.marquee([
        "12,167 PHYSICAL TRIPS",
        "2,174,173.86 KM",
        "E0 " + services.fmt(total_t, 1) + " tCO2e",
        "AVG " + speed_txt + " KM/H",
        "OD BAND " + ratio_txt,
        "GATE PASS",
        "READ-ONLY FRONTEND",
        "AMAP LIVE ROUTE",
    ])

                             max_rows=30, status_col="判定")
        c = gate.get("counts", {})

        bad = [x for x in gate.get("structural", []) if x.get("status") != "PASS"]

                mime="text/csv",
            )
        if disc:

            cards.data_table(
                pd.DataFrame([{"要素": k, "内容": str(disc[k])} for k in keys]),
                title="G4 披露六要素", desc="披露口径声明", max_rows=10,
            )
            st.download_button(
                "下载披露 JSON",
                data=pd.Series(disc).to_json(force_ascii=False, indent=2).encode("utf-8"),
                file_name="disclosure.json",
                mime="application/json",
            )

    # ---------------- 运行日志 ----------------

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 008 页
"""数据读取层（A0）：前端唯一的数据入口。

规矩：
1. 只读 data/processed/；只从 src/schema.py 取常量（文件名/列名/校验），
   不调用任何核心算法（A0 规则针对算法，不针对常量——契约必须只有一份）；
2. 缺文件、缺列、文件损坏一律返回空表 + 原因，不抛异常（答辩现场不能红屏）；
3. 页面只能通过本模块取数，不允许直接 pd.read_parquet；
4. 结果表由 python -m src.pipeline 预先生成，页面里没有一行业务公式。

docs/20 §11 增补（情景演算页）：第 7/8 页是"用户输入→即时算"的演算页，
与对拍链路隔离。全 app/ 只在本文件出现一次 ``from src import scenarios``
（演算内核）；``from src import schema`` 只取列名/文件名常量，不调算法。
页面仍只 import services——自证命令见 docs/20 §4。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROCESSED = ROOT / "data" / "processed"
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:  # noqa: E402
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from src import schema  # noqa: E402  ← 只取常量（文件名/列名），不调算法
from src import scenarios as _kernel  # noqa: E402  ← 全 app/ 唯一一处演算内核（docs/20 §11）
                                       # 别名：避免与本文件 scenarios()（结果表读取）撞名

# ---- 结果文件清单 / 列契约：唯一来源是 src/schema.py（此处只做别名） ----
RESULT_FILES: Dict[str, str] = schema.RESULT_FILES

# ---- 冻结的列名：只增不改 ----
SEGMENT_COLUMNS: List[str] = schema.SEGMENT_COLUMNS

# 页面能不能出数，只看这四列在不在（缺任何一列，页面停在数据检查，不编数字）
CORE_COLUMNS: List[str] = schema.CORE_COLUMNS

SCENARIO_COLUMNS: List[str] = schema.SCENARIO_COLUMNS

#: O6 约束结论列：唯一来源是 src/schema.py（页面 3 按契约渲染，不猜列名）
CONSTRAINT_COLUMNS: List[str] = schema.CONSTRAINT_COLUMNS

#: 方案 × 线路约束表契约列（scenario_route_constraint.parquet）
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 009 页
ROUTE_CONSTRAINT_COLUMNS: List[str] = schema.ROUTE_CONSTRAINT_COLUMNS

LEDGER_COLUMNS: List[str] = schema.LEDGER_COLUMNS

DISCLOSURE_KEYS: List[str] = schema.DISCLOSURE_KEYS


def path_of(name: str) -> Path:
    return PROCESSED / RESULT_FILES.get(name, "")


def _has(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str) -> bool:
    return df is not None and not df.empty and col in df.columns


def column_ok(df: pd.DataFrame, cols: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """返回缺失的列名；页面据此显示“该表还没就绪”。"""
    if df is None or df.empty:
        return list(cols)
    return [c for c in cols if c not in df.columns]


@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def available() -> Dict[str, bool]:
    return {k: (PROCESSED / f).exists() for k, f in RESULT_FILES.items()}


@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def _read_parquet(filename: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    p = PROCESSED / filename
    if not p.exists():
        return pd.DataFrame()
    try:
        return pd.read_parquet(p)
    except Exception:
        # 没装 pyarrow 或文件损坏，都退化成空表；页面显示空状态而不是红屏
        return pd.DataFrame()


@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def _read_json(filename: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    p = PROCESSED / filename
    if not p.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}

def constraint_contract() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """探测结果表里的约束结论列。返回 {available, columns, rows}。"""
    df = scenarios()

        return {"available": False, "columns": [], "rows": []}
    cols = [c for c in CONSTRAINT_COLUMNS if c in df.columns]
    if not cols:
        return {"available": False, "columns": [], "rows": []}
    rows = df[["scenario"] + cols].to_dict(orient="records")
    return {"available": True, "columns": cols, "rows": rows}

# ---------------- D5 · λ 敏感性曲线（预计算，页面只读） ----------------
# 公式全项目唯一一份在 src/evaluator.build_lambda_curve（与 evaluate_scenario 的
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 012 页
# NPV 口径逐点一致：年节省 = 运营节省 + 年度减排量 × λ）。页面不得自建第二套。


def lambda_curve() -> pd.DataFrame:
    """λ × 回本年限全网格表（0–3000 元/t，步长 5）。缺失返回空表。"""
    return _read_parquet(RESULT_FILES["lambda_curve"])


def lambda_grid() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """网格量程信息 {min, max, step, points}，页面据此注明“当前 λ 取最近网格点”。"""
    df = lambda_curve()
    if df.empty or "lambda_yuan_t" not in df.columns:
        return {}
    values = sorted(float(v) for v in
                    pd.to_numeric(df["lambda_yuan_t"], errors="coerce").dropna().unique())
    if len(values) < 2:
        return {}
    return {"min": values[0], "max": values[-1],
            "step": float(values[1] - values[0]), "points": len(values)}


def lambda_curve_at(lam: float) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """当前 λ 的最近网格行（只做最近点选取，不做任何换算）。"""
    df = lambda_curve()
    if df.empty or "lambda_yuan_t" not in df.columns:
        return df
    values = [float(v) for v in
              pd.to_numeric(df["lambda_yuan_t"], errors="coerce").dropna().unique()]
    if not values:
        return df
    nearest = min(values, key=lambda v: abs(v - float(lam)))
    return df[df["lambda_yuan_t"] == nearest]

    return _read_json(RESULT_FILES["composition"])

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 013 页

def composition_combo(months: Optional[List[int]] = None,
                      vclasses: Optional[List[int]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """按页面筛选取预计算组合；查不到返回 {}（页面显示提示，不自行聚合）。"""
    data = composition()
    combos = data.get("combos") or {}
    all_months = [int(m) for m in (data.get("months") or [])]
    all_classes = [int(v) for v in (data.get("vclasses") or [])]
    picked_m = sorted(int(m) for m in (months or []))
    picked_v = sorted(int(v) for v in (vclasses or []))
    m_key = "all" if (not picked_m or picked_m == all_months) else ",".join(str(m) for m in picked_m)
    v_key = "all" if (not picked_v or picked_v == all_classes) else ",".join(str(v) for v in picked_v)
    return combos.get(m_key + "|" + v_key) or {}

@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def route_lab_candidates() -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]:
    """路线实验室的策略碳账：od_key → {strategy: 指标}。

    只做单位换算（kg → t）和字段搬运，不做碳/成本公式；缺失返回 {}。
    """
    df = route_candidate_carbon()
    if df.empty or "od_key" not in df.columns or "strategy" not in df.columns:
        return {}
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 015 页
    out: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        try:
            od = str(r.get("od_key") or "")
            strategy = str(int(r.get("strategy")))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        if not od:
            continue
        def _f(key: str) -> float:
            value = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r.get(key)]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
            return float(value) if pd.notna(value) else 0.0

        kg = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([r.get("kg_co2_route")]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
        out.setdefault(od, {})[strategy] = {
            "km": _f("route_km"),
            "min": _f("route_min"),
            "toll": _f("toll_yuan"),
            "co2": (float(kg) / 1000.0) if pd.notna(kg) else None,
            "opex": _f("opex_yuan"),
            "cc": _f("carbon_cost_yuan"),
            "score": _f("score_yuan"),
            "veh": str(r.get("vehicle_type") or ""),
            "load": _f("load_t"),
            "name": str(r.get("strategy_name") or ""),
        }
    return out

@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def route_lab_rows() -> pd.DataFrame:
    """路线实验室的展示表：route_od 的几何/端点 + route_select 的碳账与推荐车型。

    只做左连接与列改名，不做碳/成本计算；页面拿到的是已经算好的数字。
    缺失 route_select 时仍返回 route_od，实验室自动隐藏碳账区块。
    """
    route = routes()
    if route.empty:
        return route
    out = route.copy()
    select = route_select()
    if not select.empty and "od_key" in select.columns:
        keep = ["od_key", "vehicle_type", "load_t", "route_km", "route_min",
                "toll_yuan", "kg_co2_route", "opex_yuan", "carbon_cost_yuan",
                "score_yuan", "flag_route_est"]
        sub = select[[c for c in keep if c in select.columns]].copy()
        sub = sub.rename(columns={
            "route_km": "sel_km", "route_min": "sel_min",
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 017 页
            "toll_yuan": "sel_toll_yuan", "kg_co2_route": "sel_kg_co2",
            "flag_route_est": "sel_flag_route_est",
        })
        out = out.merge(sub, on="od_key", how="left")

    for col in ("distance_km", "duration_min", "toll_yuan", "sel_km", "sel_min",
                "sel_toll_yuan", "sel_kg_co2", "opex_yuan", "carbon_cost_yuan",
                "score_yuan", "load_t", "flag_route_est", "sel_flag_route_est"):
        if col in out.columns:
            out[col] = pd.to_numeric(out[col], errors="coerce")

    if "distance_km" in out.columns and "sel_km" in out.columns:
        out["distance_km"] = out["distance_km"].fillna(out["sel_km"])
    if "duration_min" in out.columns and "sel_min" in out.columns:
        out["duration_min"] = out["duration_min"].fillna(out["sel_min"])
    if "toll_yuan" in out.columns and "sel_toll_yuan" in out.columns:
        out["toll_yuan"] = out["toll_yuan"].fillna(out["sel_toll_yuan"])
    if "flag_route_est" in out.columns and "sel_flag_route_est" in out.columns:
        out["flag_route_est"] = out["flag_route_est"].fillna(out["sel_flag_route_est"])
    if "flag_route_est" in out.columns:
        out["flag_route_est"] = out["flag_route_est"].fillna(0).astype(int)

    if "sel_kg_co2" in out.columns:
        out["co2_t"] = out["sel_kg_co2"] / 1000.0
    return out

def kpis() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """kpi.json 是唯一来源（pipeline 预计算，口径只有这一份）。

    D4：原函数在缺 kpi.json 时从段表自算 sum/mean/占比——那正是“前端第二套口径”，
    已删除。缺文件时返回空 dict，页面显示“—”并提示重跑 pipeline，不编数字。

def flag_counts() -> Dict[str, int]:
    """四类 FLAG 的计数：唯一来源是 data_quality.json（pipeline 预计算）。

    D4：原实现在缺键时回退到段表 sum——前端第二套口径，已删除；
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 018 页
    缺键返回 0，页面显示空态而不是自算。
    """
    q = quality()
    k = kpis()

    def pick(qkey: str, kkey: str, default: int = 0) -> int:
        for value in (q.get(qkey), k.get(kkey)):
            try:
                if value is not None:
                    return int(value)
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                pass
        return default

    return {
        "rows_wide": pick("rows_wide", "rows_wide"),
        "rows_dedup": pick("rows_dedup", "segments"),
        "dup_rows": pick("dup_rows", "dup_rows"),
        "anomaly_rows": pick("anomaly_rows", "anomaly_rows"),
        "basis_rows": pick("basis_rows", "basis_rows"),
        "estimated_rows": pick("estimated_rows", "estimated_rows"),
    }


# ---------------- 文件状态 ----------------

@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def file_status() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for key, filename in RESULT_FILES.items():
        p = PROCESSED / filename
        exists = p.exists()
        count: Optional[int] = None
        if exists and filename.endswith(".parquet"):
            count = len(_read_parquet(filename))
        updated = "—"
        if exists:
            try:
                updated = datetime.fromtimestamp(p.stat().st_mtime).strftime("%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            except Exception:
                updated = "—"
        rows.append({
            "结果表": key,
            "文件": filename,
            "状态": "已就绪" if exists else "未生成",
            "行数": count if count is not None else "—",
            "更新时间": updated,
        })
    return rows

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 019 页

def fmt(value: Any, digits: int = 0) -> str:
    """把数字变好看；None 显示为横杠。"""
    if value is None:
        return "—"
    try:
        v = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return str(value)
    if v != v:
        return "—"
    if digits == 0:
        return "{:,.0f}".format(v)
    return "{:,.{}f}".format(v, digits)


def pct(value: Any, digits: int = 1) -> str:
    try:
        return "{:.{}f}%".format(float(value) * 100.0, digits)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "—"

"""第 1 页 · 基准核算：碳排了多少、从哪来、数据有多脏。

数据契约（只读，不重算，D4）：
- kpi.json：总碳 / 总里程 / 段数 / 均速 / 重型占比 / 估算占比（全量锁定口径）
- hotspots.parquet：车型碳排排行（dimension=车型）
- data_quality.json：四类 FLAG 计数、源行号可回查率、估算原因拆分
- gate_report.json：十项对拍目标（本页只展示）
- baseline_carbon.parquet：段级明细（地图 / 直方图 / 明细表直接画原始列）

页面口径：KPI 与排行榜为**全量锁定口径**，不随本页筛选变化——
筛选只影响地图、速度分布与段级明细；这样任何筛选下的数字都与闸门对得上。
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path

APP_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(APP_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(APP_DIR))

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

import services
from components import cards, layout, charts, flags, mapview, theme

layout.setup("1 基准核算")
src = services.source()
gate = services.gate()
layout.sidebar_params(services.source_name(), gate, current="1")

seg = services.segments()
if seg.empty:
    theme.hero("基准核算", "还没有结果表", kicker="01 / BASELINE")
    layout.empty_state(["baseline_carbon.parquet"])
    st.stop()

missing = services.column_ok(seg, services.CORE_COLUMNS)
if missing:
    theme.hero("基准核算", "结果表还没有按契约落盘", kicker="01 / BASELINE", badges=["数据检查"])
    st.warning("缺少字段：" + "、".join(missing))
    st.caption("当前字段：" + "、".join([str(c) for c in seg.columns]))
    with st.expander("先看看现有数据"):
        cards.data_table(seg.head(20), title="现有字段预览", max_rows=20)
    layout.conclusion("数据契约没落地之前，页面不编数字——这是硬规矩。")
    st.stop()

mock = services.is_mock()
badges = [layout.source_badge(services.source_name()), layout.gate_badge(gate),
          "数据期 2023 · 120 天", "双轨计量"]
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 022 页
theme.hero(
    "基准核算：碳从哪里来",
    "逐趟计量每一条公路运输，先看清总量、结构与数据的可信度。",
    kicker="01 / BASELINE",
    badges=badges,
)
if mock:
    layout.demo_note(str(src.get("reason", "")))

# ---------------- 筛选条 ----------------
f1, f2, f3 = st.columns([1.1, 1.0, 1.4])
with f1:
    picked_cls = st.multiselect("车型大类", ["轻型", "中型", "重型"], placeholder="全部")
with f2:
    month_opts = sorted([int(m) for m in seg["month"].dropna().unique()]) if "month" in seg.columns else []
    picked_month = st.multiselect("月份", month_opts, placeholder="全月")
with f3:
    only_est = st.checkbox("只看估算值（黄框）", value=False,
                           help="flag_estimated = 1 的行：有字段被历史/行业均值填补")
st.caption("筛选只影响本页展示，不改计算结果；结果表的生成口径在 pipeline 出口已锁定。")

view = seg.copy()
if picked_cls and "vclass" in view.columns:
    name2cls = {"轻型": 1, "中型": 2, "重型": 3}
    view = view[view["vclass"].isin([name2cls[c] for c in picked_cls])]
if picked_month and "month" in view.columns:
    view = view[view["month"].isin(picked_month)]
if only_est and "flag_estimated" in view.columns:
    view = view[view["flag_estimated"] == 1]

if view.empty:
    st.info("当前筛选条件下没有数据。")
    st.stop()

# ---- KPI 行：全部读 kpi.json（pipeline 预计算，全量锁定口径） ----
k = services.kpis()
heavy_share = k.get("heavy_share")
est_share = k.get("estimated_rows")
est_share_pct = (est_share / k["segments"]) if (est_share is not None and k.get("segments")) else None

layout.kpi_row([
    {"label": "总碳排放（tCO₂e）", "value": services.fmt(k.get("e0_t"), 1),
     "help": "12,167 趟物理行程的法定轨碳排；33,214 条运单关联仅用于分摊/追溯"},
    {"label": "总里程（万 km）", "value": services.fmt((k.get("total_km") or 0) / 10000.0, 1),
     "help": "物理行程去重后的总里程"},
    {"label": "物理行程", "value": services.fmt(k.get("physical_trips")),
     "help": "12,167 趟；33,214 条运单关联仅供分摊/追溯"},
    {"label": "物理加权均速（km/h）", "value": services.fmt(k.get("avg_speed"), 2),
     "help": "总里程 ÷ 总时长；关联口径源均速 49.51 仍作数据质量检查"},
    {"label": "重型车碳占比", "value": (("%.1f%%" % (100 * heavy_share)) if heavy_share is not None else "—")},

    {"label": "估算值占比", "value": (("%.2f%%" % (100 * est_share_pct)) if est_share_pct is not None else "—"),
     "help": "黄框标出的行；只填补、不删除"},
], per_row=3)
st.caption("物理加权均速 %s km/h（总里程 ÷ 总时长）；关联口径源均速 %s km/h 对官方 49.57，"
           "仍作为数据质量检查。KPI 为 pipeline 出口锁定的全量口径（kpi.json），筛选只影响下方地图、"
           "速度分布与明细——避免筛选后的数字与闸门对不上。"
           % (services.fmt(k.get("avg_speed"), 2), services.fmt(k.get("source_avg_speed"), 2)))

# ---------------- 对拍口径 ----------------
st.markdown("")
layout.section("对拍口径", "闸门在 pipeline 出口自动核对十个数，本页只展示，不重算")
targets = gate.get("targets", []) if gate else []
if targets:
    tdf = pd.DataFrame(targets)
    tdf["判定"] = tdf["status"]
    tdf["label"] = tdf["label"].replace({"独立路段数": "运单—车辆关联记录"})
    tdf.loc[tdf["label"].eq("运单—车辆关联记录"), "unit"] = "条"
    tdf["目标"] = tdf.apply(lambda r: services.fmt(r["expected"], 2) + " " + str(r["unit"]), axis=1)
    tdf["实测"] = tdf.apply(
        lambda r: ("—" if r["actual"] is None else services.fmt(r["actual"], 2)) + " " + str(r["unit"]),
        axis=1)
    cards.data_table(
        tdf[["label", "stage", "目标", "实测", "判定", "note"]].rename(columns={
            "label": "核对项", "stage": "出处", "note": "说明"}),
        title="G2 对拍口径", desc="pipeline 出口锁定，本页只展示", max_rows=20, status_col="判定",
    )
    st.caption("真实数据十项全过：法定物理 E₀ 2,258,438.38 kg；物理加权均速 43.14；"
               "关联口径源均速 49.51 对官方 49.57 在 ±0.1 内。演示模式跳过依赖真数据的项。")
else:
    st.info("还没有 gate_report.json——运行 python -m src.pipeline 生成。")

    cards.stats([
        {"label": "运单关联毛值", "value": services.fmt(_cur.get("t"), 1) + " t",

        {"label": "法定物理主账", "value": services.fmt(_phy.get("t"), 1) + " t",

         "tone": "mint", "help": "12,167 趟 × 当前六档因子；主账 E₀"},

         "sub": "较法定 %+.1f t (%+.2f%%)" % (
             (_vsp.get("t") or 0) - (_phy.get("t") or 0),
             ((_vsp.get("t") or 0) / (_phy.get("t") or 1) - 1) * 100),
         "tone": "info", "help": "速度 → VSP → 排放率积分；我们自己的物理轨"},
        {"label": "HYC 代理口径", "value": services.fmt(_hyc.get("t"), 1) + " t",
         "sub": "较 VSP %+.1f t (%+.2f%%)" % (

    _rec = pd.DataFrame(_audit.get("reconciliation", []))

        )

    cards.note(

        "G2 闸门已按物理主账、物理行程、物理里程和物理加权均速更新。"

    with st.expander("查看物理行程与车型组明细（审计口径）"):

            )
            st.download_button(
                "下载物理行程审计 CSV（12,167 行）",

    value = q.get(key)
    return services.fmt(value) if value is not None else "—"

"""第 2 页 · 高排放诊断：排放集中在哪，同一条线还能省多少。

数据契约（只读，不重算，D4）：
- kpi.json：重型车碳占比 / 超长途碳占比 / 同线带宽中位 / 可比较线路数 / 覆盖率
- hotspots.parquet：四维热点（车型 / 线路 / 月份 / 里程段）
- od_band.parquet：同线带宽（evaluator 预计算）
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path

APP_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(APP_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(APP_DIR))

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

import services
from components import cards, layout, charts, theme

layout.setup("2 高排放诊断")
src = services.source()
gate = services.gate()
layout.sidebar_params(services.source_name(), gate, current="2")

if seg.empty:
    layout.empty_state(["baseline_carbon.parquet"])
    st.stop()

theme.hero(
    "高排放诊断：空间在哪",
    "四维热点告诉你排放集中在哪；同线带宽告诉你同一条线换车能差多少。",
    kicker="02 / DIAGNOSIS",

)

    layout.demo_note(str(src.get("reason", "")))

k = services.kpis()

ratio_median = k.get("od_ratio_median")
od_pairs = k.get("od_pairs") or (len(band) if not band.empty else 0)
coverage = k.get("od_coverage_pct")

layout.kpi_row([

    {"label": "超长途（>500km）碳占比",

st.markdown("---")
st.caption("可比较线路 = 同一 起点网点→终点网点 至少 3 段、至少 2 种车型；"
           "倍数 = 最高因子 / 最低因子。结果表由 evaluator.py 预先生成，本页只读不重算。"
           "当前 %d 条可比较 OD、中位 %.2f×、覆盖 %.1f%% 碳排。"
           % (od_pairs, ratio_median or 0, coverage or 0))

tabs = st.tabs(["车型维度", "线路维度", "月份维度", "里程段维度"])
DIMS = [
    ("车型", "vehicle_type", "TOP 车型碳排放（t）"),
    ("线路", "od_pair", "TOP 线路碳排放（t）"),
    ("月份", "month", "月份碳排放（t）"),
    ("里程段", "dist_bin", "里程段碳排放（t）"),
]

for tab, (dim, _key, title) in zip(tabs, DIMS):
    with tab:
        hot = services.hotspots(dim)
        if hot.empty:
            st.info("热点表还没有这一维——先运行 python -m src.pipeline")
            continue
        hot = hot.copy()
        if dim == "月份":
            hot["_sort"] = pd.to_numeric(hot["value"], errors="coerce")
            hot = hot.sort_values("_sort")
            hot = hot.drop(columns=["_sort"])
        else:
            hot["_sort"] = pd.to_numeric(hot["metric_t"], errors="coerce")
            hot = hot.sort_values("_sort", ascending=False).drop(columns=["_sort"])
        top = hot.head(12)
        charts.hbar(top, "value", "metric_t", title=title, height=380)
        show = top[["value", "metric_t", "share_pct", "n"]].rename(columns={
            "value": "类别", "metric_t": "碳排放(t)", "share_pct": "占比(%)", "n": "段数"})
        cards.data_table(show, title=dim + " · TOP12", desc="热点表预计算，前端只展示", max_rows=12)

               "这里的倍数是结构性带宽，不是可立即兑现的减排量。")

- gate_report.json：方案排放对回 E₀ 的结构校验结论（原页面级自校验，D4 迁入闸门）
- kpi.json：E₀ 与汇总口径
- 侧栏全局参数：λ（内部碳价）与成本口径（赛题 / 碳价延展）

（步长 5 元/t，页面注明）。业务口径（成本构成分解 E4）已由曾广逐车组参数化，

import json

APP_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

    sys.path.insert(0, str(APP_DIR))

def _cost_detail(row) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """读 cost_components_json（只做字段读取，不做任何换算）。"""
    try:
        out = json.loads(row.get("cost_components_json") or "{}")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return out if isinstance(out, dict) else {}


def _group_count(detail: Dict[str, Any], group_id: str) -> str:
    for g in detail.get("groups") or []:
        if str(g.get("group_id")) == group_id:
            return services.fmt(g.get("vehicle_count"), 0)
    return "—"

reigns = []   # [(λ 起点, 方案名)]——冠军更替序列
for _, r in fast.iterrows():
    if not reigns or reigns[-1][1] != str(r["scenario"]):
        reigns.append((float(r["lambda_yuan_t"]), str(r["scenario"])))

if len(reigns) > 1:
    segs = []
    for i, (lam0, name) in enumerate(reigns):
        row = _curve_row(curve, name)
        lam1 = reigns[i + 1][0] if i + 1 < len(reigns) else None
        span = ("λ " + services.fmt(lam0, 0)
                + ("–" + services.fmt(lam1 - grid_info["step"], 0) if lam1 is not None else " 以上"))
        mark = "达标" if (row is not None and bool(row.get("meets_target"))) else "未达标"
        segs.append(span + " <span class='hl2'>" + cards.esc(name) + "</span>（减排 "
                    + services.fmt(row.get("reduction_pct") if row is not None else None, 1)
                    + "% · " + mark + "）")
    cards.formula("推荐翻转点（读预计算表 is_fastest 列）：回本最快的方案随 λ 易主——"
                  + " → ".join(segs) + "。")
    dial_max = None
    for lam0, name in reigns:
        if name == main_name:
            dial_max = lam0
            break
    if dial_max is not None and dial_max <= 1000.0:
        cards.note("主推成为回本最快需 λ ≥ " + services.fmt(dial_max, 0)
                   + " 元/t，在旋钮量程内；但冠军易主不改变推荐——推荐看的是 70% 达标线。")
    else:
        cards.note("旋钮量程只有 0–1000 元/t：量程内主推从来不是回本最快的，"
                   "但易主前的冠军减排率均未达 70% 目标线，进不了推荐集。"
                   "达标集合内主推回本不慢于同价方案，且减排计数更高——同价选多减，主推是 "
                   + main_name.split(" ")[0] + "。")
else:
    cards.note("在 λ ∈ [0, %s] 内回本最快者不随碳价易主——但推荐不只看回本："
               "主推满足 70% 达标线，是达标集合内减排计数最高的方案。"
               % services.fmt(grid_info["max"], 0))

    if t.get("id") in ("e0_kg", "main_reduction_pct"):

    rows = [[name, {"PASS": "✓ 通过", "FAIL": "✗ 未通过", "SKIP": "⏭ 跳过"}.get(st_, str(st_)), note]
            for name, st_, note in gate_items]
    cls = []
    for _, st_, _ in gate_items:
        cls.append("base" if st_ == "PASS" else ("fail" if st_ == "FAIL" else "skip"))

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 056 页
"""第 6 页 · 路线实验室：三个模式（高德实时 / OSM 碳基线 / 对照）。

页面结构（docs/36）
--------------------
第 6 页本来就是"路线实验室"，用户心智在这里。所以**不新开第 9 页**——
不增加顶部导航数量，不打散"五页主线 + 路线实验室 + 两个演算页"的叙事。

页面顶部是**共用的 OD / 车型 / 载重选择器**，三个模式共用，选一次就行：

============ ================================================================
模式          内容
============ ================================================================
① 实时路线     高德 JS API 实时规划：路况、4 策略、生长动画、过路费。
  · 高德       **route_lab_html.py / amapview.py 完全保留，不动一行。**
② 碳核算基线   OSM/OSRM 路线（OpenLayers）：逐 step 道路等级 / 速度 / 碳排。
  · OSM        顶部必标：**非实时 · 固定因子 · 道路等级 proxy**
③ 对照         表格对比两套口径的距离/时间/碳排/道路结构/差异原因。
              **不把两张地图硬叠**——基准不同、口径不同，叠了反而误导。
============ ================================================================

口径纪律
--------
* 浏览器端零业务计算：碳排/成本/车型全部来自 data/processed/*.parquet；
* 高德只负责"路怎么走"；OSM 基线只负责"公开路网的可复核基线"；
* **高德实时 vs OSM 基线不是同一口径**，页面必须写清楚。
"""

rows = []

    internal_delta = (s["npv_delta"]
                      + lam * s["cut_wtw_t_per_year"] * af)
    abate = s["abatement_wtw_yuan_per_t"]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""在线地图组件：高德 JS API v2.0 实时路线（路线实验室 / 决策面板共用）。

定位（docs/23 / docs/24 / docs/28）：T7 动态路线的前端渲染层。五条纪律：

  1. 只在 config/amap_js_key.txt 配置了真实 key 时启用，否则本模块返回 False，
     由调用方回退 mapview（Plotly 离线底图）——**key 缺失不是错误，是演示模式**；
  2. 浏览器端零业务计算——碳排/成本/推荐车型全部来自 data/processed/route_*.parquet；
     高德实时规划只提供几何、距离、时长、过路费；
  3. key 只在服务端拼 HTML 时注入，不出现在任何前端源码；
  4. 断网不留灰块——SDK 未加载 / Driving 超时都会在 iframe 内显示可读提示；
  5. 几何使用 AMap.Driving 实时返回的逐点路径（约 0.1 km 一点），
     不再拿 route_od.parquet 里 RDP 1500 上限的缓存折线做主视觉。

坐标一律 GCJ-02（高德原生）。本地 CITY 表是城市级近似坐标，仅用于 Plotly 离线回退。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import streamlit as st

from . import route_lab_html

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
KEY_PATH = ROOT / "config" / "amap_js_key.txt"
CODE_PATH = ROOT / "config" / "amap_js_code.txt"
PLACEHOLDER = "请至高德开放平台申请自有 key 并替换此占位符"


def _read_secret(path: Path) -> str:
    if not path.exists():
        return ""
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    return "" if text.startswith(PLACEHOLDER) else text


def load_js_key() -> str:
    return _read_secret(KEY_PATH)


def load_js_code() -> str:
    return _read_secret(CODE_PATH)


def online_available() -> bool:
    """在线模式开关：key 存在且非占位。"""
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 079 页
    return bool(load_js_key())


def _num(value: Any, default: Optional[float] = None) -> Optional[float]:
    try:
        if value is None or value == "":
            return default
        result = float(value)
        if math.isnan(result) or math.isinf(result):
            return default
        return result
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return default


def _coord(row: Dict[str, Any], prefix: str) -> Optional[List[float]]:
    """从 o_lng/o_lat 或 o=[lng,lat] 里取坐标。"""
    packed = row.get(prefix)
    if isinstance(packed, (list, tuple)) and len(packed) >= 2:
        lng, lat = _num(packed[0]), _num(packed[1])
        if lng is not None and lat is not None and not (lng == 0 and lat == 0):
            return [round(lng, 6), round(lat, 6)]
    lng = _num(row.get(prefix + "_lng"))
    lat = _num(row.get(prefix + "_lat"))
    if lng is None or lat is None or (lng == 0 and lat == 0):
        return None
    return [round(lng, 6), round(lat, 6)]


def _parse_polyline(value: Any) -> List[List[float]]:
    """把 'lng,lat;...' 或 [[lng,lat],...] 统一成点列。仅 compact 模式使用。"""
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        points = []
        for item in value:
            if isinstance(item, (list, tuple)) and len(item) >= 2:
                lng, lat = _num(item[0]), _num(item[1])
                if lng is not None and lat is not None:
                    points.append([round(lng, 6), round(lat, 6)])
        return points
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
        return []
    points: List[List[float]] = []
    for chunk in value.split(";"):
        if "," not in chunk:
            continue
        lng_text, lat_text = chunk.split(",", 1)
        lng, lat = _num(lng_text), _num(lat_text)
        if lng is not None and lat is not None:
            points.append([round(lng, 6), round(lat, 6)])
    return points
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 080 页


def _pick(row: Dict[str, Any], *names: str) -> Any:
    for name in names:
        if name in row and row.get(name) is not None:
            return row.get(name)
    return None


def _normalize(row: Any, with_poly: bool = False) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """结果表行 → 浏览器契约。只搬运，不计算业务口径。"""
    try:
        data = dict(row)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None

    o = _coord(data, "o")
    d = _coord(data, "d")
    if not o or not d:
        return None

    origin = str(_pick(data, "origin", "on", "dep_site") or "")
    dest = str(_pick(data, "dest", "dn", "arr_site") or "")
    od_key = str(_pick(data, "od_key", "od_site", "id") or (origin + "|" + dest))
    if not origin and not dest:
        return None

    km = _num(_pick(data, "km", "distance_km", "route_km"), 0.0) or 0.0
    minutes = _num(_pick(data, "min", "duration_min", "route_min"), 0.0) or 0.0
    toll = _num(_pick(data, "toll", "toll_yuan"), 0.0) or 0.0

    co2 = _num(_pick(data, "co2", "co2_t"))
    if co2 is None:
        kg = _num(_pick(data, "kg_co2_route", "kg_co2"))
        co2 = (kg / 1000.0) if kg is not None else None

    opex = _num(_pick(data, "opex", "opex_yuan"))
    cc = _num(_pick(data, "cc", "carbon_cost_yuan"))
    score = _num(_pick(data, "score", "score_yuan"))
    veh = str(_pick(data, "veh", "vehicle_type") or "")
    load = _num(_pick(data, "load", "load_t"))
    source = str(_pick(data, "src", "source") or "")
    est = bool(int(_num(_pick(data, "est", "flag_route_est"), 0) or 0))

    item: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "id": od_key,
        "on": origin,
        "dn": dest,
        "od": str(_pick(data, "od_pair") or (origin + " → " + dest)),
        "o": o,
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 081 页
        "d": d,
        "km": round(km, 3),
        "min": round(minutes, 1),
        "toll": round(toll, 1),
        "co2": (round(co2, 4) if co2 is not None else None),
        "opex": (round(opex, 1) if opex is not None else None),
        "cc": (round(cc, 1) if cc is not None else None),
        "score": (round(score, 1) if score is not None else None),
        "veh": veh,
        "load": (round(load, 2) if load is not None else None),
        "src": source,
        "est": est,
    }
    if with_poly:
        item["poly"] = _parse_polyline(_pick(data, "polyline", "poly"))
    return item


def route_lab(routes: List[Dict[str, Any]], height: int = 820,
              default_key: Optional[str] = None, lam: float = 100.0,
              candidates: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]] = None) -> bool:
    """第 6 页：路线实验室（左侧线路列表 + 右侧高德实时路线 + 平滑动画）。

    candidates：od_key → {strategy: 同策略碳账}；有值时前端切换策略会同步
    更新碳排/成本/推荐车型，不再使用“综合策略缓存”兜底。
    未配置 key / 没有路线时返回 False，调用方回退离线示意图。
    """
    key = load_js_key()
    if not key or not routes:
        return False
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen = set()
    for row in routes:
        item = _normalize(row, with_poly=False)
        if not item or item["id"] in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(item["id"])
        if candidates:
            cand = candidates.get(item["id"])
            if cand:
                item["cand"] = {str(k): v for k, v in cand.items()}
        items.append(item)
    if not items:
        return False
    default_id = str(default_key) if default_key else items[0]["id"]
    if default_id not in seen:
        default_id = items[0]["id"]
    payload = {"routes": items, "default": default_id, "lam": float(lam or 100.0)}
    doc = route_lab_html.build_doc(
        payload, key=key, jscode=load_js_code(), mode="lab",
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 082 页
        height=max(480, int(height)), title="路线实验室 · 高德实时路线",
    )
    st.iframe(doc, height=max(480, int(height)))
    return True


def route_map(routes: List[Dict[str, Any]], height: int = 560) -> bool:
    """紧凑模式：单条/少量路线的高德实时图（第 3 页决策面板用）。

    未配置 key 返回 False（调用方回退离线图）。
    """
    key = load_js_key()
    if not key or not routes:
        return False
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for row in routes[:4]:
        item = _normalize(row, with_poly=True)
        if item:
            items.append(item)
    if not items:
        return False
    payload = {"routes": items, "default": items[0]["id"], "lam": 100.0}
    doc = route_lab_html.build_doc(
        payload, key=key, jscode=load_js_code(), mode="compact",
        height=max(360, int(height)), title="路线图 · 高德实时路线",
    )
    st.iframe(doc, height=max(360, int(height)))
    return True


def route_map_single(route: Dict[str, Any], height: int = 520) -> bool:
    """单条路线的地图（评委点一条线就出一张图）。"""
    return route_map([route], height=height)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""D · 对照层：高德实时口径 vs OSM 基线口径。

为什么是"表格对照"而不是"两张地图叠一起"
------------------------------------------
用户明确要求：**不把两张地图硬叠**。这是对的，理由有三：

1. **坐标基准不同**——高德是 GCJ-02，OSM/OSRM 是 WGS-84。硬叠必然错位
   500–800 m，评委一眼就能看出"路线对不上"，反而降低可信度；
2. **口径不同**——高德侧是**实时路况**（含拥堵/封路/收费），OSM 侧是
   **freeflow 固定因子基线**。叠在一起看，"哪条更优"是个错误的问题；
3. **车型/载重不同**——现有 AMap 缓存这条线是 9.6 m 厢式货车 18 t，
   OSM 基线是 30 t 牵引车 30 t。碳排本来就不该相等。

所以对照做成**表格**：每一行一个维度，写清数值、差异、差异原因。
**明确声明"高德实时 vs OSM 基线不是同一口径"，不许读者横向比较碳排数字。**

对照对象
--------
任务书 §1 指定的对照是"现有 AMap **苏州→北京通州**缓存"。
注意起点不同（苏州昆山 vs 上海车墩），因此**距离/时长不可直接相减**，
表里必须把"起点不同"写成第一条差异原因。

数据来源
--------
* OSM 侧：``route_baseline_sh_bj_steps.parquet`` + ``summary.json``（只读）
* AMap 侧：``route_od.parquet`` + ``route_candidate_carbon.parquet``（只读）

纪律：**只读不算**。碳排数字全部来自结果表，本模块只做**差值展示**
（差值是展示级换算，不是业务口径），不重算任何一方。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import pandas as pd

#: AMap 侧对照 OD（任务书 §1：现有 AMap 苏州→北京通州缓存）
AMAP_OD_KEY = "苏州昆山集货分拣中心|北京通州分拣中心"

#: AMap 侧默认策略（0 = 综合）
AMAP_STRATEGY = 0


def _num(value: Any) -> Optional[float]:
    try:
        if value is None:
            return None
        out = float(value)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 084 页
        if out != out:  # NaN
            return None
        return out
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def pick_amap_side(route_od: pd.DataFrame,
                   cand_carbon: pd.DataFrame,
                   od_key: str = AMAP_OD_KEY,
                   strategy: int = AMAP_STRATEGY) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """从现有结果表里取出 AMap 侧对照行。

    返回空 dict 表示该 OD 不在缓存里（调用方据此显示"无对照"）。
    """
    if route_od is None or route_od.empty:
        return {}
    mask = route_od["od_key"].astype(str) == str(od_key)
    if not mask.any():
        return {}
    row = route_od[mask].iloc[0]
    side: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "od_key": str(od_key),
        "od_pair": str(row.get("od_pair") or ("%s → %s" % (row.get("origin", ""), row.get("dest", "")))),
        "origin": str(row.get("origin", "")),
        "dest": str(row.get("dest", "")),
        "distance_km": _num(row.get("distance_km")),
        "duration_min": _num(row.get("duration_min")),
        "toll_yuan": _num(row.get("toll_yuan")),
        "source": str(row.get("source", "")),
        "vehicle_type": "",
        "load_t": None,
        "kg_co2_route": None,
        "road_class_share": None,
        "step_count": None,
    }
    if cand_carbon is not None and not cand_carbon.empty:
        cmask = (cand_carbon["od_key"].astype(str) == str(od_key)) & (
            pd.to_numeric(cand_carbon["strategy"], errors="coerce") == int(strategy))
        if cmask.any():
            crow = cand_carbon[cmask].iloc[0]
            side["vehicle_type"] = str(crow.get("vehicle_type") or "")
            side["load_t"] = _num(crow.get("load_t"))
            side["kg_co2_route"] = _num(crow.get("kg_co2_route"))
            side["strategy_name"] = str(crow.get("strategy_name") or "")
    return side


def _delta(a: Optional[float], b: Optional[float], digits: int = 1) -> str:
    """差值展示：**OSM 基准 − AMap 实时**（展示级换算，不是业务口径）。
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 085 页

    符号约定：基准（OSM）在前，所以正值表示"OSM 基线比高德实时大"。
    """
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "—"
    d = a - b
    return ("%+." + str(digits) + "f") % d


def _pct(a: Optional[float], b: Optional[float]) -> str:
    if a is None or b is None or b == 0:
        return "—"
    return "%+.1f%%" % ((a - b) / b * 100.0)

                     amap: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:

    motorway_pct = _num(osm_share.get("motorway")) or 0.0

    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
        {
            "dim": "起点 → 终点",
            "amap": str(amap.get("od_pair") or "—"),
            "osm": "上海车墩集货分拣中心 → 北京通州分拣中心",

            "osm": ("%.1f km" % osm_km) if osm_km is not None else "—",

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""D6 · 基线地图的 payload 组装：steps.parquet → OpenLayers 组件入参。

纪律（docs/35 §9）
------------------
* **只搬运，不计算**：距离/时长/碳排/占比全部来自结果表，
  这里只做"表行 → 前端 feature"的搬运与着色；
* **数值一个字节都不动**：坐标基准转换只改几何坐标，
  distance/duration/carbon 原样透传；
* 底图选谁由 :mod:`app.components.basemap_align` 决定：
  天地图（CGCS2000）→ 原样；高德（GCJ-02）→ 几何转 GCJ-02。

参考：docs/35_上海北京基线_任务书.md §5.1 / D6。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import pandas as pd

from . import basemap_align, olmapview, route_visual


def _parse_geometry(value: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """step 的 geometry_geojson 字符串 → dict。失败返回 None（跳过该 step）。"""
    if not value:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return value
    try:
        geom = json.loads(str(value))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    if not isinstance(geom, dict) or geom.get("type") != "LineString":
        return None
    coords = geom.get("coordinates") or []
    if len(coords) < 2:
        return None
    return geom


def steps_to_features(steps: pd.DataFrame,
                      geom_crs: str = basemap_align.CRS_WGS84) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """逐 step 表 → OpenLayers feature 列表（按道路等级着色 + 视觉层次）。

    :param geom_crs: 目标基准。与源基准不同时**在这里**完成坐标转换
        （服务端转换，前端零计算），且只改几何、不改任何数值。
    """
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 089 页
    features: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    if steps is None or steps.empty:
        return features
    for _, row in steps.iterrows():
        geom = _parse_geometry(row.get("geometry_geojson"))
        if geom is None:
            continue
        klass = str(row.get("road_class") or "unknown")
        distance = float(row.get("distance_km") or 0.0)
        color = olmapview.ROAD_COLORS.get(klass, "#5D6A78")
        style = route_visual.feature_style(klass, distance, color)
        features.append({
            "id": int(row.get("step_index", len(features))),
            "geometry": geom,
            "road_class": klass,
            "road_name": str(row.get("road_name") or ""),
            "road_ref": str(row.get("road_ref") or ""),
            "color": color,
            "width": style["width"],
            "halo": style["halo"],
            "haloColor": style["haloColor"],
            "opacity": style["opacity"],
            "distance_km": distance,
            "speed_kmh": float(row.get("speed_kmh") or 0.0),
            "duration_min": float(row.get("duration_min") or 0.0),
            "carbon_kg": float(row.get("carbon_kg") or 0.0),
        })
    return basemap_align.convert_features(
        features, src=basemap_align.CRS_WGS84, dst=geom_crs)


def steps_to_list(steps: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """逐 step 表 → 右侧列表条目（只搬运已算好的数字）。"""
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    if steps is None or steps.empty:
        return out
    for _, row in steps.iterrows():
        out.append({
            "id": int(row.get("step_index", len(out))),
            "road_name": str(row.get("road_name") or ""),
            "road_ref": str(row.get("road_ref") or ""),
            "road_class": str(row.get("road_class") or "unknown"),
            "road_class_confidence": str(row.get("road_class_confidence") or ""),
            "distance_km": float(row.get("distance_km") or 0.0),
            "duration_min": float(row.get("duration_min") or 0.0),
            "speed_kmh": float(row.get("speed_kmh") or 0.0),
            "carbon_kg": float(row.get("carbon_kg") or 0.0),
        })
    return out

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 090 页

def route_center(steps: pd.DataFrame) -> List[float]:
    """路线中心点（WGS-84 经纬）。取所有 step 几何点的中位数，避免被长段拉偏。"""
    xs: List[float] = []
    ys: List[float] = []
    if steps is not None and not steps.empty:
        for _, row in steps.iterrows():
            geom = _parse_geometry(row.get("geometry_geojson"))
            if not geom:
                continue
            for lng, lat in geom.get("coordinates", [])[::10]:
                xs.append(float(lng))
                ys.append(float(lat))
    if not xs:
        return [116.66, 35.46]
    xs.sort()
    ys.sort()
    return [xs[len(xs) // 2], ys[len(ys) // 2]]


def route_markers(steps: pd.DataFrame,
                  geom_crs: str = basemap_align.CRS_WGS84) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """起终点 marker（高德那张图有，这张原来没有 —— 缺了就像"一条线"）。

    起终点取**整条路线几何的首尾点**，不是首尾 step 的首尾点，
    这样和路线端点严格一致。
    """
    if steps is None or steps.empty:
        return []
    coords: List[List[float]] = []
    for _, row in steps.iterrows():
        geom = _parse_geometry(row.get("geometry_geojson"))
        if not geom:
            continue
        pts = geom.get("coordinates") or []
        if not coords:
            coords.append(list(pts[0]))
        coords.append(list(pts[-1]))
    if len(coords) < 2:
        return []
    start, end = coords[0], coords[-1]
    if geom_crs == basemap_align.CRS_GCJ02:
        start = list(basemap_align.wgs84_to_gcj02(start[0], start[1]))
        end = list(basemap_align.wgs84_to_gcj02(end[0], end[1]))
    return [
        dict(route_visual.marker_style("start"),
             lng=start[0], lat=start[1], label="起", kind="start"),
        dict(route_visual.marker_style("end"),
             lng=end[0], lat=end[1], label="终", kind="end"),
    ]
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 091 页


def _segments_for_annotations(steps: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """抽出做标注用的路段序列（几何 + 名称 + 里程 + 等级）。

    只搬运 parquet 里已有的字段，不算任何新指标。
    """
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    if steps is None or steps.empty:
        return out
    for _, row in steps.iterrows():
        geom = _parse_geometry(row.get("geometry_geojson"))
        if geom is None:
            continue
        out.append({
            "step_index": int(row.get("step_index", len(out))),
            "geometry": geom.get("coordinates") or [],
            "distance_km": float(row.get("distance_km") or 0.0),
            "road_name": str(row.get("road_name") or ""),
            "road_ref": str(row.get("road_ref") or ""),
            "road_class": str(row.get("road_class") or "unknown"),
        })
    return out


def _convert_points(points: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                    geom_crs: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """把 {lng, lat} 点集按目标基准转换（只改坐标）。"""
    if geom_crs == basemap_align.CRS_GCJ02:
        out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for p in points:
            lng, lat = basemap_align.wgs84_to_gcj02(
                float(p["lng"]), float(p["lat"]))
            item = dict(p)
            item["lng"] = round(lng, 6)
            item["lat"] = round(lat, 6)
            out.append(item)
        return out
    return [dict(p) for p in points]


def build_baseline_payload(steps: pd.DataFrame, summary: Dict[str, Any],
                           layers: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
                           zoom: float = 6.0) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """组装基线地图 payload。

    ``layers`` 传入 WMS/WMTS/XYZ 底图配置；坐标基准由图层自动推断。
    """
    layers = list(layers or [])
    geom_crs = basemap_align.geometry_crs_for(layers)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 092 页
    # 给每层带上基准标记，前端切换器会显示它
    for layer in layers:
        layer.setdefault("crs", basemap_align.layer_crs(layer))

    features = steps_to_features(steps, geom_crs=geom_crs)
    payload = olmapview.build_payload(
        features=features,
        layers=layers,
        center=route_center(steps),
        zoom=zoom,
        attribution=str(summary.get("attribution") or ""),
        geom_crs=geom_crs,
        markers=route_markers(steps, geom_crs=geom_crs),
    )
    payload["steps"] = steps_to_list(steps)
    payload["summary"] = summary
    payload["road_class_share"] = summary.get("road_class_share") or {}

    # 里程刻度 + 道路名标注（服务端算，前端只画）
    # 注意 key 命名：**不能叫 "labels"** —— 前端 payload 里 "labels" 已经
    # 用来存"道路等级 → 中文名"的映射（图例要用）。撞名会让图例退化成英文。
    # 这里用 "roadNames" 存路名标注，"mileage" 存里程刻度。
    segments = _segments_for_annotations(steps)
    payload["mileage"] = _convert_points(
        route_visual.mileage_ticks(segments), geom_crs)
    payload["roadNames"] = _convert_points(
        route_visual.road_labels(segments), geom_crs)
    return payload

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""底图基准对齐层：让 WGS-84 路线安全地叠在任何合规底图上。

为什么需要这一层
----------------
第 6 页 Tab② 的路线几何来自 OSRM/OSM，基准是 **WGS-84**。
而国内合���底图的基准各不相同：

=============== ============== ===============================
底图             数据基准         直接叠加 WGS-84 路线的后果
=============== ============== ===============================
高德 / 腾讯      GCJ-02          ❌ 偏 500–800 m（肉眼可见错位）
百度             BD-09          ❌ 偏 500–800 m + 二次偏移
天地图 WMTS      CGCS2000        ✅ ≈ WGS-84，差 < 1 m，可直接叠
=============== ============== ===============================

所以**不是"不能用高德底图"，而是"用之前必须先对齐基准"**。
本模块提供这层对齐：
高德底图 → 服务端把几何转成 GCJ-02 再下发；
天地图底图 → 几何原样下发（CGCS2000 ≈ WGS-84）。

关键纪律
--------
转换只发生在**显示层**，且**只改坐标不改数值**：

* 距离 / 时长 / 碳排全部来自 parquet，**一个字节都不动**；
* 转换发生在 ``baseline_map`` 组装 payload 时，不是前端算的；
* payload 里带 ``geom_crs`` 标记，前端据此知道几何是什么基准。

这样做的两个好处：

1. 底图可以自由切换（高德 / 天地图 / 无底图），**数值口径始终是 OSM 基线**；
2. 评审时"这条线画在高德底图上却不偏"这件事本身就是个可解释的技术点。

合规
----
底图只使用持牌服务（高德 / 天地图 / 腾讯 / 百度），符合国内地图数据规范。
本模块**不引入任何 key**：高德栅格瓦片是公开端点，天地图 token 由服务端读取注入。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

from src.routing.coord import wgs84_to_gcj02

__all__ = [
    "CRS_WGS84", "CRS_GCJ02", "CRS_CGCS2000",
    "AMAP_XYZ_URL", "AMAP_CRS", "AMAP_ATTRIBUTION",
    "wgs84_to_gcj02",
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 094 页
    "amap_xyz_layer", "convert_geometry", "convert_features",
    "layer_crs", "geometry_crs_for",
]

#: 底图基准标识
CRS_WGS84 = "wgs84"
CRS_GCJ02 = "gcj02"
CRS_CGCS2000 = "cgcs2000"   # 天地图：与 WGS-84 差 < 1 m，按 WGS-84 处理


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 高德栅格底图

#: 高德栅格瓦片（公开端点，无需 key）。
#:
#: - ``webst0{1-4}``：4 个子域负载均衡，``{1-4}`` 是 OL XYZ 源的子域占位符写法。
#:   注意**不能写 ``{s}``**——OL 的 XYZ 源不认这个占位符，会全部请求到
#:   ``webst0`` 这一个主机；本项目实测踩过：写 ``{s}`` 时瓦片全白（179 B 空瓦片），
#:   改 ``{1-4}`` 后同一张图正常出内容（8.3 KB → 61 KB）。
#: - ``style=8``：标准矢量路网（含省道/国道/高速分级与文字标注）。
#:   实测 style=8 在上海 z=12 有 61 KB 内容；style=7 偏淡，style=6 是卫星影像。
AMAP_XYZ_URL = ("https://webst0{1-4}.is.autonavi.com/appmaptile"
                "?lang=zh_cn&size=1&scale=1&style=8&x={x}&y={y}&z={z}")

#: 高德栅格底图的基准
AMAP_CRS = CRS_GCJ02

#: 高德栅格底图的署名（合规要求，必须显示）
AMAP_ATTRIBUTION = "© 高德地图 AutoNavi"


def amap_xyz_layer(name: str = "高德栅格",
                   opacity: float = 1.0,
                   visible: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """高德栅格底图配置（XYZ）。

    基准是 GCJ-02，配它**必须**把路线几何转成 GCJ-02（见 :func:`convert_geometry`）。
    """
    return {
        "kind": "xyz",
        "name": name,
        "url": AMAP_XYZ_URL,
        "opacity": float(opacity),
        "visible": bool(visible),
        "attribution": AMAP_ATTRIBUTION,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 几何转换

def convert_geometry(geom: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 095 页
                     src: str = CRS_WGS84,
                     dst: str = CRS_WGS84) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """GeoJSON LineString 几何做基准转换。

    只支持 LineString（路线几何）。非 LineString 原样返回。

    WGS-84 → CGCS2000 **不做转换**（差 < 1 m），
    直接返回原对象，避免无意义的浮点噪声。
    """
    if not geom or geom.get("type") != "LineString":
        return geom
    if src == dst:
        return geom
    if dst not in (CRS_GCJ02,):
        return geom

    coords = geom.get("coordinates") or []
    out: List[List[float]] = []
    for pt in coords:
        lng, lat = float(pt[0]), float(pt[1])
        g_lng, g_lat = wgs84_to_gcj02(lng, lat)
        out.append([g_lng, g_lat])
    return {"type": "LineString", "coordinates": out}


def convert_features(features: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                     src: str = CRS_WGS84,
                     dst: str = CRS_WGS84) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """批量转换 feature 列表里的几何。"""
    if src == dst:
        return list(features)
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for f in features:
        item = dict(f)
        item["geometry"] = convert_geometry(item.get("geometry"), src, dst)
        out.append(item)
    return out


def layer_crs(layer: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """推断某个底图图层的基准。"""
    if not layer:
        return CRS_WGS84
    kind = str(layer.get("kind", "")).lower()
    name = str(layer.get("name", "")).lower()
    url = str(layer.get("url", "")).lower()
    if kind == "wmts" and "tianditu" in url:
        return CRS_CGCS2000
    if kind == "xyz" and "autonavi" in url:
        return CRS_GCJ02
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 096 页
    if kind == "xyz" and "tianditu" in url:
        return CRS_CGCS2000
    if "autonavi" in name or "高德" in name:
        return CRS_GCJ02
    return CRS_WGS84


def geometry_crs_for(layers: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """一组底图里"最严格的基准"——决定路线几何该转成什么。

    取 GCJ-02 > CGCS2000 > WGS-84 的**最严格者**：
    只要有一层是 GCJ-02，几何就必须转成 GCJ-02，
    否则那一层上的路线会偏 500–800 m。

    天地图（CGCS2000）严格来说比 GCJ-02 更"正"，但两者不能同时满足，
    所以**以 GCJ-02 底图为准**——这是唯一能保证所有可见底图都对齐的选择。
    """
    worst = CRS_WGS84
    for layer in layers or []:
        crs = layer_crs(layer)
        if crs == CRS_GCJ02:
            return CRS_GCJ02
        if crs == CRS_CGCS2000:
            worst = CRS_CGCS2000
    return worst

























碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 097 页
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""情景演算页共用组件（第 7/8 页）：假设值黄框、结论头条、节省计量条、分解图。

边界（docs/20 §11）：
- 本组件**只做展示**：所有数字由调用方从 services（→ src/scenarios.py）取得后传入；
- 颜色/字体走 theme 与 charts 的既有约定，断网可渲染（纯 HTML + Plotly 本地渲染）；
- 假设值一律黄框标注（工程师建议 3 的延伸：估算值黄框，假设值同等待遇）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import streamlit as st

from . import cards

# 分解图四组件配色（与 theme 的 info/accent/accent-2/coral 对齐）
_COMP_COLORS = {
    "vehicle_net_residual": "#6FB1FF",
    "energy": "#5EE7FF",
    "maintenance": "#FFC46B",
    "insurance": "#FF8A5B",
}
_COMP_NAMES = {
    "vehicle_net_residual": "购置（净残值）",
    "energy": "能耗",
    "maintenance": "维护",
    "insurance": "保险",
}


def assumption_note(items: List[str], title: str = "假设值（黄框=非实测，可改）") -> None:
    """假设值清单：黄框逐条列出，评委一眼能看到哪些数字不是实测。"""
    if not items:
        return
    rows = "".join(
        "<li style='margin:2px 0'>" + cards.esc(x) + "</li>" for x in items)
    st.markdown(
        "<div style='border-left:3px solid var(--accent-2);"
        "background:rgba(245,200,107,0.08);border-radius:0 8px 8px 0;"
        "padding:10px 14px;margin:6px 0'>"
        "<div style='font-size:13px;font-weight:500;color:var(--accent-2)'>"
        + cards.esc(title) + "</div>"
        "<ul style='margin:6px 0 0 0;padding-left:18px;font-size:12px;"
        "color:var(--muted);line-height:1.7'>" + rows + "</ul></div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

def verdict(text: str, tone: str = "mint") -> None:
    """结论头条（ChargeMath 的一句话结论）：大字 + 高亮数字由调用方嵌入。"""
    color = {"mint": "var(--accent)", "amber": "var(--accent-2)",
             "coral": "var(--coral)"}.get(tone, "var(--accent)")
    st.markdown(
        "<div style='font-size:19px;font-weight:500;line-height:1.5;"
        "color:var(--text);margin:4px 0 10px 0'>"
        "<span style='color:" + color + "'>■</span> " + text + "</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def savings_meter(ice_label: str, bev_label: str, ice_value: float,
                  bev_value: float, unit: str = "万元/年") -> None:
    """节省计量条（ChargeMath live savings meter）：双轨成本对比 + 差额。"""
    peak = max(abs(ice_value), abs(bev_value), 1e-9)
    w_ice = 100.0 * abs(ice_value) / peak
    w_bev = 100.0 * abs(bev_value) / peak
    gap = ice_value - bev_value
    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-savings'>"
        "<div class='cm-savings-h'><span>年度成本对比 / ANNUAL COST</span>"
        "<span>单位：%s</span></div>"
        "<div class='cm-savings-row'>"
        "<span class='lbl'>%s</span>"
        "<div class='track'><i class='ice' style='width:%.1f%%'></i></div>"
        "<span class='val'>%s</span></div>"
        "<div class='cm-savings-row'>"
        "<span class='lbl'>%s</span>"
        "<div class='track'><i class='bev' style='width:%.1f%%'></i></div>"
        "<span class='val'>%s</span></div>"
        "<div class='cm-savings-delta'>年差额 <b>%s %s</b>"
        "<span>（燃油 − 电动；负值代表电动更省）</span></div>"
        "</div>"
        % (cards.esc(unit),
           cards.esc(ice_label), w_ice, cards.esc("%.1f" % ice_value),
           cards.esc(bev_label), w_bev, cards.esc("%.1f" % bev_value),
           cards.esc("%+.1f" % gap), cards.esc(unit)),
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def npv_stacked(npv_by_scenario: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]],
                labels: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
                title: str = "NPV 四分解（元/辆）", height: int = 300) -> None:
    """NPV 分解对比：纯 HTML 堆叠条（不用 Plotly 默认图，避免工具栏/图例压标题）。"""
    labels = labels or {}
    names = list(npv_by_scenario.keys())
    if not names:
        st.caption("暂无 NPV 分解")
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 099 页
        return
    comps = ("vehicle_net_residual", "energy", "maintenance", "insurance")
    totals = []
    for n in names:
        totals.append(sum(max(float(npv_by_scenario[n].get(c, 0.0)) / 1e4, 0.0) for c in comps))
    peak = max(totals) if totals else 0.0
    peak = max(peak, 1e-9)
    legend = "".join(
        "<span><i style='background:%s'></i>%s</span>"
        % (_COMP_COLORS[c], cards.esc(_COMP_NAMES[c])) for c in comps
    )
    rows = []
    for n in names:
        segs = []
        total = 0.0
        for c in comps:
            v = float(npv_by_scenario[n].get(c, 0.0)) / 1e4
            total += v
            w = 100.0 * max(v, 0.0) / peak
            if w <= 0:
                continue
            txt = ("%.1f" % v) if w >= 7 else ""
            segs.append(
                "<span class='cm-npv-seg' style='width:%.3f%%;background:%s' "
                "title='%s：%.2f 万元'>%s</span>"
                % (w, _COMP_COLORS[c], cards.esc(_COMP_NAMES[c]), v, cards.esc(txt))
            )
        rows.append(
            "<div class='cm-npv-row'><div class='cm-npv-name'>%s</div>"
            "<div class='cm-npv-track'>%s</div>"
            "<div class='cm-npv-total'>%s</div></div>"
            % (cards.esc(labels.get(n, n)), "".join(segs), cards.esc("%.1f" % total))
        )
    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-npv'>"
        "<div class='cm-npv-h'><span>%s</span><span>单位：万元/辆</span></div>"
        "<div class='cm-npv-legend'>%s</div>%s"
        "<div class='cm-npv-foot'>总长度按最大总成本归一；四项相加 = 该场景 NPV。"
        "数值由调用方从 scenarios 结果表带入，页面只展示。</div>"
        "</div>" % (cards.esc(title), legend, "".join(rows)),
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def cumulative_chart(cumulative: List[Dict[str, Any]],
                     name_map: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
                     title: str = "车队累计成本（万元，名义口径）",
                     height: int = 320) -> None:
    """累计成本曲线：纯 SVG 展示，避免 Plotly 默认坐标轴/工具栏破坏整体视觉。"""
    name_map = name_map or {}
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 100 页
    series: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]] = {}
    years = set()
    for point in cumulative:
        year = int(point.get("year", 0))
        years.add(year)
        for key, value in point.items():
            if key == "year":
                continue
            name = name_map.get(key, key)
            series.setdefault(name, {})[year] = float(value) / 1e4
    if not series or not years:
        st.caption("暂无累计成本曲线")
        return
    import math

    xs = sorted(years)
    raw_max = max(max(v.values()) for v in series.values()) or 1.0
    step = 1000.0 if raw_max >= 4000 else (500.0 if raw_max >= 1500 else (100.0 if raw_max >= 300 else 10.0))
    ymax = math.ceil(raw_max * 1.08 / step) * step
    W, H = 900, 250
    L, R, T, B = 58, 20, 18, 32
    pw, ph = W - L - R, H - T - B

    def sx(year: int) -> float:
        if len(xs) == 1:
            return L + pw / 2.0
        return L + (year - xs[0]) / float(xs[-1] - xs[0]) * pw

    def sy(value: float) -> float:
        return T + (ymax - value) / ymax * ph

    colors = ["#5EE7FF", "#FF7A5C", "#FFC46B", "#8FB8FF", "#2DD4A7"]
    grid = []
    for i in range(5):
        gy = T + ph * i / 4.0
        val = ymax * (4 - i) / 4.0
        grid.append(
            "<line x1='%d' y1='%.1f' x2='%d' y2='%.1f' class='cm-line-grid'/>"
            "<text x='%d' y='%.1f' class='cm-line-ytxt' text-anchor='end'>%s</text>"
            % (L, gy, W - R, gy, L - 9, gy + 3.5, ("%.0f" % val))
        )
    xlab = []
    for year in xs:
        xlab.append("<text x='%.1f' y='%d' class='cm-line-xtxt' text-anchor='middle'>%d</text>"
                    % (sx(year), H - 10, year))
    paths = []
    legend = []
    for idx, (name, vals) in enumerate(series.items()):
        color = colors[idx % len(colors)]
        pts = [(sx(y), sy(vals[y])) for y in sorted(vals)]
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 101 页
        if not pts:
            continue
        d = "M " + " L ".join("%.1f %.1f" % p for p in pts)
        paths.append("<path d='%s' class='cm-line-path' stroke='%s'/>" % (d, color))
        for (x, y), year in zip(pts, sorted(vals)):
            paths.append(
                "<circle cx='%.1f' cy='%.1f' r='3.2' class='cm-line-dot' fill='%s'>"
                "<title>%s · %d 年：%.0f 万元</title></circle>"
                % (x, y, color, cards.esc(name), year, vals[year])
            )
        lx, ly = pts[-1]
        paths.append("<text x='%.1f' y='%.1f' class='cm-line-last' fill='%s' text-anchor='end'>%.0f</text>"
                     % (lx - 2, ly - 8, color, vals[sorted(vals)[-1]]))
        legend.append("<span><i style='background:%s'></i>%s</span>" % (color, cards.esc(name)))

    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-line'>"
        "<div class='cm-line-h'><span>%s</span><span>单位：万元 · 名义口径</span></div>"
        "<div class='cm-line-legend'>%s</div>"
        "<svg class='cm-line-svg' viewBox='0 0 %d %d'>"
        "%s%s%s</svg>"
        "<div class='cm-line-foot'>曲线由 scenarios 结果表带入；页面只展示，不重算。</div></div>"
        % (cards.esc(title), "".join(legend), W, H,
           "".join(grid), "".join(xlab), "".join(paths)),
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def param_slider(label: str, min_value: float, max_value: float, value: float,
                 step: float = 1.0, help_text: str = "",
                 fmt: str = "%.1f", assumed: bool = False) -> float:
    """参数滑块：assumed=True 时标签带黄点（假设值标记）。

    Streamlit 要求 value/min/max 同类：整数边界走整数，小数边界统一转 float。
    """
    if (isinstance(min_value, int) and isinstance(max_value, int)
            and isinstance(step, int)):
        value = int(value)
    else:
        min_value, max_value, step = float(min_value), float(max_value), float(step)
        value = float(value)
    suffix = " <span style='color:var(--accent-2)'>●</span>" if assumed else ""
    st.markdown("<div style='font-size:13px;margin-bottom:2px'>"
                + cards.esc(label) + suffix + "</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)
    return st.slider(label, min_value=min_value, max_value=max_value,
                     value=value, step=step, help=help_text or None,
                     label_visibility="collapsed", format=fmt)


def result_json(params: Dict[str, Any], result: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 102 页

    import json

    payload = {"params": params, "result": result,
               "note": "情景演算结果，非对拍口径（docs/20 §11）"}
    st.download_button(
        "下载本次测算 JSON",
        data=json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str).encode("utf-8"),
        file_name="what_if_result.json", mime="application/json",
        width="stretch")








































碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 103 页
"""展示组件（A1 扩展）：纯 HTML 构件，供 3–5 页复用。

约束：
- 只做展示，不算业务（页面的展示级换算在各页自己写并标口径）；
- 所有输入先 html.escape，防止数据里的尖括号把布局撑坏；
- 不引外部资源，断网可演示。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional

TONE = {"mint", "amber", "coral", "red", "info", "ok", "warn", "bad", "skip", "main", "plain"}


def _esc(value: Any) -> str:
    return html.escape(str(value), quote=True)


def esc(value: Any) -> str:
    """公开转义工具：页面拼 formula / panel_head 等富文本时，先过一遍再插入。"""
    return html.escape(str(value), quote=True)


def panel_head(title: str, desc: str = "", right: str = "") -> None:
    """面板头：标题 + 说明 + 右侧附加信息。与下方 Streamlit 原生内容配套使用。"""
    import streamlit as st
    right_html = ("<span class='r'>" + _esc(right) + "</span>") if right else ""
    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-panel-h'><span class='t'>" + _esc(title) + "</span>"
        + ("<span class='d'>" + _esc(desc) + "</span>" if desc else "")
        + right_html + "</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def stats(items: List[Dict[str, Any]], cols: int = 4) -> None:
    """统计网格。items: {label, value, sub, tone}。tone ∈ mint/amber/coral/red/info。"""
    import streamlit as st
    cells = []
    for item in items:
        tone = item.get("tone", "plain")
        tone_cls = (" " + tone) if tone in TONE and tone != "plain" else ""
        cells.append(
            "<div class='cm-stat" + tone_cls + "'>"
            "<div class='l'>" + _esc(item.get("label", "")) + "</div>"
            "<div class='v'>" + _esc(item.get("value", "—")) + "</div>"
            + ("<div class='s'>" + _esc(item.get("sub", "")) + "</div>" if item.get("sub") else "")
            + "</div>"
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 104 页
        )
    grid = "grid-template-columns: repeat(" + str(max(1, cols)) + ", 1fr);"
    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-stats' style='" + grid + "'>" + "".join(cells) + "</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def chips(items: Iterable[Any], tones: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> None:
    """一行 chip。items 为文本；tones 可给每个 chip 指定色系。"""
    import streamlit as st
    tones = list(tones or [])
    spans = []
    for i, item in enumerate(items):
        tone = tones[i] if i < len(tones) and tones[i] in TONE else ""
        cls = ("cm-chip " + tone) if tone else "cm-chip"
        spans.append("<span class='" + cls + "'>" + _esc(item) + "</span>")
    st.markdown("<div style='margin:2px 0 6px 0'>" + "".join(spans) + "</div>",
                unsafe_allow_html=True)


def kv_table(rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    """KV 表：rows: {k, v, tone?, big?}。"""
    import streamlit as st
    body = ["<table class='cm-kv'>"]
    for row in rows:
        v = row.get("v", "—")
        if row.get("big"):
            v = "<span class='big'>" + _esc(v) + "</span>"
        else:
            v = _esc(v)
        body.append("<tr><td class='k'>" + _esc(row.get("k", "")) + "</td>"
                    + "<td class='v'>" + v + "</td></tr>")
    body.append("</table>")
    st.markdown("".join(body), unsafe_allow_html=True)


def note(text: str, sub: str = "") -> None:
    """灰色小注。"""
    import streamlit as st
    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-note'>" + _esc(text)
        + ("<br>" + _esc(sub) if sub else "") + "</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    """公式条。text 支持 <span class='hl'>…</span> 高亮（调用方自行转义）。"""
    import streamlit as st
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 105 页
    st.markdown("<div class='cm-formula'>" + text + "</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)


def steps(items: List[Dict[str, Any]], active: int = 0) -> None:
    """决策闭环步骤条。items: {n, t, d}；active 为高亮下标（-1 全不高亮）。"""
    import streamlit as st
    parts = []
    for i, item in enumerate(items):
        on = " on" if i == active else ""
        parts.append(
            "<div class='cm-step" + on + "'>"
            "<span class='n'>" + _esc(item.get("n", "")) + "</span>"
            "<span class='t'>" + _esc(item.get("t", "")) + "</span>"
            + ("<span class='d' style='display:block;font-size:11px;margin-top:2px'>"
               + _esc(item.get("d", "")) + "</span>" if item.get("d") else "")
            + "</div>"
        )
        if i < len(items) - 1:
            parts.append("<div class='cm-step-arrow'>→</div>")
    st.markdown("<div class='cm-steps'>" + "".join(parts) + "</div>",
                unsafe_allow_html=True)


def trace_chain(nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    """追溯链。nodes: {t, d, tone?}，tone ∈ amber/muted。"""
    import streamlit as st
    out = ["<div class='cm-trace'>"]
    for i, node in enumerate(nodes):
        tone = node.get("tone", "")
        dot_cls = "dot" + ((" " + tone) if tone in ("amber", "muted") else "")
        out.append(
            "<div class='node'><span class='" + dot_cls + "'></span>"
            "<div class='body'><span class='t'>" + _esc(node.get("t", "")) + "</span>"
            + ("<br><span class='d'>" + _esc(node.get("d", "")) + "</span>"
               if node.get("d") else "")
            + "</div></div>"
        )
        if i < len(nodes) - 1:
            out.append("<div class='link'></div>")
    out.append("</div>")
    st.markdown("".join(out), unsafe_allow_html=True)

        + "</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,

def htable(headers: List[str], rows: List[List[Any]], row_cls: Optional[List[str]] = None,
           num_cols: Optional[Iterable[int]] = None) -> None:
    """通用 HTML 表。headers: 列名；rows: 字符串/已转义片段；row_cls: 每行附加类。"""
    import streamlit as st
    num_cols = set(num_cols or [])
    head = "".join(
        "<th class='" + ("num" if i in num_cols else "") + "'>" + _esc(h) + "</th>"
        for i, h in enumerate(headers))
    body = []
    for r, row in enumerate(rows):
        cls = (row_cls[r] if row_cls and r < len(row_cls) and row_cls[r] else "")
        tds = []
        for i, cell in enumerate(row):
            tds.append("<td class='" + ("num" if i in num_cols else "") + "'>"
                       + _esc(cell) + "</td>")
        body.append("<tr" + ((" class='" + cls + "'") if cls else "") + ">"
                    + "".join(tds) + "</tr>")
    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-table cm-table-plain'><div class='cm-table-scroll'>"
        "<table class='cm-htable'><thead><tr>"
        + head + "</tr></thead><tbody>" + "".join(body) + "</tbody></table></div></div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def data_table(df, title: str = "", desc: str = "", max_rows: int = 50,
               numeric: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,
               status_col: Optional[str] = None,
               highlight: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    """数据台账卡：替代 st.dataframe 的默认 Excel 框。

    只做展示：df 由调用方传入；这里只格式化、截断和加行状态样式。
    """
    import math
    import streamlit as st

    if df is None or getattr(df, "empty", True):
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    total = len(df)
    show = df.head(max_rows)
    numeric = set(numeric or [])
    cols = list(show.columns)
    headers = [str(c) for c in cols]

    def cell(value, col):
        if value is None:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 107 页
            return "<span class='cm-mut'>—</span>"
        if isinstance(value, float) and math.isnan(value):
            return "<span class='cm-mut'>—</span>"
        text = str(value)
        if col == status_col:
            low = text.strip()
            tone = {"PASS": "ok", "通过": "ok", "FAIL": "bad", "未通过": "bad",
                    "SKIP": "skip", "跳过": "skip", "WARN": "warn", "警示": "warn"}.get(low)
            if tone:
                return "<span class='cm-pill " + tone + "'>" + _esc(text) + "</span>"
        return _esc(text)

    head = "".join(
        "<th class='" + ("num" if h in numeric else "") + "'>" + _esc(h) + "</th>"
        for h in headers
    )
    body = []
    for _, row in show.iterrows():
        cls = ""
        if highlight and highlight in show.columns and bool(row.get(highlight)):
            cls = " main"
        tds = []
        for h, col in zip(headers, cols):
            v = row.get(col)
            if str(v).strip() in ("FAIL", "未通过"):
                cls = " fail"
            tds.append("<td class='" + ("num" if h in numeric else "") + "'>"
                       + cell(v, h) + "</td>")
        body.append("<tr class='" + cls.strip() + "'>" + "".join(tds) + "</tr>")

    top = ""
    if title or desc:
        top = ("<div class='cm-table-top'><div><span class='t'>" + _esc(title)
               + "</span>" + ("<span class='d'>" + _esc(desc) + "</span>" if desc else "")
               + "</div><span class='n'>" + "{:,}".format(total) + " 行</span></div>")
    foot = ""
    if total > len(show):
        foot = ("<div class='cm-table-foot'>显示前 " + str(len(show)) + " 行 / 共 "
                + "{:,}".format(total) + " 行；完整数据用下方下载。</div>")
    st.markdown(
        "<div class='cm-table'>" + top

        + head + "</tr></thead><tbody>" + "".join(body) + "</tbody></table></div>"
        + foot + "</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

"""图表封装：统一 Plotly 暗色风格。页面只调这里的函数，不直接写 px。

口径：所有图表只消费结果表字段（或页面已标“展示口径”的换算列），
不做业务重算；颜色走 SCALE，断网可渲染（Plotly 本地渲染，无外部瓦片）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import List, Optional

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import plotly.graph_objects as go
import streamlit as st

ACCENT = "#5EE7FF"
ACCENT2 = "#FFC46B"
SCALE = ["#08252B", "#0E6A78", "#5EE7FF", ACCENT, ACCENT2]

BASE = dict(
    template="plotly_dark",
    paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
    plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
    font=dict(size=12, color="#98A3B3", family="Inter, Segoe UI, Microsoft YaHei, sans-serif"),
    margin=dict(l=54, r=14, t=64, b=48),
    coloraxis_showscale=False,
    hoverlabel=dict(bgcolor="#11151C", bordercolor="#5EE7FF", font_size=12),
)

AXIS = dict(
    showgrid=True, gridcolor="rgba(255,255,255,0.06)", zeroline=False,
    showline=False, ticks="outside", tickcolor="rgba(255,255,255,0.15)",
)


def _finish(fig: go.Figure, title: Optional[str], height: int) -> go.Figure:
    fig.update_layout(**BASE, height=height)
    fig.update_xaxes(**AXIS)
    fig.update_yaxes(**AXIS)
    if title:
        fig.update_layout(title=dict(text=title, font=dict(size=13, color="#F3F5F8"), x=0.01, y=0.995, yanchor="top"))
    return fig

    if df is None or df.empty or x not in df.columns or y not in df.columns:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    data = df[[x, y]].dropna()
    fig = px.bar(data, x=x, y=y, color=y, color_continuous_scale=SCALE)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 109 页
    fig.update_traces(marker_line_width=0)
    st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig, title, height), width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False})


def hist(df: pd.DataFrame, x: str, title: Optional[str] = None, bins: int = 30,
         height: int = 300, x_label: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    """直方图：速度分布、偏差分布这类“看形状”的图用它。"""
    if df is None or df.empty or x not in df.columns:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    data = pd.to_numeric(df[x], errors="coerce").dropna()
    if data.empty:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    fig = px.histogram(data, nbins=bins, color_discrete_sequence=[ACCENT])
    fig.update_traces(marker_line_width=0)
    fig.update_layout(bargap=0.06)
    if x_label:
        fig.update_xaxes(title_text=x_label)
    fig.update_yaxes(title_text="段数")
    st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig, title, height), width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False})


def hbar(df: pd.DataFrame, y: str, x: str, title: Optional[str] = None, height: int = 340) -> None:
    """横向条形：排行类图表用它，标签好读。"""
    if df is None or df.empty or x not in df.columns or y not in df.columns:
        st.caption("暂无数据")

    data = df[[x, y]].dropna().sort_values(x, ascending=True)
    fig = px.bar(data, x=x, y=y, orientation="h", color=x, color_continuous_scale=SCALE)
    fig.update_traces(marker_line_width=0)
    fig.update_layout(yaxis=dict(showgrid=False))
    st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig, title, height), width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False})

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 110 页
