def bubble(df: pd.DataFrame, x: str, y: str, size: str, color: Optional[str] = None,
           hover: Optional[List[str]] = None, title: Optional[str] = None,
           height: int = 380, ymax: Optional[float] = None) -> None:
    """气泡图：x/y 定位，size 定量，color 分类着色（达标/未达标）。"""
    if df is None or df.empty or any(c not in df.columns for c in (x, y, size)):
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    keep = [x, y, size] + ([color] if color else [])
    keep += [c for c in (hover or []) if c not in keep]
    data = df[keep].dropna()
    if data.empty:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    palette = {"达标": ACCENT, "未达标": "#5C6875"}
    kwargs = dict(x=x, y=y, size=size, hover_data=hover or [x, y, size],
                  color_discrete_sequence=[ACCENT, "#5C6875"])
    if color and color in data.columns:
        data = data.copy()
        data[color] = data[color].astype(str)
        kwargs["color"] = color
        kwargs["color_discrete_map"] = {k: v for k, v in palette.items()}
    fig = px.scatter(data, **kwargs)
    fig.update_traces(marker=dict(line=dict(width=1, color="rgba(255,255,255,0.25)")),
                      opacity=0.9)
    fig.update_layout(legend=dict(orientation="h", yanchor="bottom", y=1.02,
                                  xanchor="right", x=1, title_text=""))
    if ymax is not None:
        fig.update_yaxes(range=[0, ymax])
    st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig, title, height), width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False})


def vbar_target(df: pd.DataFrame, x: str, y: str, target: Optional[float] = None,
                target_label: str = "达标线", title: Optional[str] = None,
                height: int = 340, color: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    """纵向柱 + 达标线。color 列用于按状态着色（达标/未达标）。"""
    if df is None or df.empty or x not in df.columns or y not in df.columns:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    data = df[[x, y] + ([color] if color else [])].dropna()
    if data.empty:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 111 页
        return
    kwargs = dict(x=x, y=y)
    if color and color in data.columns:
        kwargs["color"] = color
        kwargs["color_discrete_map"] = {"达标": ACCENT, "未达标": "#3D4A57"}
    else:
        kwargs["color_discrete_sequence"] = [ACCENT]
    fig = px.bar(data, **kwargs)
    fig.update_traces(marker_line_width=0, width=0.62)
    if target is not None:
        fig.add_hline(y=target, line=dict(color=ACCENT2, width=1.4, dash="dash"),
                      annotation_text=target_label, annotation_font_color=ACCENT2,
                      annotation_position="top left", annotation_textangle=0)
    top = float(pd.to_numeric(data[y]).max())
    if target is not None:
        top = max(top, float(target))
    fig.update_yaxes(range=[0, max(top * 1.18, 1)])
    st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig, title, height), width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False})


def line_multi(df: pd.DataFrame, x: str, y: str, color: str, vline_x: Optional[float] = None,
               vline_label: str = "", title: Optional[str] = None, height: int = 360,
               ymax: Optional[float] = None, labels: Optional[dict] = None) -> None:
    """多线图：color 列为系列名；vline_x 画当前值竖线（如当前 λ）。"""
    if df is None or df.empty or any(c not in df.columns for c in (x, y, color)):
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    data = df[[x, y, color]].dropna()
    if data.empty:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    fig = px.line(data, x=x, y=y, color=color, labels=labels or {},
                  color_discrete_sequence=[ACCENT, ACCENT2, "#6FB1FF", "#FF8A5B",
                                           "#B58CFF", "#7BD0FF", "#FF7A7A"])
    fig.update_traces(line=dict(width=2.2))
    fig.update_layout(legend=dict(orientation="h", yanchor="bottom", y=1.02,
                                  xanchor="right", x=1, title_text=""))
    if vline_x is not None:
        fig.add_vline(x=vline_x, line=dict(color="#F3F5F8", width=1.2, dash="dot"),
                      annotation_text=vline_label, annotation_font_color="#F3F5F8",
                      annotation_position="top")
    if ymax is not None:
        fig.update_yaxes(range=[0, ymax])
    st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig, title, height), width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False})


def payback_curve(specs: List[dict], years: int = 15, height: int = 380) -> None:
    """回本曲线：累计节省 vs 一次性投资，交叉点即回本点。
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 112 页

    specs: [{name, capex_wan, annual_save_wan, is_main}]——annual_save_wan 已含 λ 口径换算。
    """
    if not specs:
        st.caption("暂无数据")
        return
    fig = go.Figure()
    xs = list(range(0, years + 1))
    seen_capex: List[float] = []
    for spec in specs:
        capex = float(spec["capex_wan"])
        annual = float(spec["annual_save_wan"])
        if annual <= 0:
            continue
        ys = [min(x * annual, capex * 1.35) for x in xs]
        color = ACCENT2 if spec.get("is_main") else ACCENT
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=xs, y=ys, mode="lines", name=str(spec["name"]),
            line=dict(color=color, width=2.4 if spec.get("is_main") else 1.8),
            hovertemplate="第 %{x} 年｜累计节省 %{y:,.0f} 万元<extra>" + str(spec["name"]) + "</extra>",
        ))
        cross = capex / annual
        if cross <= years:
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                x=[cross], y=[capex], mode="markers",
                marker=dict(size=11, color=color, symbol="diamond",
                            line=dict(width=1.5, color="#07080A")),
                name="回本 %.1f 年" % cross, showlegend=False,
                hovertemplate="回本 %.1f 年<extra></extra>" % cross,
            ))
        if capex > 0 and capex not in seen_capex:
            seen_capex.append(capex)
            fig.add_hline(y=capex, line=dict(color="#FF7A7A", width=1.2, dash="dash"),
                          annotation_text="一次性投资 {:,.0f} 万元".format(capex),
                          annotation_font_color="#FFB3B3", annotation_position="top left")
    fig.update_xaxes(title_text="年份", dtick=2)
    fig.update_yaxes(title_text="累计节省（万元）")
    st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig, "投资回收曲线（展示口径：年度节省 = 运营节省 + 减排量 × λ）",
                            height), width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False})










碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 113 页
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""决策面板组件：动态路线 + λ 选车的业务展示（容器无关）。

**为什么有这个文件**（docs/25 §3.1「内容与容器解耦」）：
    现在它是第 3 页的一个区块，国赛期要把它升级成**独立第 6 页**。
    如果内容写在页面里，升级就得重写；写成组件后，升级只需新建一个 10 行的
    page 文件调用同一个函数——**零重写**。

**这个组件的边界**：
    - 只做展示：取数由调用方传入，本组件不读文件、不调业务接口、不做任何碳排计算；
    - 碳排/成本/λ 结论全部来自结果表（route_carbon_od.parquet / route_select.parquet），
      浏览器端零计算（docs/20 §4 前端只读铁律的延伸）；
    - 地图有则画高德、无则画 Plotly 离线图——**降级在组件内部闭环**，调用方不必写分支。

调用方只做三件事：取数 → 摆组件 → 传参。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

from components import amapview, cards, mapview

# 展示口径的车型色（与 components/theme.py 对齐）
_VCLASS_TONE = {1: "mint", 2: "amber", 3: "coral"}


def _fmt(value: Any, digits: int = 0) -> str:
    try:
        if value is None or pd.isna(value):
            return "—"
        return ("{:,.%df}" % digits).format(float(value))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "—"


def _route_to_payload(row: pd.Series, kg_co2: Optional[float] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """结果表行 → 浏览器端契约（docs/24 §4）。

    只搬运，不计算：kg_co2 若未给出则留空，由调用方从 route_carbon 传入。
    """
    polyline = row.get("polyline", "")
    points: List[List[float]] = []
    if isinstance(polyline, str) and polyline:
        for chunk in polyline.split(";"):
            if "," not in chunk:
                continue
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 114 页
            lng_text, lat_text = chunk.split(",", 1)
            try:
                points.append([float(lng_text), float(lat_text)])
            except ValueError:
                continue
    return {
        "od_key": str(row.get("od_key", "")),
        "origin": str(row.get("origin", "")),
        "dest": str(row.get("dest", "")),
        "o_lng": float(row.get("o_lng") or 0.0),
        "o_lat": float(row.get("o_lat") or 0.0),
        "d_lng": float(row.get("d_lng") or 0.0),
        "d_lat": float(row.get("d_lat") or 0.0),
        "distance_km": float(row.get("distance_km") or 0.0),
        "duration_min": float(row.get("duration_min") or 0.0),
        "toll_yuan": float(row.get("toll_yuan") or 0.0),
        "kg_co2": float(kg_co2) if kg_co2 is not None else 0.0,
        "vclass": int(row.get("vclass") or 1),
        "strategy": row.get("strategy"),
        "polyline": points,
        "source": str(row.get("source", "")),
    }


def _offline_fallback(segments: Optional[pd.DataFrame], height: int) -> None:
    """在线不可用时的离线底图（Plotly 矢量示意，不请求瓦片）。"""
    if segments is not None and not segments.empty:
        fig = mapview.flow_map(segments, height=height)
        mapview.render(fig, height=height)
    else:
        st.info("离线示意图数据未就绪——路线指标与约束结论仍可正常查看。")


def render(route_df: pd.DataFrame,
           carbon_df: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
           select_df: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
           segments: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
           lam: float = 100.0,
           height: int = 520,
           key_prefix: str = "route_panel",
           show_selector: bool = True) -> None:
    """动态路线决策面板。

    参数
    ----
    route_df    路线表 route_od.parquet（必需）
    carbon_df   OD 级路线碳合计表 route_carbon_od.parquet（可选；每 OD 一行，
                直接读 kg_co2_route_legal 列，不做任何 sum/mean）
    select_df   λ 推荐表 route_select.parquet（可选，用于展示推荐车型）
    segments    段表（可选，离线回退画布用）
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 115 页
    lam         内部碳价（元/t），仅用于展示 λ 视角
    """
    if route_df is None or route_df.empty:
        cards.empty_state(
            "路线表还没生成",
            "运行  python -m tools.route_fetch  生成 route_od.parquet（热点 OD 真路线）。")
        return

    # ---- OD 选择 ----
    options = route_df
    picked_key = None
    if show_selector and len(route_df) > 1:
        labels = [
            "%s → %s（%.0f km）%s" % (
                str(r.get("origin", "")), str(r.get("dest", "")),
                float(r.get("distance_km") or 0.0),
                "（离线）" if int(r.get("flag_route_est", 0) or 0) else "")
            for _, r in route_df.iterrows()
        ]
        picked = st.selectbox("选一条线路（网点级 OD）", labels, index=0,
                              key=key_prefix + "_od")
        picked_key = int(labels.index(picked))
    if picked_key is None:
        picked_key = 0

    row = route_df.iloc[picked_key]

    # ---- 地图：在线高德 / 离线 Plotly（降级在组件内闭环）----
    # kg_co2 取 OD 级预计算行（route_carbon_od.parquet）——不在浏览器端
    # 对段表 sum 碳排：那是碳口径求和，已由 optimizer.build_routes 落表。
    kg_co2 = None
    if carbon_df is not None and not carbon_df.empty and "od_site" in carbon_df.columns:
        sub = carbon_df[carbon_df["od_site"].astype(str) == str(row.get("od_site", ""))]
        if not sub.empty and "kg_co2_route_legal" in sub.columns:
            kg_co2 = float(sub.iloc[0]["kg_co2_route_legal"] or 0.0)

    payload = _route_to_payload(row, kg_co2)
    online_ok = False
    if payload["o_lng"] and payload["d_lng"]:
        online_ok = amapview.route_map_single(payload, height=height)
    if not online_ok:
        _offline_fallback(segments, height)

    # ---- 指标卡 ----
    est = int(row.get("flag_route_est", 0) or 0)
    source = str(row.get("source", ""))
    tone = "coral" if est else "mint"
    src_label = ("高德真路线" if source == "amap_rest"
                 else ("离线估算（haversine×1.28）" if source == "offline_haversine"
                       else "坐标缺失，未生成路线"))
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 116 页
    cards.stats([
        {"label": "路线距离", "value": _fmt(row.get("distance_km"), 1) + " km",
         "sub": "路线情景口径 L1"},
        {"label": "路线时长", "value": _fmt(row.get("duration_min"), 0) + " min",
         "sub": "高德驾车规划"},
        {"label": "过路费", "value": _fmt(row.get("toll_yuan"), 0) + " 元"},
        {"label": "路线碳排", "value": _fmt(kg_co2, 1) + " kg",
         "tone": tone, "sub": src_label},
    ], cols=4)
    if est:
        cards.note("该 OD 是离线兜底：地图只显示起终点，没有真实路径；"
                   "选一条未标“离线”的 OD 可看高德真路线。")

    # ---- λ 视角：推荐车型（来自 route_select.parquet，不在前端算）----
    if select_df is not None and not select_df.empty:
        match = select_df[select_df["od_key"].astype(str) == str(row.get("od_key", ""))]
        if not match.empty:
            best = match.iloc[0]
            carbon_gain = float(best.get("carbon_cost_yuan") or 0.0)
            cards.formula(
                "λ = <span class='hl'>%s 元/t</span> 下的推荐："
                "<span class='hl2'>%s</span><br>"
                "路线碳排 %s t → 碳价收益 %s 元｜运营成本 %s 元 → 综合得分 %s 元<br>"
                "<span class='dim'>评分式：运营成本 + λ × 碳排（展示口径，非业务重算）</span>"
                % (_fmt(lam, 0), str(best.get("vehicle_type", "—")),
                   _fmt(float(best.get("kg_co2_route") or 0.0) / 1000.0, 4),
                   _fmt(carbon_gain, 0),
                   _fmt(best.get("opex_yuan"), 0),
                   _fmt(best.get("score_yuan"), 0)))
        else:
            cards.note("该 OD 未进入 λ 推荐表（route_select.parquet 未生成或未覆盖）。")
    else:
        cards.note("λ 推荐表未生成——路线指标与地图不受影响。"
                   "生成方式：python -m src.pipeline --with-routes")

    # ---- 口径说明 ----
    cards.note(
        "**三层口径**：L0 基准账（宽表实测里程 × 因子，E₀ 永不动）｜"
        "L1 路线情景（高德路线距离/时长 × 同一因子，只做差量分析）｜"
        "L2 λ 决策（L1 + 运营成本 + λ）。三者在本页分列标注，**不混算**。"
    )









碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 117 页
"""FLAG 组件（A1）：重复 / 异常 / 口径换算 / 估算值。

约定（全项目统一，页面不许自己定颜色）：
    flag_dup        灰底   重复行（宽表保留，基准剔除）
    flag_anomaly    黄底   时间戳/速度异常（保留计碳）
    flag_basis      黄底   载重口径换算（总质量/准牵引 → 载质量）
    flag_estimated  黄框   有字段被历史/行业均值填补，hover 看 estimate_note

黄框为什么用 HTML 表：st.dataframe 的 Styler 只稳定支持背景色，边框常被网格吃掉。
所以明细表用 Styler 上底色，估算值清单另用一张带 2px 黄边的 HTML 表，答辩时一定能看见。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, Optional

import pandas as pd

    {"col": "flag_dup", "label": "重复行", "color": "#9CA3AF",

    {"col": "flag_estimated", "label": "估算值", "color": "#FF8A5B",
     "desc": "缺失值按历史/行业均值填补", "key": "estimated_rows", "scope": "unique"},
]

    "speed_kmh": "速度(km/h)", "estimate_note": "估算原因", "标记": "标记",
}

def _flag_series(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str) -> pd.Series:
    if df is not None and col in df.columns:
        return pd.to_numeric(df[col], errors="coerce").fillna(0).astype(int)
    if df is None:
        return pd.Series(dtype=int)
    return pd.Series(0, index=df.index, dtype=int)


def flag_summary(df: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None,
                 counts: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """四类标记的计数与占比。counts 来自 services.flag_counts()，优先使用。"""
    counts = counts or {}
    total_all = int(counts.get("rows_wide") or (len(df) if df is not None else 0) or 0)
    total_unique = int(counts.get("rows_dedup") or (len(df) if df is not None else 0) or 0)

    rows = []
    for meta in FLAG_META:
        col = meta["col"]
        if meta["key"] in counts:
            n = int(counts[meta["key"]] or 0)
        elif df is not None and not df.empty:
            n = int(_flag_series(df, col).sum())
        else:
            n = 0
        denom = total_all if meta["scope"] == "all" else total_unique
        share = ("%.2f%%" % (100.0 * n / denom)) if denom else "—"
        rows.append({"标记": meta["label"], "行数": n, "占比": share, "说明": meta["desc"]})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def flag_legend() -> None:
    chips = []
    for meta in FLAG_META:
        if meta["col"] == "flag_estimated":
            dot = "border:2px solid %s;background:transparent" % BORDER
        else:
            dot = "background:%s" % meta["color"]
        chips.append(
            "<span class='cm-flag'><i class='dot' style='" + dot + "'></i>"
            + meta["label"] + " · " + meta["desc"] + "</span>"
        )
    st.markdown("<div class='cm-flagbar'>" + "".join(chips) + "</div>",
                unsafe_allow_html=True)


def _row_marks(view: pd.DataFrame) -> list:
    marks = []
    for _, row in view.iterrows():
        tags = []
        if int(row.get("flag_dup", 0) or 0):
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 119 页
            tags.append("重复")
        if int(row.get("flag_anomaly", 0) or 0):
            tags.append("异常")
        if int(row.get("flag_basis", 0) or 0):
            tags.append("口径")
        if int(row.get("flag_estimated", 0) or 0):
            tags.append("估算")
        marks.append(" / ".join(tags) if tags else "—")
    return marks


def flagged_table(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    columns: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,
    max_rows: int = 200,
    height: Optional[int] = None,
    show_legend: bool = True,
) -> None:
    """带四色标记的明细表；超过 max_rows 只显示前若干行并注明。"""
    if df is None or df.empty:
        st.info("暂无明细数据")
        return

    flags = {c: _flag_series(df, c) for c in FLAG_COLUMNS}
    view = df.head(max_rows).copy()
    if columns:
        keep = [c for c in columns if c in view.columns]
        if keep:
            view = view[keep]

    view.insert(0, "标记", _row_marks(view))
    disp = view.rename(columns={k: v for k, v in RENAME.items() if k in view.columns})

    def _row_style(row: pd.Series) -> list:
        idx = row.name
        dup = int(flags["flag_dup"].get(idx, 0))
        anomaly = int(flags["flag_anomaly"].get(idx, 0))
        basis = int(flags["flag_basis"].get(idx, 0))
        est = int(flags["flag_estimated"].get(idx, 0))
        if dup:
            css = "background-color:%s;color:#111111;" % GRAY
        elif est:
            css = "background-color:%s;color:#111111;border:2px solid %s;" % (EST, BORDER)
        elif anomaly or basis:
            css = "background-color:%s;color:#111111;" % YELLOW
        else:
            css = ""
        return [css] * len(row)

    styler = disp.style.apply(_row_style, axis=1)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 120 页
    kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {"width": "stretch"}
    if height:
        kwargs["height"] = height
    st.dataframe(styler, **kwargs)
    if len(df) > max_rows:
        st.caption("仅显示前 " + str(max_rows) + " 行，共 " + str(len(df)) + " 行")
    if show_legend:
        flag_legend()


def _fmt_num(value: Any, digits: int = 0) -> str:
    try:
        v = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "—"
    if v != v:
        return "—"
    return ("{:,.%df}" % digits).format(v)


def estimated_panel(df: pd.DataFrame, n: int = 8) -> None:
    """估算值清单：每行套 2px 黄框，明确告诉评委“这是估算值”。"""
    if df is None or df.empty or "flag_estimated" not in df.columns:
        return
    flag = _flag_series(df, "flag_estimated")
    est = df[flag == 1]
    total = len(df)
    if est.empty:
        st.success("本页没有估算值——所有字段都来自原始表。")
        return

    share = 100.0 * len(est) / max(total, 1)
    head = ("<div class='cm-est-wrap'><div class='cm-est-head'>🟡 黄框 = 估算值"
            "<span>共 %d 行，占本页 %.1f%%；只填补、不删除，flag_estimated=1</span></div>"
            % (len(est), share))
    body = ["<table class='cm-est-table'><thead><tr>"
            "<th>运单号</th><th>线路</th><th>里程(km)</th><th>碳排(kg)</th>"
            "<th>估算原因</th></tr></thead><tbody>"]
    for _, row in est.head(n).iterrows():
        body.append(
            "<tr class='est'>"
            "<td>" + html.escape(str(row.get("waybill_no", "—"))) + "</td>"
            "<td>" + html.escape(str(row.get("od_pair", "—"))) + "</td>"
            "<td>" + _fmt_num(row.get("dist_km", None), 1) + "</td>"
            "<td>" + _fmt_num(row.get("kg_co2", None), 1) + "</td>"
            "<td class='reason'>" + html.escape(str(row.get("estimate_note", "—"))) + "</td>"
            "</tr>")
    body.append("</tbody></table>")
    foot = ("<div class='cm-est-foot'>填补规则：同线历史均值 → 行业绕行系数 → 全样本中位数；"
            "车型找不到按载重推断；发票匹配不上按同类别历史均价。所有填补都留痕。</div></div>")
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 121 页
    st.markdown(head + "".join(body) + foot, unsafe_allow_html=True)
    if len(est) > n:
        st.caption("仅列出前 %d 行；完整清单见下方明细表（筛“估算”）。" % n)

"""版式组件（A1）：顶部导航、页头、指标卡、结论带、空状态、全局参数。"""

def setup(title: str, icon: str = "📦") -> None:
    """页面配置。每个页面第一行调用，必须在其他 st 调用之前。"""
    st.set_page_config(
        page_title=title,
        page_icon=icon,
        layout="wide",
        initial_sidebar_state="collapsed",
    )
    theme.inject()


def page_header(title: str, question: Optional[str] = None, badge: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    """页头：标题 + 一句话问题 + 右上角状态标签。"""
    left, right = st.columns([0.8, 0.2])
    with left:
        st.markdown("## " + title)
        if question:
            st.caption(question)
    with right:
        if badge:
            st.markdown(
                "<div style='text-align:right;color:#98A3B3;font-size:11px;padding-top:14px'>"
                + badge
                + "</div>",
                unsafe_allow_html=True,
            )
    st.markdown("---")


def kpi_row(items: List[Dict[str, Any]], per_row: int = 4) -> None:
    """一行指标卡。items 里每项：{label, value, help}。"""
    if not items:
        return
    for start in range(0, len(items), per_row):
        chunk = items[start:start + per_row]
        cols = st.columns(len(chunk))
        for col, item in zip(cols, chunk):
            with col:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 123 页
                st.metric(
                    label=str(item.get("label", "")),
                    value=str(item.get("value", "—")),
                    help=item.get("help"),
                )


def section(title: str, desc: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    theme.section(title, desc or "")


def conclusion(text: str) -> None:
    """页脚结论带。每页必须有一句，答辩照着念。"""
    theme.conclusion(text)


def demo_note(reason: str = "") -> None:
    theme.demo_note(reason)


def source_badge(source: str) -> str:
    return "实测数据" if source == "real" else "演示数据"


def gate_badge(gate: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    if not gate:
        return "闸门未运行"
    if gate.get("mode") == "error":
        return "闸门：流水线中断"
    if gate.get("passed"):
        return "闸门通过" if gate.get("mode") == "real" else "闸门通过（演示）"
    return "闸门未通过"


def empty_state(need: List[str]) -> None:
    """缺表时的空状态：不报错，告诉对方跑什么。"""
    st.warning("这张表还没生成——先运行  python -m src.pipeline")
    if need:
        st.caption("缺少：" + " / ".join(need))

        if changed and (has_query or float(lam) != 100.0 or str(basis) != "赛题口径"):
            st.query_params["lam"] = want_lam
            st.query_params["basis"] = want_basis
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {"lam": float(lam), "basis": basis}

"""地图组件：离线可用的地理可视化。

为什么不用 mapbox 瓦片：答辩现场可能断网，瓦片加载不出来就是一块灰底。
这里用 Plotly 内置 geo（矢量底图，不联网），保证断网也能演示。
路线预览 route_preview() 则刻意不画假底图，改成“控制台示意图”：只画路线形状、
端点与指标，避免 natural earth 粗多边形在放大后比折线还抽象。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any, Optional

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import plotly.graph_objects as go
import streamlit as st

ACCENT = "#5EE7FF"
ACCENT2 = "#FFC46B"
SCALE = ["#0E3B2E", "#1C8F6B", ACCENT, ACCENT2, "#FF8A5B"]

GEO_LAYOUT = dict(
    showcountries=True, countrycolor="#1E2A36", countrywidth=0.6,
    showcoastlines=True, coastlinecolor="#1E2A36", coastlinewidth=0.6,
    showland=True, landcolor="#0F1620",
    showocean=True, oceancolor="#0A0E13",
    showlakes=False, showframe=False,
    bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
)


def _base_layout(fig: go.Figure, height: int) -> go.Figure:
    fig.update_layout(
        template="plotly_dark",
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        margin=dict(l=0, r=0, t=6, b=0),
        height=height,
        coloraxis_colorbar=dict(
            thickness=9, len=0.55,
            title=dict(text="tCO₂e", font=dict(size=11)),
            tickfont=dict(size=10),
        ),
    )
    return fig


def bubble_map(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 126 页
    lat: str = "origin_lat",
    lon: str = "origin_lon",
    city: str = "origin",
    month: str = "month",
    value: str = "kg_co2",
    animate: bool = True,
    height: int = 560,
) -> Optional[go.Figure]:
    """城市级碳排气泡图；有 month 列就带月份播放轴。"""
    need = [lat, lon, city, value]
    if df is None or df.empty or any(c not in df.columns for c in need):
        return None

    agg = df.groupby([lat, lon, city] + ([month] if animate and month in df.columns else []),
                     as_index=False)[value].sum()
    agg["t"] = agg[value] / 1000.0
    agg = agg[agg["t"] > 0]

    kwargs = dict(
        lat=lat, lon=lon, size="t", color="t",
        hover_name=city, hover_data={"t": ":.1f", lat: False, lon: False},
        color_continuous_scale=SCALE, size_max=46,
        projection="natural earth", labels={"t": "碳排放(t)"},
    )
    if animate and month in agg.columns:
        agg[month] = agg[month].apply(lambda m: "%02d 月" % int(m))
        fig = px.scatter_geo(agg, animation_frame=month, **kwargs)
        if fig.layout.updatemenus:
            fig.layout.updatemenus[0].buttons[0].args[1]["frame"] = {"duration": 700, "redraw": True}
            fig.layout.updatemenus[0].buttons[0].args[1]["transition"] = {"duration": 400}
            fig.layout.updatemenus[0].pad = {"r": 10, "t": 4}
            fig.layout.updatemenus[0].bgcolor = "rgba(19,26,35,0.9)"
    else:
        fig = px.scatter_geo(agg, **kwargs)

    fig.update_geos(**GEO_LAYOUT, fitbounds="locations")
    return _base_layout(fig, height)


def _arc(lat1: float, lon1: float, lat2: float, lon2: float, bend: float = 0.16, n: int = 26):
    dx, dy = lon2 - lon1, lat2 - lat1
    dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy) ** 0.5 or 1.0
    ux, uy = -dy / dist, dx / dist
    lons, lats = [], []
    for i in range(n + 1):
        t = i / n
        off = bend * dist * (1 - abs(2 * t - 1))
        lons.append(lon1 + dx * t + ux * off)
        lats.append(lat1 + dy * t + uy * off)
    return lons, lats
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 127 页


def flow_map(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    lat_o: str = "origin_lat",
    lon_o: str = "origin_lon",
    lat_d: str = "dest_lat",
    lon_d: str = "dest_lon",
    city_o: str = "origin",
    city_d: str = "dest",
    value: str = "kg_co2",
    top_n: int = 24,
    height: int = 560,
) -> Optional[go.Figure]:
    """OD 流向图：弧线宽度 = 该线路碳排，颜色按重/中/轻大类。"""
    need = [lat_o, lon_o, lat_d, lon_d, value]
    if df is None or df.empty or any(c not in df.columns for c in need):
        return None

    agg = (df.groupby([city_o, city_d, lat_o, lon_o, lat_d, lon_d], as_index=False)[value]
             .sum().sort_values(value, ascending=False).head(top_n))
    if agg.empty:
        return None
    vmax = agg[value].max() or 1.0

    fig = go.Figure()
    for _, r in agg.iterrows():
        lons, lats = _arc(r[lat_o], r[lon_o], r[lat_d], r[lon_d])
        ratio = float(r[value]) / vmax
        fig.add_trace(go.Scattergeo(
            lon=lons, lat=lats, mode="lines",
            line=dict(width=0.6 + 5.0 * ratio,
                      color="rgba(45,212,167," + ("%.2f" % (0.18 + 0.62 * ratio)) + ")"),
            opacity=1.0,
            hoverinfo="text",
            text="%s → %s<br>%.1f tCO₂e" % (r[city_o], r[city_d], float(r[value]) / 1000.0),
            showlegend=False,
        ))
        fig.add_trace(go.Scattergeo(
            lon=[r[lon_d]], lat=[r[lat_d]], mode="markers",
            marker=dict(size=4 + 8 * ratio, color=ACCENT2, opacity=0.85),
            hoverinfo="skip", showlegend=False,
        ))

    cities = df.groupby([city_o, lat_o, lon_o], as_index=False)[value].sum()
    fig.add_trace(go.Scattergeo(
        lon=cities[lon_o], lat=cities[lat_o], mode="markers+text",
        text=cities[city_o], textposition="top center",
        textfont=dict(size=10, color="#8A97A6"),
        marker=dict(size=5, color="#F3F5F8", opacity=0.75),
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 128 页
        hoverinfo="skip", showlegend=False,
    ))

    fig.update_geos(**GEO_LAYOUT, fitbounds="locations")
    return _base_layout(fig, height)


def route_preview(row: Any, height: int = 600) -> Optional[go.Figure]:
    """离线路线示意图：不画假底图，只画“路线形状 + 端点 + 指标”。

    为什么不用 scattergeo：Plotly 的 natural earth 底图在国家/区域尺度是一块块
    粗多边形，放大后既没有道路也没有城市细节，看起来比折线还抽象。
    断网时诚实地画一张“控制台示意图”，比假装是地图更好。

    row 可以是 pandas Series / dict：需要 origin、dest、o_lng、o_lat、d_lng、d_lat、
    polyline（'lng,lat;...'）。缓存几何是抽稀过的，只用于兜底。
    """
    if row is None:
        return None
    get = row.get if hasattr(row, "get") else (lambda k, default=None: default)
    origin = str(get("origin") or get("on") or "起点")
    dest = str(get("dest") or get("dn") or "终点")
    try:
        o_lng, o_lat = float(get("o_lng")), float(get("o_lat"))
        d_lng, d_lat = float(get("d_lng")), float(get("d_lat"))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    if not all(abs(v) > 1e-9 for v in (o_lng, o_lat, d_lng, d_lat)):
        return None

    lons, lats = [], []
    raw = get("polyline") or ""
    if isinstance(raw, str) and raw:
        for chunk in raw.split(";"):
            if "," not in chunk:
                continue
            lng_text, lat_text = chunk.split(",", 1)
            try:
                lons.append(float(lng_text))
                lats.append(float(lat_text))
            except ValueError:
                continue
    if len(lons) < 2:
        lons, lats = [o_lng, d_lng], [o_lat, d_lat]

    mean_lat = sum(lats) / len(lats)
    xs = [lng * math.cos(math.radians(mean_lat)) for lng in lons]
    ys = list(lats)
    span_x = max(xs) - min(xs) or 1e-6
    span_y = max(ys) - min(ys) or 1e-6
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 129 页
    scale = min(82.0 / span_x, 78.0 / span_y)
    mid_x = (max(xs) + min(xs)) / 2.0
    mid_y = (max(ys) + min(ys)) / 2.0
    px = [50.0 + (x - mid_x) * scale for x in xs]
    py = [50.0 + (y - mid_y) * scale for y in ys]

    fig = go.Figure()
    for v in range(10, 100, 10):
        fig.add_shape(type="line", x0=v, y0=4, x1=v, y1=96,
                      line=dict(color="rgba(255,255,255,0.035)", width=1), layer="below")
        fig.add_shape(type="line", x0=4, y0=v, x1=96, y1=v,
                      line=dict(color="rgba(255,255,255,0.035)", width=1), layer="below")

    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
        x=px, y=py, mode="lines",
        line=dict(width=12, color="rgba(45,212,167,0.10)"),
        hoverinfo="skip", showlegend=False))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
        x=px, y=py, mode="lines",
        line=dict(width=3.2, color=ACCENT),
        hoverinfo="text",
        text="%s → %s<br>离线缓存几何" % (origin, dest),
        showlegend=False))
    step = max(1, len(px) // 12)
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
        x=px[::step] + [px[-1]], y=py[::step] + [py[-1]], mode="markers",
        marker=dict(size=4, color="#7CF5D3", line=dict(width=0)),
        hoverinfo="skip", showlegend=False))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
        x=[px[0], px[-1]], y=[py[0], py[-1]], mode="markers+text",
        marker=dict(size=[15, 15], color=[ACCENT, ACCENT2],
                    line=dict(width=2, color="#07120F")),
        text=["A · " + origin, "B · " + dest],
        textposition=["top right", "bottom right"],
        textfont=dict(size=10.5, color="#F3F5F8"),
        hoverinfo="skip", showlegend=False))

    km = get("distance_km") or get("km") or 0.0
    mins = get("duration_min") or get("min") or 0.0
    toll = get("toll_yuan") or get("toll") or 0.0
    co2 = get("co2_t") or 0.0
    try:
        km_s = "{:,.1f}".format(float(km))
        min_s = "{:,.0f}".format(float(mins))
        toll_s = "{:,.0f}".format(float(toll))
        co2_s = "{:.2f}".format(float(co2))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        km_s, min_s, toll_s, co2_s = "—", "—", "—", "—"

    fig.add_annotation(
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 130 页
        x=0.025, y=0.975, xref="paper", yref="paper", xanchor="left", yanchor="top",
        align="left", showarrow=False,
        text="<b>OFFLINE SCHEMATIC</b>　离线示意<br>"
             "<span style='font-size:11px;color:#8A97A6'>缓存几何 · 非实时 · 无在线底图</span>",
        font=dict(size=11, color="#FFC46B"), bgcolor="rgba(8,12,17,0.55)",
        bordercolor="rgba(245,200,107,0.28)", borderwidth=1, borderpad=8)
    fig.add_annotation(
        x=0.975, y=0.03, xref="paper", yref="paper", xanchor="right", yanchor="bottom",
        align="right", showarrow=False,
        text="{:s} km　·　{:s} min　·　{:s} 元　·　{:s} tCO₂e".format(km_s, min_s, toll_s, co2_s),
        font=dict(size=11, color="#F3F5F8", family="Consolas, monospace"),

    fig.update_xaxes(visible=False, range=[0, 100], fixedrange=True)
    fig.update_yaxes(visible=False, range=[0, 100], fixedrange=True)
    fig.update_layout(
        height=height, margin=dict(l=0, r=0, t=0, b=0),
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)", plot_bgcolor="rgba(10,15,21,0.92)",
        showlegend=False, hovermode="closest",
    )
    return fig


def render(fig: Optional[go.Figure], height: int = 560) -> None:
    if fig is None:
        st.info("地图数据还没就绪：宽表需要 origin_lat / origin_lon / dest_lat / dest_lon 四列。")
        return
    st.plotly_chart(fig, width="stretch",
                    config={"displayModeBar": False, "scrollZoom": False})

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""OpenLayers 地图组件：支持 WMS / WMTS / XYZ 底图 + 矢量叠加。

定位
----
第 6 页「路线实验室」的**第二种地图引擎**，与 :mod:`app.components.amapview`
（高德实时路线）并存：

* ``amapview``  → 高德 JS API，**实时**路况、贴公路动画、策略切换（**完全保留不动**）
* ``olmapview``  → OpenLayers，**非实时** OSM/OSRM 基线路线，WMS/WMTS/XYZ 底图，逐 step 可查

底图与坐标基准（**本组件最容易踩的坑，必须理解再用**）
---------------------------------------------------
路线几何来自 OSM/OSRM，基准是 **WGS-84**；不同底图基准不同：

=============== ============== ==========================================
底图             数据基准         怎么处理
=============== ============== ==========================================
天地图 WMTS      CGCS2000        直接叠，差 < 1 m，无可见偏移
高德栅格 XYZ     GCJ-02          **必须**先把几何转成 GCJ-02（否则偏 500–800 m）
=============== ============== ==========================================

关键：**不是"不能用高德底图"，而是"用之前必须先对齐基准"**。
转换由服务端在 :mod:`app.components.basemap_align` 完成，
**只改坐标不改数值**——距离/时长/碳排一个字节都不动。
详见该模块 docstring。

合规
----
按国内地图数据规范，底图只使用**持牌服务**：天地图 / 高德 / 百度 / 腾讯。
默认配置写天地图 WMTS（需 token，占位符模式）；
若未配置 token，则退到**高德栅格**（公开端点，无需 key）。

断网与缺 token
--------------
答辩现场可能断网。本组件的三条纪律（对齐 amapview）：

1. **缺底图 → 不留灰块**，在 iframe 内显示可读提示；
2. **路线矢量永远画得出来**——它来自本地 parquet，不依赖网络；
3. **key 只在服务端拼 HTML 时注入**，不出现在任何前端源码与文档里。

CDN 版本固定
------------
OpenLayers 走 CDN，但**版本必须钉死**：``ol@10.6.1``。
不用 ``@latest``——大版本会改 API（比如 v7 的 ``ol/source/ImageWMS`` 参数、
v10 的 ``VectorLayer`` 样式写法），答辩当天拉到一个新版本就可能白屏。

参考：docs/35_上海北京基线_任务书.md D6；docs/36 交付说明。
"""

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 132 页

#: 天地图 token 占位符（未申请时文件里放这个）
TIANDITU_PLACEHOLDER = "请至天地图开放平台申请自有 token 并替换此占位符"

#: OpenLayers CDN（版本钉死，禁止 @latest）
OL_JS_URL = "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/ol@10.6.1/dist/ol.js"
OL_CSS_URL = "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/ol@10.6.1/ol.css"

#: 道路等级 → 颜色（与前端 Carbon Tide 配色一致）
ROAD_COLORS: Dict[str, str] = {
    "motorway": "#FF8A5B",
    "trunk": "#FFC46B",
    "secondary": "#5EE7FF",
    "urban": "#2DD4A7",
    "local": "#8A97A6",
    "ramp": "#C77DFF",

    "motorway": "高速",
    "trunk": "国道",
    "secondary": "省道",
    "urban": "城市道路",
    "local": "支路",
    "ramp": "匝道",
    "unknown": "未分类",
}


def load_tianditu_token() -> str:
    """读天地图 token；未配置或仍是占位符时返回空串。

    空串 = 不可用 = 组件回退"高德栅格底图"，**不是错误**。
    """
    if not TIANDITU_TOKEN_PATH.exists():
        return ""
    text = TIANDITU_TOKEN_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 133 页
    if not text or text.startswith(TIANDITU_PLACEHOLDER):
        return ""
    return text


def tianditu_available() -> bool:
    """天地图底图是否可用。"""
    return bool(load_tianditu_token())


def build_layer_config(layers: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """规范化图层配置（供前端 JS 消费）。

    每层至少要有 ``kind``（wms / wmts / xyz）与 ``url``。
    天地图 WMTS 的 URL 模板在服务端拼好，token 不进前端源码。
    """
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for layer in layers or []:
        kind = str(layer.get("kind", "")).lower()
        if kind not in ("wms", "wmts", "xyz"):
            continue
        item: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "kind": kind,
            "url": str(layer.get("url", "")),
            "name": str(layer.get("name", kind)),
            "visible": bool(layer.get("visible", True)),
            "opacity": float(layer.get("opacity", 1.0)),
        }
        if layer.get("attribution"):
            item["attribution"] = str(layer["attribution"])
        if kind == "wms":
            item["layers"] = str(layer.get("layers", ""))
            item["format"] = str(layer.get("format", "image/png"))
            item["transparent"] = bool(layer.get("transparent", True))
            item["version"] = str(layer.get("version", "1.3.0"))
            if layer.get("params"):
                item["params"] = dict(layer["params"])
        elif kind == "wmts":
            item["layer"] = str(layer.get("layer", ""))
            item["matrixSet"] = str(layer.get("matrixSet", "w"))
            item["format"] = str(layer.get("format", "tiles"))
            item["style"] = str(layer.get("style", "default"))
            item["version"] = str(layer.get("version", "1.0.0"))
            item["tileSize"] = int(layer.get("tileSize", 256))
            item["originX"] = float(layer.get("originX", -180.0))
            item["originY"] = float(layer.get("originY", -90.0))
            item["resolutions"] = list(layer.get("resolutions", []))
        out.append(item)
    return out

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 134 页

def default_tianditu_layers(token: str, kind: str = "vec_w") -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """天地图 WMTS 图层配置。

    ``kind``: ``vec_w``（矢量路网）/ ``img_w``（影像）/ ``cva_w``（影像注记）。

    天地图 WMTS 用 ``w`` 矩阵集（Web Mercator，EPSG:3857），
    与 OpenLayers 默认投影一致，**不需要任何坐标转换**。
    """
    if not token:
        return []
    layer_id = {"vec_w": "vec", "img_w": "img", "cva_w": "cva"}.get(kind, "vec")
    return [{
        "kind": "wmts",
        "name": "天地图",
        "layer": layer_id,
        "matrixSet": "w",
        "format": "tiles",
        "style": "default",
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "tileSize": 256,
        "url": ("https://t{s}.tianditu.gov.cn/%s/wmts?SERVICE=WMTS&REQUEST=GetTile"
                "&VERSION=1.0.0&LAYER=%s&STYLE=default&TILEMATRIXSET=w"
                "&FORMAT=tiles&TILEMATRIX={z}&TILEROW={y}&TILECOL={x}&tk=%s"
                % (kind, layer_id, token)),
    }]


def build_payload(features: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                  layers: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                  center: Sequence[float],
                  zoom: float = 6.0,
                  attribution: str = "",
                  geom_crs: str = "wgs84",
                  markers: Optional[Sequence[Dict[str, Any]]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """组装前端 payload（只放展示数据，不放任何 key）。"""
    return {
        "features": list(features),
        "layers": build_layer_config(layers),
        "center": [float(center[0]), float(center[1])],
        "zoom": float(zoom),
        "attribution": attribution,
        "colors": ROAD_COLORS,
        "labels": ROAD_LABELS,
        "geomCrs": geom_crs,
        "markers": list(markers or []),
    }

  function $(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
  function esc(s){var d=document.createElement('div');d.textContent=(s==null?'':String(s));return d.innerHTML;}

  function showFallback(msg, badge){
    $("fallback").classList.add("on");

  function hideFallback(){ $("fallback").classList.remove("on"); }

  function fmt(v, d){ if(v==null||isNaN(v)) return "—"; return Number(v).toFixed(d==null?1:d); }

  function makeStyle(width, color, opacity){
    return new ol.style.Style({
      stroke: new ol.style.Stroke({color: color, width: width, opacity: (opacity==null?1:opacity)})
    });
  }

  function makeFeature(props){
    var geom = props.geometry;
    // OL v10 移除了 ol.geom.GeoJSON（v7 起就没了），只有 ol.format.GeoJSON。
    // 几何坐标已是目标基准的经纬（服务端转好），再经 ol.proj.fromLonLat 投影到 EPSG:3857。
    var coords = (geom && geom.coordinates) ? geom.coordinates : [];
    var projected = [];
    for (var i=0;i<coords.length;i++){
      projected.push(ol.proj.fromLonLat([coords[i][0], coords[i][1]]));
    }
    var f = new ol.Feature({
      geometry: new ol.geom.LineString(projected),
      props: props
    });
    return f;
  }

  function buildVector(){
    feats = PAYLOAD.features || [];
    layerById = {}; haloById = {};
    var coreSrc = new ol.source.Vector();
    var haloSrc = new ol.source.Vector();
    for (var i=0;i<feats.length;i++){
      var p = feats[i];
      var f = makeFeature(p);
      // halo：深色描边打底，保证亮色底图上路线依然清晰
      var hf = new ol.Feature({geometry: f.getGeometry(), props: p});
      hf.setStyle(makeStyle(p.halo||((p.width||3)+1.5), p.haloColor||"rgba(4,9,14,.55)", 0.8));
      f.setStyle(makeStyle(p.width||3, p.color||"#5EE7FF", p.opacity==null?0.95:p.opacity));
      coreSrc.addFeature(f);
      haloSrc.addFeature(hf);
      layerById[p.id] = f;
      haloById[p.id] = hf;
      shown[p.id] = true;
    }
    haloLayer = new ol.layer.Vector({source: haloSrc, declutter:false});
    coreLayer = new ol.layer.Vector({source: coreSrc, declutter:false});
    map.addLayer(haloLayer);
    map.addLayer(coreLayer);
    buildMarkers();
    buildLabels();
    buildMileage();
  }

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 139 页
  function buildMarkers(){
    var markers = PAYLOAD.markers || [];
    if (!markers.length){ markerLayer = null; return; }
    var src = new ol.source.Vector();
    for (var i=0;i<markers.length;i++){
      var m = markers[i];
      var f = new ol.Feature({
        geometry: new ol.geom.Point(ol.proj.fromLonLat([m.lng, m.lat])),
        props: m
      });
      var img = new ol.style.Icon({
        src: m.src, width: m.width||38, height: m.height||38,
        anchor:[0.5,0.5], anchorXUnits:'fraction', anchorYUnits:'fraction'
      });
      // 起终点加文字标签（起 / 终），小圆点太不起眼。
      // 注意：text 必须在 Style 构造时一起传，
      // 只给 image 的 Style 调 getText() 会返回 null —— 实测踩过，
      // 报错 "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'setText')"，
      // 整个 buildVector 中断，路线层根本没建起来。
      var tone = (m.kind === "start") ? "#2DD4A7" : "#FF8A5B";
      var st = new ol.style.Style({
        image: img,
        text: new ol.style.Text({
          text: m.label || "",
          font: "500 12px " + FONT,
          fill: new ol.style.Fill({color: "#0B1218"}),
          padding: [2, 3, 2, 3],
          backgroundFill: new ol.style.Fill({color: tone}),
          backgroundStroke: new ol.style.Stroke({color: tone, width: 1})
        })
      });
      f.setStyle(st);
      src.addFeature(f);
    }
    markerLayer = new ol.layer.Vector({source: src, declutter:true});
    map.addLayer(markerLayer);
  }

  // 道路名标注层：把路名打在路线上（高德那张图有这个，这里补上）。
  //
  // 按 tier 分成**三个独立图层**，各自 declutter —— OL 的 declutter 只在
  // 同一图层内避让，跨图层会互相挤掉：
  //   long   （≥120 km）→ 一直显示
  //   medium （≥8 km）  → zoom ≥ 8 才显示
  //   short  （≥18 km 门槛内更短的）→ zoom ≥ 10 才显示
  // 这样全国视图干净，放大后信息逐级浮现。
  function buildLabels(){
    var labels = PAYLOAD.roadNames || [];
    if (!labels.length){ labelLayer = null; return; }
    var tiers = {long: [], medium: [], short: []};

    for (var i=0;i<labels.length;i++){
      var t = labels[i].tier || "medium";
      if (!tiers[t]) tiers[t] = [];
      tiers[t].push(labels[i]);
    }
    var groups = [];
    for (var key in tiers){

      lyr.set('tier', key);
      map.addLayer(lyr);
      groups.push(lyr);

  }

  // 按当前缩放显示对应档位的路名标注
  function applyLabelTier(){
    if (!labelLayer) return;
    var z = map.getView().getZoom();
    var want = (z >= 10) ? "short" : (z >= 8 ? "medium" : "long");
    for (var i=0;i<labelLayer.length;i++){
      var lyr = labelLayer[i];
      var tier = lyr.get('tier');
      // long 档在所有缩放都显示；其余按门槛显示
      lyr.setVisible(tier === "long" ? true : tier === want || (want === "short" && tier === "medium"));
    }
  }

  // 里程刻度层：每 100 km 一个圆点 + 里程数字。
  // 这是"这趟路有多长、我在第几百公里"的直观读数。
  function buildMileage(){
    var miles = PAYLOAD.mileage || [];
    if (!miles.length){ mileLayer = null; return; }
    var src = new ol.source.Vector();
    for (var i=0;i<miles.length;i++){
      var m = miles[i];
      var f = new ol.Feature({
        geometry: new ol.geom.Point(ol.proj.fromLonLat([m.lng, m.lat])),
        props: m
      });
      var st = new ol.style.Style({
        image: new ol.style.Circle({
          radius: 4.5,
          fill: new ol.style.Fill({color: "#0B1218", opacity: 0.9}),
          stroke: new ol.style.Stroke({color: "#F5C86B", width: 2})
        }),
        text: new ol.style.Text({
          text: Math.round(m.km) + " km",
          font: "500 10px " + FONT,
          fill: new ol.style.Fill({color: "#F5C86B"}),
          stroke: new ol.style.Stroke({color: "rgba(4,9,14,.92)", width: 3}),
          offsetY: -12,
          overflow: true,
          padding: [1, 3, 1, 3],
          backgroundFill: new ol.style.Fill({color: "rgba(8,12,17,.78)"})
        })
      });
      f.setStyle(st);
      src.addFeature(f);
    }
    mileLayer = new ol.layer.Vector({source: src, declutter: true});
    map.addLayer(mileLayer);
  }

  function buildBase(){
    var layers = PAYLOAD.layers || [];
    baseOrder = []; baseLayers = {};
    for (var i=0;i<layers.length;i++){
      var L = layers[i];
      var src = null;
      try{
        if (L.kind === "wms"){
          src = new ol.source.TileWMS({
            url: L.url,
            params: {"LAYERS": L.layers, "FORMAT": L.format, "TRANSPARENT": L.transparent?"TRUE":"FALSE"},
            crossOrigin: "anonymous",
            serverType: (L.version === "1.1.1") ? ol.source.serverType.OGS : ol.source.serverType.WMS,
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 142 页
            transition: 120
          });
        } else if (L.kind === "wmts"){
          var res = (L.resolutions && L.resolutions.length) ? L.resolutions : null;
          src = new ol.source.WMTS({
            url: L.url,
            layer: L.layer, matrixSet: L.matrixSet, format: L.format,
            style: L.style, version: L.version,
            tileSize: L.tileSize, originX: L.originX, originY: L.originY,
            resolutions: res || undefined,
            requestEncoding: "REST"
          });
        } else {
          // XYZ：{1-4} 是 OL 支持的子域占位符写法。
          // 注意不能写 {s}——OL 的 XYZ 源不认，实测会全部落到同一个主机上拿空瓦片。
          src = new ol.source.XYZ({url: L.url, crossOrigin:"anonymous", attributions: L.attribution || undefined});
        }
      }catch(err){
        continue;
      }
      // className: 把底图瓦片层单独标记出来，
      // 暗色滤镜只作用于它，矢量层颜色保持原样。
      var tl = new ol.layer.Tile({source: src, opacity: L.opacity, visible: L.visible,
                                 className: "basemap-tile"});
      tl.set('baseName', L.name);
      tl.set('baseCrs', L.crs || 'wgs84');
      // 亮色栅格底图（高德/天地图）反相成暗色，融入 Carbon Tide 主题。
      // 暗色化的开关由底图本身决定，初始就要生效——不能只在用户手动切换时才触发。
      tl.set('needDark', (L.crs === 'gcj02' || /高德|tianditu|autonavi/i.test(L.url||'')) ? 1 : 0);
      baseLayers[L.name] = tl;
      baseOrder.push(tl);
    }
    // 底图必须在最下层：先全部加，再把矢量层置顶
    for (var j=0;j<baseOrder.length;j++){ map.addLayer(baseOrder[j]); }
    if (baseOrder.length){ baseKey = baseOrder[0].get('baseName'); }
    applyDark();
    renderSwitch();
  }

  // 用 zIndex 排图层顺序（OL 官方方式，不依赖已移除的 map.getLayers()）
  function applyZIndex(){
    var z = 0;
    for (var i=0;i<baseOrder.length;i++){ baseOrder[i].setZIndex(z++); }
    if (haloLayer) haloLayer.setZIndex(z++);
    if (coreLayer) coreLayer.setZIndex(z++);
    // 里程刻度优先于路名标注：数字读数比路名更重要，
    // 让它独占一层、独占 declutter，避免被路名挤掉（实测 700 km 后刻度消失）。
    if (mileLayer) mileLayer.setZIndex(z++);
    // labelLayer 现在是数组（按 tier 分层）
    if (labelLayer){
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 143 页
      if (labelLayer.length){
        for (var k=0;k<labelLayer.length;k++){ labelLayer[k].setZIndex(z++); }
      } else {
        labelLayer.setZIndex(z++);
      }
    }
    if (markerLayer) markerLayer.setZIndex(z++);
  }

  // 当前底图是否需要暗色化
  function currentNeedDark(){
    for (var i=0;i<baseOrder.length;i++){
      if (baseOrder[i].get('baseName')===baseKey) return !!baseOrder[i].get('needDark');
    }
    return false;
  }

  function applyDark(){
    $("map").classList.toggle("darkmode", currentNeedDark());
  }

  function renderSwitch(){
    var box = $("sw-items");
    if (!box){ return; }
    if (!baseOrder.length){
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="it" style="cursor:default;color:#8A97A6">无可用底图</div>';
      return;
    }
    var html = "";
    for (var i=0;i<baseOrder.length;i++){
      var L = baseOrder[i];
      var nm = L.get('baseName');
      var crs = L.get('baseCrs');
      html += '<div class="it" data-n="'+esc(nm)+'"><span class="dot"></span>'+esc(nm)
        +'<span class="crs">'+esc(crs)+'</span></div>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
    var items = box.querySelectorAll(".it");
    for (var j=0;j<items.length;j++){
      items[j].addEventListener("click", function(ev){
        var nm = ev.currentTarget.getAttribute("data-n");
        switchBase(nm);
      });
    }
    markActive();
  }

  function markActive(){
    var items = $("sw-items").querySelectorAll(".it");
    for (var j=0;j<items.length;j++){

      items[j].classList.toggle("on", items[j].getAttribute("data-n")===baseKey);
    }
  }

  function switchBase(name){
    for (var i=0;i<baseOrder.length;i++){
      baseOrder[i].setVisible(baseOrder[i].get('baseName')===name);
    }
    baseKey = name;
    markActive();
    hideFallback();
    applyDark();
  }

  function fitToRoute(){
    if (!coreLayer) return;
    var src = coreLayer.getSource();
    if (!src) return;
    var ext = src.getExtent();
    if (!ext || (isNaN(ext[0]) && isNaN(ext[1]))){ return; }
    try{
      map.getView().fit(ext, {padding:[70, 340, 60, 60], duration:0, maxZoom:11});
    }catch(e){}
  }

  function renderList(){

  function focusStep(i){
    var s = (PAYLOAD.steps||[])[i];
    if (!s) return;
    var items = $("lst").querySelectorAll(".it");
    for (var j=0;j<items.length;j++){ items[j].classList.toggle("on", j===i); }
    var f = layerById[s.id];
    if (!f) return;
    try{
      map.getView().animate({center: f.getGeometry().getCoordinates()[0], zoom: Math.max(map.getView().getZoom(), 9), duration: 420});
    }catch(e){}
  }

  function bindTooltip(){
    var tip = $("tip");
    map.on("pointermove", function(e){
      if (e.dragging){ tip.style.display="none"; return; }
      var hit = map.hasFeatureAtPixel(e.pixel, {layerFilter: function(l){return l===coreLayer;}});
      if (!hit || !hit.length){ tip.style.display="none"; return; }
      var p = hit[0].get("props") || {};
      var labels = PAYLOAD.labels || {}, colors = PAYLOAD.colors || {};
      var px = map.getPixelFromCoordinate(hit[0].getGeometry().getCoordinates()[0]);
      if (!px){ tip.style.display="none"; return; }
      var nm = p.road_name || "（无名路段）";
      if (p.road_ref){ nm = nm + " " + p.road_ref; }
      var html = '<div class="n">'+esc(nm)+'</div><div class="r">'
        + '等级 '+esc(labels[p.road_class]||p.road_class||"—")+' · proxy<br>'
        + '里程 '+fmt(p.distance_km,1)+' km · '+fmt(p.speed_kmh,0)+' km/h<br>'
        + '时长 '+fmt(p.duration_min,1)+' min · 碳排 '+fmt(p.carbon_kg,1)+' kg</div>';
      tip.innerHTML = html;
      tip.style.display = "block";
      tip.style.left = (px[0]+14)+"px";
      tip.style.top = (px[1]-12)+"px";
    });
    map.on("pointerleave", function(){ $("tip").style.display="none"; });
  }

  // 单步渲染的异常隔离。
  // 为什么需要：渲染链是 buildBase → buildVector → applyZIndex → renderLegend
  // → renderList → renderFoot 的串行调用，任何一步抛错都会让**后面全部不执行**。
  // 本项目实测踩过：map.getLayers()（OL v10 已移除）抛 TypeError，
  // 结果图例、逐 step 列表、页脚三块全空，页面看上去"地图能画，就是没内容"。
  // 现在每一步单独 try，失败只记日志，不影响其余部分。
  function safe(fn, name){
    try{ fn(); }
    catch(err){
      if (window.console && console.warn) console.warn("[olmapview] " + name + " 失败: " + (err && err.message ? err.message : err));
    }
  }

  function boot(){
    // ---- 第一段：完全不依赖 OpenLayers 的内容 ----
    // 逐 step 列表、图例、页脚、声明条都只用 PAYLOAD 里的数据，
    // **不需要 OL**。所以必须在 OL 加载检查之前渲染，
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 147 页
    // 否则 CDN 一挂（断网 / 抖动），整页就只剩一句"底图不可用"，
    // 连 65 条 step 明细都没了 —— 实测踩过：CDN 偶发失败时列表全空。
    safe(renderLegend, "renderLegend");
    safe(renderList, "renderList");
    safe(renderFoot, "renderFoot");
    var chip = $("crs-chip");
    if (chip){
      chip.textContent = "几何 " + (PAYLOAD.geomCrs||"wgs84").toUpperCase()
        + (PAYLOAD.geomCrs==="gcj02" ? "（已对齐底图）" : "");
    }

    if (typeof ol === "undefined"){
      showFallback("OpenLayers 未能从 CDN 加载（可能断网）。路线几何来自本地结果表，但底图与渲染依赖 JS 库。",
        "CDN: "+esc(FALLBACK.oljs));
      return;
    }
    try{
      // OL v10 的破坏性变更：ol.control.defaults 从"函数"变成了"对象"，
      // 默认控件集合挪到了 ol.control.defaults.defaults。
      // v7 及更早是 ol.control.defaults({...})，v10 起必须用下面这个写法。
      var defaultsFn = (ol.control.defaults && typeof ol.control.defaults === "function")
        ? ol.control.defaults
        : (ol.control.defaults && ol.control.defaults.defaults);
      map = new ol.Map({
        target: "map",
        layers: [],
        view: new ol.View({center: ol.proj.fromLonLat(PAYLOAD.center), zoom: PAYLOAD.zoom}),
        controls: defaultsFn({attribution:true, zoom:true, rotate:false})
      });
    }catch(err){
      showFallback("地图初始化失败："+esc(err.message||err), "");
      return;
    }
    safe(buildBase, "buildBase");
    safe(buildVector, "buildVector");
    // 层级最终校正：底图 zIndex 最低，halo 次之，路线与 marker 在最上。
    // 用 setZIndex 而不是 map.getLayers().setAt()——
    // OL v10 移除了 map.getLayers()，用它会直接抛 TypeError，
    // 把后续的图例/列表/页脚渲染全部打断（本项目实测踩过）。
    safe(applyZIndex, "applyZIndex");
    safe(fitToRoute, "fitToRoute");
    safe(bindTooltip, "bindTooltip");
    safe(applyLabelTier, "applyLabelTier");
    // 缩放变化时切换路名标注档位（全国视图只显示超长段，放大后逐级浮现）
    map.on("change:resolution", function(){ safe(applyLabelTier, "applyLabelTier"); });

    // 底图瓦片加载失败 → 明确提示（不留灰块）
    var t0 = Date.now();
    var probe = setTimeout(function(){
      if (!baseOrder.length){
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 148 页
        showFallback("未配置可用底图。路线矢量已渲染，但缺少底图参照。", FALLBACK.note||"");
      }
    }, 2500);
    map.on("rendercomplete", function(){
      clearTimeout(probe);
      if (baseOrder.length){
        var src = baseOrder[0].getSource();
        if (src){
          var failed = false;
          src.on("tileloaderror", function(){ failed = true; });
          setTimeout(function(){
            if (failed){
              showFallback("底图瓦片加载失败（token 无效或断网）。路线矢量仍可正常查看，"
                +"可在右上角切换其他底图。", FALLBACK.note||"");
            }
          }, 2600);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  if (document.readyState === "loading"){
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
  } else { boot(); }
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def build_doc(payload: Dict[str, Any], height: int = 760,
              title: str = "碳基线 · OSM 路线（OpenLayers）",
              fallback_note: str = "") -> str:
    """拼装自包含 HTML。payload 里只放展示数据，不放 key。"""
    safe_payload = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).replace("</", "<\\/")
    doc = (_TEMPLATE
           .replace("__TITLE__", html.escape(title))
           .replace("__OLJS__", OL_JS_URL)
           .replace("__OLCSS__", OL_CSS_URL)
           .replace("__HEIGHT__", str(int(height)))
           .replace("__PAYLOAD__", safe_payload)
           .replace("__FALLBACK__", json.dumps({
               "oljs": OL_JS_URL,
               "note": fallback_note,
           }, ensure_ascii=False)))
    return doc


def render(payload: Dict[str, Any], height: int = 760,
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 149 页
           title: str = "碳基线 · OSM 路线（OpenLayers）",
           fallback_note: str = "") -> None:
    """在 Streamlit 里渲染地图。"""
    doc = build_doc(payload, height=height, title=title, fallback_note=fallback_note)
    st.iframe(doc, height=int(height))

  function esc(s){

  }

  function clearOverlays(){

  function parseResult(result){
    var routes = (result.routes || []).map(function(rt){
      var path = [], last = null;
      (rt.steps || []).forEach(function(st){
        (st.path || []).forEach(function(p){
          var q = toLL(p);
          if(q && (!last || Math.abs(q[0]-last[0]) > 1e-9 || Math.abs(q[1]-last[1]) > 1e-9)){
            path.push(q); last = q;
          }
        });
      });
      var steps = (rt.steps || []).map(function(st){
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 161 页
        return {road: st.road || "", ins: st.instruction || "", km: (+st.distance || 0)/1000, min: (+st.time || 0)/60};
      });
      return {path:path, km:(+rt.distance||0)/1000, min:(+rt.time||0)/60, toll:+(rt.tolls || rt.toll || 0), steps:steps};
    }).filter(function(x){ return x.path.length > 1; });
    return {routes:routes, route:routes[0] || null};
  }

    $("#hudSub").textContent = "正在向高德请求 " + (r.on || "") + " → " + (r.dn || "") + " 的实时路线…";

    var done = false;
    var timer = setTimeout(function(){
      if(done) return;
      done = true;
      if(seq === state.seq) fallback(r, "高德路线请求超时");
    }, 14000);
    try{
      var driving = new AMap.Driving({policy: state.policy, hideMarkers: true, showTraffic: false});
      driving.search(r.o, r.d, function(status, result){
        if(done) return;
        done = true;
        clearTimeout(timer);
        if(seq !== state.seq) return;
        if(status !== "complete" || !result || !result.routes || !result.routes.length){
          fallback(r, "高德未返回可用路线");
          return;
        }
        var parsed = parseResult(result);
        if(!parsed.route){ fallback(r, "高德路线几何为空"); return; }
        state.cache[key] = parsed;
        applyLive(parsed, r, false);
      });
    }catch(err){
      clearTimeout(timer);
      fallback(r, "高德路线规划异常：" + (err && err.message ? err.message : err));
    }
  }

  function applyLive(parsed, r, cached){

  }

  function lineWeights(){
    var z = state.map ? state.map.getZoom() : 6;
    if(z >= 11) return {glow:8, main:3.6, core:1.1};
    if(z >= 9) return {glow:11, main:4.2, core:1.3};
    return {glow:16, main:5, core:1.6};

  function applyLineWeights(){
    if(!state.lines) return;
    var w = lineWeights();
    state.lines.glow.setOptions({strokeWeight:w.glow});
    state.lines.main.setOptions({strokeWeight:w.main});
    state.lines.core.setOptions({strokeWeight:w.core});
  }

  function drawRoute(r, route, keepCamera){
    var color = r.est ? "#F5C86B" : "#2DD4A7";
    var path = route.path;
    var w = lineWeights();
    var glow = new AMap.Polyline({path:path, strokeColor:color, strokeWeight:w.glow, strokeOpacity:.13,
      lineJoin:"round", lineCap:"round", zIndex:30, bubble:true});
    var main = new AMap.Polyline({path:path, strokeColor:color, strokeWeight:w.main, strokeOpacity:.94,
      lineJoin:"round", lineCap:"round", zIndex:40, bubble:true});
    var core = new AMap.Polyline({path:path, strokeColor:"#E9FFF8", strokeWeight:w.core, strokeOpacity:.7,
      lineJoin:"round", lineCap:"round", zIndex:41, bubble:true});
    state.lines = {glow:glow, main:main, core:core};
    addOverlay(glow); addOverlay(main); addOverlay(core);
    if(!keepCamera) fitView(path, 950);
    addArrows(path, color);
    // 生长动画：用重采样点做 700 个点的推进，最后回填全量几何。
    if(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches){
      return;
    }
    var sample = resample(path, 640);
    if(sample.length < 3) return;
    main.setPath(sample.slice(0,2)); core.setPath(sample.slice(0,2));
    glow.setOptions({strokeOpacity:0});
    var t0 = performance.now(), dur = 1050;
    function frame(t){
      var p = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / dur);
      var e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);
      var n = Math.max(2, Math.floor(e * sample.length));
      main.setPath(sample.slice(0, n));
      core.setPath(sample.slice(0, n));
      glow.setOptions({strokeOpacity: 0.14 * e});
      if(p < 1){ requestAnimationFrame(frame); }
      else { main.setPath(path); core.setPath(path); glow.setOptions({strokeOpacity:.14}); }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    main.on("click", function(){ showInfo(r, route); });
    main.on("mouseover", function(){ main.setOptions({strokeWeight:lineWeights().main + 2}); });
    main.on("mouseout", function(){ main.setOptions({strokeWeight:lineWeights().main}); });
  }

  function resample(path, n){
    if(path.length <= n) return path.slice();
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 164 页
    var step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(path.length / n));
    var out = [];
    for(var i=0;i<path.length;i+=step) out.push(path[i]);
    if(out[out.length-1] !== path[path.length-1]) out.push(path[path.length-1]);
    return out;
  }

  function addArrows(path, color){
    var total = path.length;
    if(total < 8) return;
    var count = Math.min(26, Math.max(6, Math.floor(state.totalKm / 55)));
    var step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(total / count));
    for(var i = step; i < total - 2; i += step){
      var a = path[i], b = path[Math.min(total-1, i+2)];
      var ang = bearing(a,b);
      var m = new AMap.Marker({
        position: a, anchor:"center", zIndex:45, bubble:true,
        content: "<div class='dir' style='transform:rotate(" + ang + "deg)'>▲</div>"
      });
      addOverlay(m);
    }
  }

  function bearing(a,b){
    var dx = b[0]-a[0], dy = b[1]-a[1];
    return Math.atan2(dx, dy) * 180 / Math.PI;
  }

  function addEndpoints(r, color){

  function fitView(path, duration){
    if(!state.map || !path || path.length < 2) return;
    var minLng=180,maxLng=-180,minLat=90,maxLat=-90;
    path.forEach(function(p){
      if(p[0]<minLng) minLng=p[0]; if(p[0]>maxLng) maxLng=p[0];
      if(p[1]<minLat) minLat=p[1]; if(p[1]>maxLat) maxLat=p[1];
    });
    var mode = "none";
    try{
      // setFitView 对手上的 overlay 数组最稳；immediately=false 让高德自己做缓动。
      state.map.setFitView(state.overlays, false, [90,90,90,90], 16);
      mode = "fit";
    }catch(e){
      try{
        var bounds = new AMap.Bounds([minLng,minLat],[maxLng,maxLat]);
        state.map.setBounds(bounds, true, [90,90,90,90]);
        mode = "bounds";
      }catch(e2){
        window.__ROUTE_LAB__.fitError = String((e2 && e2.message) || e2 || e);
      }
    }
    setTimeout(function(){
      if(!state.map) return;
      try{
        var c = state.map.getCenter();
        window.__ROUTE_LAB__.debug = {zoom: state.map.getZoom(), lng: c.getLng(), lat: c.getLat(),
          fit: mode, bounds: [minLng,minLat,maxLng,maxLat], overlays: state.overlays.length};
      }catch(_){}
    }, Math.max(200, (duration || 0) + 200));
  }

  function buildCum(){
    if(!state.path || state.path.length < 2){ state.cum=null; state.totalKm=0; return; }
    var cum = [0], total = 0;
    for(var i=1;i<state.path.length;i++){
      total += haversine(state.path[i-1], state.path[i]);
      cum.push(total);
    }
    state.cum = cum; state.totalKm = total;
  }
  function haversine(a,b){
    var R = 6371.0088;
    var p1 = a[1]*Math.PI/180, p2 = b[1]*Math.PI/180;
    var dp = (b[1]-a[1])*Math.PI/180, dl = (b[0]-a[0])*Math.PI/180;
    var x = Math.sin(dp/2)*Math.sin(dp/2) + Math.cos(p1)*Math.cos(p2)*Math.sin(dl/2)*Math.sin(dl/2);
    return 2*R*Math.asin(Math.sqrt(Math.min(1,x)));
  }
  function pointAt(km){
    if(!state.cum || !state.path) return null;
    var lo=0, hi=state.cum.length-1;
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 166 页
    while(lo<hi){ var mid=(lo+hi)>>1; if(state.cum[mid] < km) lo=mid+1; else hi=mid; }
    if(lo===0) return state.path[0];
    var a=state.path[lo-1], b=state.path[lo];
    var seg=state.cum[lo]-state.cum[lo-1] || 1;
    var t=(km-state.cum[lo-1])/seg;
    return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t, a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t];
  }
  function angleAt(km){
    if(!state.cum || !state.path) return 0;
    var lo=0, hi=state.cum.length-1;
    while(lo<hi){ var mid=(lo+hi)>>1; if(state.cum[mid] < km) lo=mid+1; else hi=mid; }
    var a=state.path[Math.max(0,lo-1)], b=state.path[Math.min(state.path.length-1,lo+1)];
    return bearing(a,b);
  }

def build_doc(payload: Dict[str, Any], key: str, jscode: str = "",
              mode: str = "lab", height: int = 820,
              title: str = "路线实验室 · 高德实时路线") -> str:
    """拼装自包含 HTML。payload 里只放展示数据，不放 key。"""
    safe_payload = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).replace("</", "<\\/")
    doc = (TEMPLATE
           .replace("__TITLE__", str(title))
           .replace("__KEY__", str(key))
           .replace("__JSCODE__", str(jscode or ""))
           .replace("__HEIGHT__", str(int(height)))
           .replace("__MODE__", str(mode))
           .replace("__PAYLOAD__", safe_payload))
    return doc

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""路线视觉增强层：把"一条线"变成"一张能读懂的图"。

为什么单独拆一个模块
--------------------
本项目实测过：Tab② 最初只有一条 3px 的线悬在纯黑底上，
和高德那侧（底图 + 路况 + 标注 + 起终点 + 动效）一比，**判若两图**。
但根因不在"渲染代码写得差"，而在**缺了视觉层次本身**。

高德那张地图好看的四个要素，逐条对应到这里：

=================== ====================== ======================
高德有的            本模块提供              实现方式
=================== ====================== ======================
底图                （由底图层负责）          basemap_align
道路有粗细层级      按 step 里程加权线宽      :func:`line_width`
路线有光晕/描边      三层描边（halo+core）    :func:`feature_style`
有起终点标记        圆形 marker + 标签       :func:`marker_style`
=================== ====================== ======================

关键设计
--------
1. **halo 层**：深色描边打底，浅色主线压在上面。
   这是高德/Google 路线渲染的通用做法——没有它，亮色线在亮色底图上会"糊"掉。
2. **里程加权线宽**：高速段本来就长，线宽再按里程给一点权重，
   视觉上"粗的段 = 高速占比高"，与右侧等级结构表互相印证。
3. **marker 用 SVG data URI**：不依赖任何外部图片、不触发 CDN 请求，
   断网也能画出来。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence

from src.routing.coord import haversine_km

#: 道路等级 → 基础线宽（px）
BASE_WIDTHS: Dict[str, float] = {
    "motorway": 5.2,
    "trunk": 4.0,
    "secondary": 3.0,
    "urban": 2.4,
    "local": 1.8,
    "ramp": 2.0,
    "unknown": 1.8,
}

#: 里程权重上限（px）：最长的 step 最多比基准宽这么多
MILEAGE_WEIGHT_MAX = 2.4

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 172 页
#: 里程刻度间隔（km）——每 100 km 在路线上打一个刻度
MILEAGE_TICK_INTERVAL_KM = 100.0

#: 道路名标注的最小路段里程（km）——短于此的不标，避免密集重叠
LABEL_MIN_KM = 18.0

#: 道路名标注分级：
#:   zoom < 8   → 只显示「超长段」（≥ 120 km），全国尺度上一眼能看清
#:   8 ≤ zoom  → 显示全部合格段（≥ LABEL_MIN_KM）
#:   zoom ≥ 10  → 再放宽到中等段（≥ 8 km）
#:
#: 为什么分级：全国视图里 15 个标签会挤成一团糊掉；
#: 而放大后路段变短，再多标也放得下。分级 = 一次取舍换来两个尺度都可读。
LABEL_LONG_KM = 120.0
LABEL_MEDIUM_KM = 8.0


def line_width(road_class: str, distance_km: float,
               zoom: Optional[float] = None) -> float:
    """按道路等级 + 里程算线宽。

    :param road_class: 道路等级（motorway/trunk/...）
    :param distance_km: 该 step 的里程
    :param zoom: 当前缩放级别；给定时按 log 缩放（放大时线宽略增，视觉更稳）
    """
    base = BASE_WIDTHS.get(road_class, 1.8)
    # 里程权重：10 km 以内不加权，100 km 封顶加满
    weight = min(max(distance_km, 0.0) / 100.0, 1.0) * MILEAGE_WEIGHT_MAX
    width = base + weight
    if zoom:
        # zoom 5→1.0，zoom 12→1.6
        scale = 1.0 + max(0.0, min(zoom - 5.0, 7.0)) / 7.0 * 0.6
        width *= scale
    return round(width, 2)


def feature_style(road_class: str, distance_km: float,
                  color: str, zoom: Optional[float] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """生成一个 step 的三层描边样式描述。

    返回结构（前端消费）::

        {"width": 主线宽, "halo": halo 宽, "color": 主线色,
         "haloColor": halo 色, "opacity": 主线不透明度}

    halo 比主线宽 2.5 px，颜色是主线的深色版——在亮色底图上也能压住。
    注意 halo **不能太宽**：底图反相成暗色后，宽 halo 会盖住主线，
    整条路线看起来发白（实测踩过：halo 宽 +4px 时高速段变成白带）。
    """
    w = line_width(road_class, distance_km, zoom)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 173 页
    return {
        "width": w,
        # halo 只比主线宽 1.5px：底图反相成暗色后，halo 与主线颜色叠加会让
        # 高速段发白（实测 halo 宽 4px 时整条高速呈白带，读不出等级色）。
        # 1.5px 足够压住底图，又不会淹没主线颜色。
        "halo": round(w + 1.5, 2),
        "color": color,
        "haloColor": "rgba(4,9,14,0.55)",
        "opacity": 1.0,
    }


#: 起终点 marker 的 SVG（data URI，内联，不发任何网络请求）
def _marker_svg(fill: str, ring: str) -> str:
    svg = (
        '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="38" height="38">'
        '<circle cx="19" cy="19" r="17" fill="{fill}" fill-opacity="0.20"/>'
        '<circle cx="19" cy="19" r="10" fill="{fill}" stroke="{ring}" stroke-width="2.5"/>'
        '</svg>'
    ).format(fill=fill, ring=ring)
    return "data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8," + (
        svg.replace("#", "%23").replace('"', "'").replace(" ", "%20")
    )


START_MARKER = _marker_svg("#2DD4A7", "#04121A")
END_MARKER = _marker_svg("#FF8A5B", "#2A0F06")


def marker_style(kind: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """起终点 marker 样式描述。"""
    return {
        "src": START_MARKER if kind == "start" else END_MARKER,
        "width": 38,
        "height": 38,
    }


# ================================================================ 里程刻度

def _cumulative_km(coords: Sequence[Sequence[float]]) -> List[float]:
    """几何点的累计里程（km），长度与 coords 相同，首项为 0。"""
    out: List[float] = [0.0]
    for i in range(1, len(coords)):
        out.append(out[-1] + haversine_km(
            coords[i - 1][0], coords[i - 1][1], coords[i][0], coords[i][1]))
    return out


def _point_at_offset(coords: Sequence[Sequence[float]],
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 174 页
                     cum: Sequence[float],
                     offset_km: float) -> Optional[Sequence[float]]:
    """在几何上找"距起点 offset_km 处"的点（线性插值）。

    找不到（超出几何长度）时返回 None。
    """
    if not coords or len(coords) < 2:
        return None
    total = cum[-1]
    if total <= 0 or offset_km < 0 or offset_km > total:
        return None
    # 二分找区间
    lo, hi = 0, len(cum) - 1
    while hi - lo > 1:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if cum[mid] <= offset_km:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    span = cum[hi] - cum[lo]
    if span <= 0:
        return coords[lo]
    ratio = (offset_km - cum[lo]) / span
    return [
        coords[lo][0] + (coords[hi][0] - coords[lo][0]) * ratio,
        coords[lo][1] + (coords[hi][1] - coords[lo][1]) * ratio,
    ]


def mileage_ticks(segments: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                  interval_km: float = MILEAGE_TICK_INTERVAL_KM) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """沿整条路线打里程刻度。

    :param segments: 有序的路段列表，每项含 ``geometry``（坐标数组）、
        ``distance_km``、``road_class``、``step_index``
    :param interval_km: 刻度间隔（默认 100 km）

    返回每项::

        {"km": 累计里程, "lng": ..., "lat": ..., "road_class": ...}

    **口径纪律**：刻度读数必须与右侧逐 step 列表**用同一套里程**
    （OSRM 的 ``distance_km``），否则会出现"刻度说 500 km、列表加起来对不上"。
    所以累计基准用 parquet 的 ``distance_km``，
    几何长度只用来**在段内插值定位**，不用来累加。

    为什么在**服务端**算：里程是"业务口径"的一部分，前端只负责画，不能自己数。
    """
    ticks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    if not segments:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 175 页
        return ticks

    # 1) 整理有效段。
    #
    # **两套里程，各司其职**（这是本函数最容易搞错的地方）：
    #
    #   · ``km_len``（parquet 的 distance_km）→ 决定**刻度读数**落在哪一段，
    #     保证 "500 km" 这个数字和右侧逐 step 列表口径一致；
    #   · ``cum[-1]``（几何折线长度）→ 决定**点在段内的位置**，
    #     保证点一定落在折线上，不会飘到线外。
    #
    # 真实数据里两者只差 0.27%（几何是折线近似，parquet 是 OSRM 精确里程）；
    # 但如果混用，段级差距大时点会飘到线外（实测可偏 11 km）。
    valid: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    total_km = 0.0
    for seg in segments:
        coords = seg.get("geometry") or []
        if len(coords) < 2:
            continue
        cum = _cumulative_km(coords)
        valid.append({
            "coords": coords,
            "cum": cum,
            "km_start": total_km,
            "km_len": float(seg.get("distance_km") or 0.0),
            "geom_len": cum[-1],
        })
        total_km += float(seg.get("distance_km") or 0.0)

    if total_km <= 0 or not valid:
        return ticks

    # 2) 按间隔取刻度点
    step_km = interval_km if interval_km > 0 else MILEAGE_TICK_INTERVAL_KM
    n = int(total_km // step_km)
    for k in range(1, n + 1):
        target = k * step_km
        # 用 parquet 里程定位段落
        seg = None
        for v in valid:
            if target <= v["km_start"] + v["km_len"] + 1e-9:
                seg = v
                break
        if seg is None:
            continue
        # 段内比例（按 parquet 里程算）→ 几何上的偏移（按几何长度换算）
        offset_in_geom = 0.0
        if seg["km_len"] > 0 and seg["geom_len"] > 0:
            ratio = (target - seg["km_start"]) / seg["km_len"]
            ratio = max(0.0, min(1.0, ratio))
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 176 页
            offset_in_geom = ratio * seg["geom_len"]
        pt = _point_at_offset(seg["coords"], seg["cum"], offset_in_geom)
        if pt is None:
            continue
        ticks.append({
            "km": round(target, 1),
            "lng": round(pt[0], 6),
            "lat": round(pt[1], 6),
        })
    return ticks


def road_labels(segments: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                min_km: float = LABEL_MIN_KM,
                max_labels: int = 40) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """挑出值得标注道路名的路段，并给出标注锚点。

    挑选规则（避免标签糊成一片）：

    * 里程 ≥ ``min_km``（太短的路段标了也看不清）；
    * 有路名（无名路段不标）；
    * 按里程从大到小取前 ``max_labels`` 个 —— 长路段才配拥有标签。

    锚点取路段的**中点**，而不是首点：首点往往紧邻上一段的末端，容易重叠。

    每项带一个 ``tier`` 字段（``long`` / ``medium`` / ``short``），
    前端按缩放级别决定显示哪一档 —— 见 :data:`LABEL_LONG_KM` 的说明。
    """
    cands: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for seg in segments:
        name = str(seg.get("road_name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        dist = float(seg.get("distance_km") or 0.0)
        if dist < min_km:
            continue
        coords = seg.get("geometry") or []
        if len(coords) < 2:
            continue
        cum = _cumulative_km(coords)
        mid = _point_at_offset(coords, cum, cum[-1] / 2.0)
        if mid is None:
            continue
        if dist >= LABEL_LONG_KM:
            tier = "long"
        elif dist >= LABEL_MEDIUM_KM:
            tier = "medium"
        else:
            tier = "short"
        cands.append({
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 177 页
            "step_index": int(seg.get("step_index", 0)),
            "text": name,
            "road_ref": str(seg.get("road_ref") or "").strip(),
            "road_class": str(seg.get("road_class") or "unknown"),
            "distance_km": round(dist, 1),
            "tier": tier,
            "lng": round(mid[0], 6),
            "lat": round(mid[1], 6),
        })

    cands.sort(key=lambda x: -x["distance_km"])
    return cands[:max_labels]

      if (observer) { observer.disconnect(); observer = null; }
      if (timer) { p.clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; }
      if (maxTimer) { p.clearTimeout(maxTimer); maxTimer = null; }

    }

    function normHref(h) {
      var s = (h || '').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
      try { s = decodeURIComponent(s); } catch(e) {}
      if (s.indexOf(p.location.origin) === 0) s = s.slice(p.location.origin.length);
      while (s.charAt(0) === '/') s = s.slice(1);
      return s;
    }

    function pageLinkFor(href) {
      var target = normHref(href);
      if (!target) return null;
      var links = doc.querySelectorAll('a[data-testid="stPageLink-NavLink"]');
      for (var i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
        if (links[i].getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true') continue;
        if (normHref(links[i].getAttribute('href')) === target) return links[i];
      }
      return null;

        if (timer) p.clearTimeout(timer);
        timer = p.setTimeout(hideVeil, 80);

      if (a.getAttribute('data-testid') !== 'stPageLink-NavLink') {

        if (bridge) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          p.__cmBridging = true;
          bridge.click();
          p.__cmBridging = false;
