def build_kpi(seg: pd.DataFrame, wide: pd.DataFrame, schemes: pd.DataFrame,
              band: pd.DataFrame, quality: Dict[str, Any], source: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    total_kg = float(pd.to_numeric(seg["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
    trip_view = seg.drop_duplicates("trip_id") if "trip_id" in seg.columns else seg
    km_col = "trip_km" if "trip_km" in trip_view.columns else "dist_km"
    total_km = float(pd.to_numeric(trip_view[km_col], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
    speed = pd.to_numeric(trip_view["speed_kmh"], errors="coerce") if "speed_kmh" in trip_view.columns else pd.Series(dtype=float)
    valid = speed.notna() & (speed > 0)
    clean = valid & speed.between(1, 100)
    duration = pd.to_numeric(trip_view["duration_h"], errors="coerce") if "duration_h" in trip_view.columns else pd.Series(dtype=float)
    valid_dur = valid & duration.gt(0)
    clean_dur = clean & duration.gt(0)
    avg_speed = (float(pd.to_numeric(trip_view.loc[valid_dur, km_col], errors="coerce").sum()) / float(duration[valid_dur].sum())) if valid_dur.any() and duration[valid_dur].sum() > 0 else None
    clean_km = float(pd.to_numeric(trip_view.loc[clean_dur, km_col], errors="coerce").sum())
    avg_speed_clean = (clean_km / float(duration[clean_dur].sum())) if clean_dur.any() and duration[clean_dur].sum() > 0 else None
    source_speed = pd.to_numeric(seg["speed_kmh"], errors="coerce") if "speed_kmh" in seg.columns else pd.Series(dtype=float)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 260 页
    source_valid = source_speed.notna() & source_speed.gt(0)
    source_avg_speed = float(source_speed[source_valid].mean()) if source_valid.any() else None
    vclass = pd.to_numeric(seg["vclass"], errors="coerce") if "vclass" in seg.columns else pd.Series(dtype=float)
    heavy = float(pd.to_numeric(seg.loc[vclass == 3, "kg_co2"], errors="coerce").sum()) if len(vclass) else 0.0
    # 第 2 页“超长途（>500 km）碳占比”：原页面公式，D4 迁入 kpi（口径只有这一份）
    longhaul = 0.0
    if "dist_km" in seg.columns:
        dist = pd.to_numeric(seg["dist_km"], errors="coerce")
        longhaul = float(pd.to_numeric(seg.loc[dist > 500, "kg_co2"], errors="coerce").sum())
    band_ratio = _num(band["ratio"].median()) if not band.empty and "ratio" in band.columns else None
    band_kg = float(pd.to_numeric(band["kg_t"], errors="coerce").sum()) * 1000.0 if not band.empty else 0.0
    main = schemes[schemes["is_main"]] if schemes is not None and not schemes.empty else pd.DataFrame()

    return {
        "source": source,
        "generated_at": _now(),
        "total_kg": total_kg,
        "e0_t": total_kg / 1000.0,
        "total_km": total_km,
        "segments": int(len(seg)),
        "physical_trips": int(len(trip_view)),
        "rows_wide": int(len(wide)),
        "valid_speed_rows": int(valid.sum()),
        "avg_speed": avg_speed,
        "avg_speed_clean": avg_speed_clean,
        "source_avg_speed": source_avg_speed,
        "heavy_share": (heavy / total_kg) if total_kg > 0 else None,
        "longhaul_share": (longhaul / total_kg) if total_kg > 0 else None,
        # 第 5 页“年度化排放”展示值：原页面 E₀ × 年化系数，D4 迁入 kpi
        "annual_emission_t": (total_kg / 1000.0 * evaluator.ANNUALIZATION_FACTOR),
        "annualization_factor": float(evaluator.ANNUALIZATION_FACTOR),
        "dup_rows": int(quality.get("dup_rows", 0) or 0),
        "anomaly_rows": int(quality.get("anomaly_rows", 0) or 0),
        "basis_rows": int(quality.get("basis_rows", 0) or 0),
        "estimated_rows": int(quality.get("estimated_rows", 0) or 0),
        "dist_filled": int(quality.get("fill_dist", 0) or 0),
        "vehicle_filled": int(quality.get("fill_vehicle", 0) or 0),
        "invoice_missing": int(quality.get("fill_invoice", 0) or 0),
        "od_ratio_median": band_ratio,
        "od_pairs": int(len(band)),
        "od_coverage_pct": (band_kg / total_kg * 100.0) if total_kg > 0 else None,
        "main_reduction_pct": _num(main.iloc[0]["reduction_pct"]) if not main.empty else None,
        # E4 起：主方案可能是逐车组参数化（real）或历史标定（demo）；
        # 闸门 note 与披露 limitations 按此切换措辞，不再谎称"全部已核验"。
        "main_calculation_mode": (str(main.iloc[0]["calculation_mode"])
                                  if not main.empty and "calculation_mode" in main.columns else None),
        "main_decision_eligible": (bool(main.iloc[0]["decision_eligible"])
                                   if not main.empty and "decision_eligible" in main.columns else None),
        "factor_version": optimizer.FACTOR_VERSION,
    }
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 261 页


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G3.5 · 车队真实统计（第 7/8 页计算器的默认值来源，docs/20 §11）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def build_fleet_stats(seg: pd.DataFrame, source: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """聚合段表得到车队真实统计，供情景演算页当默认值。

    纪律：
    - 只聚合已去重、已对拍的段表，不引入任何新口径；
    - 运单是**抽样**数据（不是全年全车队），因此**不推导**"年均里程/车"这类
      会造假精度的指标——该默认值在 config/calc_factors.json 标注为假设值；
    - 本函数失败不得阻断主流程：调用方包 try，失败时写最小统计。
    """
    trip_view = seg.drop_duplicates("trip_id") if "trip_id" in seg.columns else seg
    km_col = "trip_km" if "trip_km" in trip_view.columns else "dist_km"
    total_km = float(pd.to_numeric(trip_view[km_col], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
    total_kg = float(pd.to_numeric(seg["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
    diesel_ttw = scenarios.load_factors()["co2_factors"]["diesel_ttw_kg_per_l"]["value"]
    vclass = pd.to_numeric(seg["vclass"], errors="coerce") if "vclass" in seg.columns else pd.Series(dtype=float)
    names = {1: "轻型", 2: "中型", 3: "重型"}
    by_vclass: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for vc in (1, 2, 3):
        sub = seg[vclass == vc] if len(vclass) else pd.DataFrame()
        if sub.empty:
            continue
        km = float(pd.to_numeric(sub["dist_km"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
        kg = float(pd.to_numeric(sub["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
        load = pd.to_numeric(sub["load_t"], errors="coerce") if "load_t" in sub.columns else pd.Series(dtype=float)
        by_vclass[str(vc)] = {
            "name": names[vc],
            "segments": int(len(sub)),
            "km": round(km, 1),
            "km_share_pct": round(100.0 * km / total_km, 1) if total_km > 0 else None,
            "implied_l_per_100km": round(kg / diesel_ttw / km * 100.0, 1) if km > 0 and diesel_ttw > 0 else None,
            "avg_load_t": round(float(load.mean()), 2) if load.notna().any() else None,
        }
    load_tkm = 0.0
    if "load_t" in seg.columns:
        load_tkm = float((pd.to_numeric(seg["dist_km"], errors="coerce").fillna(0)
                          * pd.to_numeric(seg["load_t"], errors="coerce").fillna(0)).sum())
    months = []
    if "month" in seg.columns:
        months = sorted(int(m) for m in pd.to_numeric(seg["month"], errors="coerce").dropna().unique())
    return {
        "source": source,
        "generated_at": _now(),
        "segments": int(len(seg)),
        "total_km": round(total_km, 1),
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 262 页
        "e0_kg": round(total_kg, 1),
        "plates": int(seg["plate"].nunique()) if "plate" in seg.columns else None,
        "months": months,
        "by_vclass": by_vclass,
        "implied_g_per_tkm": round(total_kg / load_tkm * 1000.0, 1) if load_tkm > 0 else None,
        "total_load_tkm": round(load_tkm, 1),
        "diesel_ttw_kg_per_l": diesel_ttw,
        "note": "运单为抽样数据：不推导年均里程/车；该默认值见 config/calc_factors.json（假设值）",
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G4.5 · E₀ 构成下钻（第 5 页“E₀ 由哪些车、哪几个月堆出来”，D4 迁入）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _subset_key(subset: List[int], universe: List[int]) -> str:
    """组合键：全集记 all，否则按升序逗号连接（页面按同一规则查表）。"""
    return "all" if list(subset) == list(universe) else ",".join(str(int(x)) for x in subset)


def build_composition(seg: pd.DataFrame, max_combos: int = 800) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """E₀ 构成预计算：按“月份子集 × 车型大类子集”把构成逐组算好。

    D4：原第 5 页在页面上做 groupby sum / 占比 / idxmax，全部迁到这里——
    页面只按组合键查表显示，不做任何聚合与比率。
    组合空间 = (2^月份数 − 1) × (2^车型数 − 1)；真实与演示数据都是 4 个月份 → 105 组。
    月份过多时（>6）退化为“单月 + 全月”，避免组合爆炸。
    """
    empty: Dict[str, Any] = {"generated_at": _now(), "e0_t": 0.0, "months": [],
                             "vclasses": [], "month_rows": {}, "combos": {}}
    if seg is None or seg.empty or "kg_co2" not in seg.columns:
        return empty

    frame = seg.copy()
    frame["_kg"] = pd.to_numeric(frame["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
    total_kg = float(frame["_kg"].sum()) or 1.0
    months = (sorted(int(m) for m in pd.to_numeric(frame["month"], errors="coerce").dropna().unique())
              if "month" in frame.columns else [])
    vclasses = (sorted(int(v) for v in pd.to_numeric(frame["vclass"], errors="coerce").dropna().unique())
                if "vclass" in frame.columns else [])
    if not months or not vclasses:
        return {**empty, "months": months, "vclasses": vclasses}

    if len(months) <= 6:
        month_subsets = [list(c) for r in range(1, len(months) + 1)
                         for c in itertools.combinations(months, r)]
    else:  # 组合爆炸保护：只保留单月与全月
        month_subsets = [[m] for m in months] + [months]
    vclass_subsets = [list(c) for r in range(1, len(vclasses) + 1)
                      for c in itertools.combinations(vclasses, r)]
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 263 页
    if len(month_subsets) * len(vclass_subsets) > max_combos:
        month_subsets = [[m] for m in months] + [months]

    m_num = pd.to_numeric(frame["month"], errors="coerce") if "month" in frame.columns else None
    v_num = pd.to_numeric(frame["vclass"], errors="coerce") if "vclass" in frame.columns else None
    e_num = (pd.to_numeric(frame["flag_estimated"], errors="coerce").fillna(0)
             if "flag_estimated" in frame.columns else pd.Series(0, index=frame.index))

    combos: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for ms in month_subsets:
        mmask = m_num.isin(ms) if m_num is not None else pd.Series(True, index=frame.index)
        for vs in vclass_subsets:
            vmask = v_num.isin(vs) if v_num is not None else pd.Series(True, index=frame.index)
            sub = frame[mmask & vmask]
            if sub.empty:
                continue
            sub_kg = float(sub["_kg"].sum())
            share = (lambda v: round(100.0 * float(v) / sub_kg, 3)) if sub_kg > 0 else (lambda v: None)
            by_month: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            peak_month = None
            if m_num is not None:
                g = sub.assign(_m=pd.to_numeric(sub["month"], errors="coerce")).groupby("_m")["_kg"].sum().sort_index()
                by_month = [{"month": int(m), "kg": round(float(v), 2), "share_pct": share(v)}
                            for m, v in g.items()]
                peak_month = int(g.idxmax()) if len(g) else None
            top_vehicles: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            if "vehicle_type" in sub.columns:
                tv = (sub.assign(_vt=sub["vehicle_type"].astype(str))
                      .groupby("_vt").agg(kg=("_kg", "sum"), n=("_kg", "size"))
                      .sort_values(["kg", "_vt"], ascending=[False, True]).head(10))
                top_vehicles = [{"vehicle_type": str(k), "kg": round(float(r["kg"]), 2),
                                 "share_pct": share(r["kg"]), "n": int(r["n"])}
                                for k, r in tv.iterrows()]
            combos[_subset_key(ms, months) + "|" + _subset_key(vs, vclasses)] = {
                "months": [int(m) for m in ms],
                "vclasses": [int(v) for v in vs],
                "segments": int(len(sub)),
                "kg": round(sub_kg, 2),
                "share_pct": share(sub_kg),
                "est_rows": int(pd.to_numeric(sub["flag_estimated"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
                if "flag_estimated" in sub.columns else 0,
                "by_month": by_month,
                "peak_month": peak_month,
                "top_vehicles": top_vehicles,
            }

    month_rows = ({str(int(m)): int(n) for m, n in m_num.value_counts().items()}
                  if m_num is not None else {})
    return {
        "generated_at": _now(),
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 264 页
        "e0_t": round(total_kg / 1000.0, 3),
        "months": months,
        "vclasses": vclasses,
        "month_rows": month_rows,
        "combos": combos,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G2 · 闸门
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _target_items(kpi: Dict[str, Any], schemes: pd.DataFrame, mode: str,
                  strict: bool) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    factor = optimizer.self_test()
    actual: Dict[str, Optional[float]] = {
        "segments": _num(kpi.get("segments")),
        "physical_trips": _num(kpi.get("physical_trips")),
        "total_km": _num(kpi.get("total_km")),
        "valid_speed_rows": _num(kpi.get("valid_speed_rows")),
        "avg_speed": _num(kpi.get("avg_speed")),
        "source_avg_speed": _num(kpi.get("source_avg_speed")),
        "e0_kg": (_num(kpi.get("total_kg")) or 0.0),
        "er30_vsp2": factor["er30_vsp2"],
        "vsp3_dev_pct": factor["vsp3_dev_pct"],
        "main_reduction_pct": _num(kpi.get("main_reduction_pct")),
    }

    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for spec in TARGETS:
        got = actual.get(spec["id"])
        expected = float(spec["expected"])
        tol = float(spec["tol"])
        skip = bool(spec["data_dependent"]) and mode == "demo" and not strict
        if skip:
            status = "SKIP"
            note = "演示数据不参与对拍；真数据落盘后自动启用"
        elif got is None:
            status = "FAIL"
            note = "结果缺失"
        else:
            status = "PASS" if abs(float(got) - expected) <= tol else "FAIL"
            note = "" if status == "PASS" else "超出容差 ±%s" % tol
            if status == "FAIL":
                diff = float(got) - expected
                if spec["id"] == "avg_speed":
                    note = ("差 %+.3f km/h；数据版本/去重口径差异，待确认" % diff)
                elif spec["id"] == "e0_kg":
                    note = ("差 %+.0f kg（%+.3f%%）；法定物理主账，关联毛值另见 vsp_audit.json" % (diff, diff / expected * 100.0))
        if spec["id"] == "main_reduction_pct":
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 265 页
            # D6 自洽：real 模式下"目标值 83.25"来自物理主账，实测值"来自段表
            # ×车组参数的逐车组复算——两套来源互证，改了任一侧闸门都会红。
            if str(kpi.get("main_calculation_mode") or "") == "parameterized":
                note = ("主方案逐车组参数化复算（底稿表 14/18/19 参数 × 物理段表）；"
                        "闸门校验其与物理主账口径一致（目标/实测不同源，互不循环）")
            else:
                note = "方案表为静态标定（答辩稿表 19–21），闸门只校验它没被改动"
            if skip:
                note += "；演示数据不参与对拍"
        elif spec["id"] == "avg_speed" and status == "PASS":
            note = "物理主账：总里程 ÷ 总时长，%.2f km/h" % float(got)
        elif spec["id"] == "source_avg_speed" and status == "PASS":
            note = "关联口径源均速：官方 49.57 / 重算 49.51，差 -0.057（±0.1 内）"
        elif not spec["data_dependent"]:
            note = "因子表自测，与数据无关"
        items.append({
            "id": spec["id"], "label": spec["label"], "stage": spec["stage"],
            "expected": expected, "actual": got, "tol": tol, "unit": spec["unit"],
            "status": status, "note": note,
        })
    return items


def _structural_items(wide: pd.DataFrame, seg: pd.DataFrame, schemes: pd.DataFrame,
                      hotspots: pd.DataFrame, band: pd.DataFrame, ledger: pd.DataFrame,
                      disclosure: Dict[str, Any], kpi: Dict[str, Any],
                      quality: Dict[str, Any], source: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    def check(cid: str, label: str, fn: Callable[[], Tuple[bool, str]]) -> None:
        try:
            ok, detail = fn()
            status = "PASS" if ok else "FAIL"
        except Exception as exc:
            status, detail = "FAIL", "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc)
        items.append({"id": cid, "label": label, "status": status, "detail": detail})

    def need_cols(df: pd.DataFrame, cols: List[str]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        miss = [c for c in cols if c not in df.columns]
        return (not miss), ("缺列：" + "、".join(miss) if miss else "契约齐全")

    check("wide_rows", "宽表非空", lambda: (len(wide) > 0, "宽表 %d 行" % len(wide)))
    check("wide_cols", "宽表字段契约", lambda: need_cols(wide, WIDE_REQUIRED))
    check("seg_rows", "段表非空", lambda: (len(seg) > 0, "段表 %d 行" % len(seg)))
    check("seg_cols", "段表字段契约", lambda: need_cols(seg, SEG_REQUIRED))

    def _dedup() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        dup = int(quality.get("dup_rows", -1))
        dedup = int(quality.get("rows_dedup", -1))
        return (len(seg) == dedup and len(wide) - len(seg) == dup,
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 266 页
                "宽表 %d − 段表 %d = 重复 %d；质量报告 %d / %d"
                % (len(wide), len(seg), len(wide) - len(seg), dup, dedup))

    check("dedup", "去重口径一致", _dedup)

    def _finite() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        cols = ["kg_co2", "dist_km", "factor_gkm"]
        miss = [c for c in cols if c not in seg.columns]
        if miss:
            return False, "缺列：" + "、".join(miss)
        bad = int(seg[cols].isna().any(axis=1).sum())
        neg = int((pd.to_numeric(seg["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0) < 0).sum())
        zero = int((pd.to_numeric(seg["dist_km"], errors="coerce").fillna(0) <= 0).sum())
        return (bad == 0 and neg == 0 and zero == 0,
                "NaN %d｜负碳 %d｜零里程 %d" % (bad, neg, zero))

    check("finite", "碳排/里程无空值", _finite)

    def _kpi_total() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        total = float(pd.to_numeric(seg["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
        return (abs(total - float(kpi.get("total_kg") or 0)) < 1e-6,
                "段表 %.2f kg vs kpi %.2f kg" % (total, float(kpi.get("total_kg") or 0)))

    check("kpi_total", "E₀ 与 kpi 同源", _kpi_total)

    check("ledger_fields", "台账六字段",
          lambda: (all(c in ledger.columns for c in SIX_LEDGER) and len(ledger) >= 3,
                   "字段 %d 个｜%d 行" % (len(ledger.columns), len(ledger))))

    def _ledger_total() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        base = ledger[ledger.get("is_baseline", False) == True] if "is_baseline" in ledger.columns else ledger.head(1)
        if base.empty:
            return False, "没有基准行"
        emission = float(pd.to_numeric(base.iloc[0]["emission_t"], errors="coerce")) * 1000.0
        total = float(kpi.get("total_kg") or 0)
        return (abs(emission - total) <= 1.0,
                "台账基准 %.2f kg vs 第 1 页 %.2f kg" % (emission, total))

    check("ledger_total", "台账总碳 = 首页总碳", _ledger_total)
    check("disclosure_six", "披露六要素",
          lambda: (all(k in disclosure and str(disclosure[k]).strip() != "" for k in SIX_DISCLOSURE),
                   "命中 %d / 6" % sum(1 for k in SIX_DISCLOSURE
                                       if k in disclosure and str(disclosure[k]).strip() != "")))

    def _schemes() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        if schemes is None or len(schemes) < 3:
            return False, "方案数不足 3"
        for col in ("reduction_pct", "emission_t", "capex_wan", "payback_years"):
            if col not in schemes.columns:
                return False, "缺列：" + col
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 267 页
        red = pd.to_numeric(schemes["reduction_pct"], errors="coerce")
        em = pd.to_numeric(schemes["emission_t"], errors="coerce")
        pay = pd.to_numeric(schemes["payback_years"], errors="coerce")
        ok = (red.between(0, 100).all() and em.notna().all() and (em >= 0).all()
              and pay.notna().all() and (pay >= 0).all())
        return ok, "减排 %.1f–%.1f%%｜回本 %.1f–%.1f 年" % (red.min(), red.max(), pay.min(), pay.max())

    check("schemes", "方案表可对账", _schemes)

    def _scheme_emission() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        # 每个方案的排放必须对回 E₀ × (1 − 减排率)（原第 4 页的页面级自校验，
        # D4 迁入闸门：页面不再自算，改读本项结论）。
        if schemes is None or schemes.empty:
            return False, "方案表为空"
        if not {"emission_t", "reduction_pct"}.issubset(schemes.columns):
            return False, "缺列：emission_t / reduction_pct"
        e0_t = float(kpi.get("total_kg") or 0) / 1000.0
        red = pd.to_numeric(schemes["reduction_pct"], errors="coerce")
        em = pd.to_numeric(schemes["emission_t"], errors="coerce")
        expect = e0_t * (1.0 - red / 100.0)
        drift = float((em - expect).abs().max()) if len(schemes) else 0.0
        return (drift <= 0.01,
                "%d 个方案排放全部对回 E₀ × (1 − 减排率)，最大偏差 %.4f t" % (len(schemes), drift))

    check("scheme_emission", "方案排放对回 E₀", _scheme_emission)

    def _scheme_boundary() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        if schemes is None or schemes.empty or "boundary" not in schemes.columns:
            return False, "方案表缺 boundary 列"
        drift = schemes[(schemes["calculation_mode"] == "parameterized")
                        & (schemes["boundary"].astype(str) != BOUNDARY)]
        return (drift.empty,
                "参数化行边界与核算边界一致" if drift.empty
                else "边界漂移：" + "、".join(drift["scenario"].astype(str)))

    check("scheme_boundary", "参数化方案边界一致", _scheme_boundary)

    def _main_parameterized() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        # real 模式：主推行必须是逐车组参数化且过了运营约束（E4 闭环的最低保证）。
        # demo 模式：没有真实段表可派生车组，回退历史标定是预期行为，不算失败。
        if source != "real":
            return True, "演示模式无参数化主方案，回退历史标定（预期行为）"
        main = schemes[schemes["is_main"]] if schemes is not None and not schemes.empty else pd.DataFrame()
        if main.empty:
            return False, "没有主推行"
        row = main.iloc[0]
        ok = (str(row.get("calculation_mode")) == "parameterized"
              and bool(row.get("decision_eligible"))
              and str(row.get("operational_status")) == "pass")
        return ok, "%s｜%s｜decision_eligible=%s｜运营约束=%s" % (
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 268 页
            row.get("scenario"), row.get("calculation_mode"),
            row.get("decision_eligible"), row.get("operational_status"))

    check("main_parameterized", "主方案逐车组参数化且过约束", _main_parameterized)

    def _hotspots() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        if hotspots.empty or "dimension" not in hotspots.columns:
            return False, "热点表为空"
        dims = set(hotspots["dimension"].astype(str))
        need = {"车型", "线路", "月份", "里程段"}
        return (need <= dims), "维度：" + "、".join(sorted(dims))

    check("hotspots", "四维热点齐全", _hotspots)
    check("od_band", "同线带宽非空",
          lambda: (not band.empty and "ratio" in band.columns,
                   "可比较 OD %d 条" % len(band)))

    def _composition_total() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        # E₀ 构成下钻表（第 5 页）：全量组合的碳排必须对回 kpi 总碳，
        # 否则页面下钻出来的数会与第 1 页对不上。
        comp = _read_json(COMPOSITION_PATH)
        combos = comp.get("combos") or {}
        full = combos.get("all|all")
        if not full:
            return False, "构成表缺 all|all 组合"
        total = float(pd.to_numeric(seg["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
        drift = abs(float(full.get("kg") or 0.0) - total)
        return (drift <= 1.0,
                "全量组合 %.2f kg vs 段表 %.2f kg（偏差 %.4f）｜组合 %d 组"
                % (float(full.get("kg") or 0.0), total, drift, len(combos)))

    check("composition_total", "E₀ 构成下钻对回总碳", _composition_total)
    check("source", "数据来源标记",
          lambda: (source in ("real", "demo"), "source = " + str(source)))

    return items


def _route_items() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """T7 · 路线情景软校验 S1–S3（仅在 route_od.parquet 存在时启用）。

    这三项**不参与退出码判定**（避免 L1 情景问题拖垮 L0 主口径），
    但结论照常写进 gate_report.json，答辩时可现场翻出来看。

    S1 路线距离 vs 实测里程：中位 |Δ|/km ≤ 10%     —— 防止路线数据源错乱
    S2 路线碳 vs 基准碳：同向偏差、无系统性漂移    —— 防止因子口径串味
    S3 热点 OD 缓存率 ≥ 95%                       —— 防止"看起来有路线其实没数据"
    """
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    if not ROUTE_PATH.exists():
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 269 页
        return items

    route = _read_parquet(ROUTE_PATH)
    seg = _read_parquet(SEG_PATH)
    if route.empty or seg.empty:
        return items

    # ---- S1：路线距离 vs 宽表实测里程 ----
    s1_status, s1_detail = "SKIP", "路线表或段表为空"
    if "distance_km" in route.columns:
        rdist = pd.to_numeric(route["distance_km"], errors="coerce")
        valid = rdist > 0
        if valid.any():
            # 与段表按 OD 对齐（od_site 才是网点级连接键，od_key 是城市级）
            merged = seg.merge(route[["od_site", "distance_km"]],
                                left_on="od_site", right_on="od_site", how="inner")
            if not merged.empty:
                base = pd.to_numeric(merged["dist_km"], errors="coerce")
                line = pd.to_numeric(merged["distance_km"], errors="coerce")
                mask = (base > 0) & (line > 0)
                if mask.any():
                    # **按 OD 聚合**再算：同一 OD 的多段不该重复加权，
                    # 一个 OD 的路线距离只有一个值，与实测里程中位比才有意义。
                    per_od = (pd.DataFrame({
                        "od_site": merged.loc[mask, "od_site"].astype(str),
                        "base": base[mask],
                        "line": line[mask],
                    }).groupby("od_site")
                     .agg(base_km=("base", "median"), line_km=("line", "first")))
                    # 判据用**倍数比值**而不是相对偏差：
                    #   宽表里程 = 附件给的 OD 规划里程；高德里程 = 真实道路距离。
                    #   两者口径本就不同（真实道路会绕行/走高速），
                    #   用 |Δ|/km 衡量会把"口径差异"误判成"数据错误"。
                    #   倍数比值的中位偏离度（|ratio−1|）才回答"数据是否对得上"。
                    #
                    # 阈值 20% 的依据（实测）：
                    #   真路线 OD 的偏离中位 = 15.7%（两套口径的固有差异）
                    #   被合理性校验剔除的错配 OD 偏离中位 = 3246%（差 200 倍）
                    #   → 20% 既放得过正常差异，又抓得住真正的数据错乱。
                    ratio = per_od["line_km"] / per_od["base_km"]
                    med_ratio = float(ratio.median())
                    dev = float((ratio - 1.0).abs().median() * 100.0)
                    s1_status = "PASS" if dev <= 20.0 else "FAIL"
                    s1_detail = ("路线/实测倍数中位 %.2f（偏离 %.1f%%，目标 ≤20%%，"
                                 "比对 %d 个 OD）"
                                 % (med_ratio, dev, len(per_od)))
                else:
                    s1_detail = "无可比段"
            else:
                s1_detail = "OD 未对齐"
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 270 页
    items.append({"id": "S1", "label": "路线距离 vs 实测里程", "stage": "route",
                  "status": s1_status, "detail": s1_detail})

    # ---- S2：路线碳 vs 基准碳（同 OD 聚合，双向偏差不得系统性漂移） ----
    s2_status, s2_detail = "SKIP", "路线情景表未生成"
    _alloc_mode = ("baseline_mode" in seg.columns
                   and seg["baseline_mode"].astype(str).eq("physical_alloc_v1").any())
    if _alloc_mode:
        s2_status, s2_detail = "SKIP", "主口径已切 physical_alloc_v1；路线表仍按关联毛值，待重算"
    elif ROUTE_CARBON_PATH.exists():
        rc = _read_parquet(ROUTE_CARBON_PATH)
        if not rc.empty and "kg_co2_route_legal" in rc.columns and "flag_route_hit" in rc.columns:
            hit = rc[rc["flag_route_hit"] == 1]
            if not hit.empty:
                base = pd.to_numeric(hit["kg_co2"], errors="coerce")
                line = pd.to_numeric(hit["kg_co2_route_legal"], errors="coerce")
                mask = (base > 0) & (line > 0)
                if mask.any():
                    rel = ((line[mask] - base[mask]) / base[mask]) * 100.0
                    med = float(rel.median())
                    # 只判"系统性漂移"：中位偏差超过 ±50% 才算口径串味
                    s2_status = "PASS" if abs(med) <= 50.0 else "FAIL"
                    s2_detail = "中位偏差 %+.2f%%（阈值 ±50%%，比对 %d 段）" % (med, int(mask.sum()))
                else:
                    s2_detail = "无可比段"
            else:
                s2_detail = "无命中段"
    items.append({"id": "S2", "label": "路线碳 vs 基准碳（无系统漂移）", "stage": "route",
                  "status": s2_status, "detail": s2_detail})

    # ---- S3：热点 OD 缓存率 ----
    s3_status, s3_detail = "SKIP", "路线表为空"
    if not route.empty:
        total = len(route)
        hit = int((pd.to_numeric(route.get("flag_route_est", pd.Series(dtype=int)),
                                 errors="coerce").fillna(1) == 0).sum())
        rate = 100.0 * hit / max(total, 1)
        # 真路线占比；离线兜底不算"已缓存真路线"，但也不阻断
        s3_status = "PASS" if rate >= 95.0 else "WARN"
        s3_detail = "真路线 %d / %d（%.1f%%，目标 ≥95%%）；其余为离线兜底或坐标缺失" % (
            hit, total, rate)
    items.append({"id": "S3", "label": "热点 OD 真路线覆盖率", "stage": "route",
                  "status": s3_status, "detail": s3_detail})

    return items


def run_gate(strict: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    source = str(_read_json(SOURCE_PATH).get("source", "unknown"))
    mode = "strict" if strict else ("real" if source == "real" else "demo")
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 271 页
    wide = _read_parquet(WIDE_PATH)
    seg = _read_parquet(SEG_PATH)
    schemes = _read_parquet(SCHEMES_PATH)
    hotspots = _read_parquet(HOTSPOTS_PATH)
    band = _read_parquet(BAND_PATH)
    ledger = _read_parquet(LEDGER_PATH)
    disclosure = _read_json(DISCLOSURE_PATH)
    kpi = _read_json(KPI_PATH)
    quality = _read_json(QUALITY_PATH)

    targets = _target_items(kpi, schemes, "demo" if mode == "strict" and source != "real" else mode, strict)
    structural = _structural_items(wide, seg, schemes, hotspots, band, ledger,
                                   disclosure, kpi, quality, source)
    route_items = _route_items()
    counts = {
        "pass": sum(1 for x in targets + structural if x["status"] == "PASS"),
        "fail": sum(1 for x in targets + structural if x["status"] == "FAIL"),
        "skip": sum(1 for x in targets + structural if x["status"] == "SKIP"),
    }
    report = {
        "generated_at": _now(),
        "source": source,
        "mode": mode,
        "passed": counts["fail"] == 0,
        "targets": targets,
        "structural": structural,
        "route_checks": route_items,
        "counts": counts,
    }
    _write_json(GATE_PATH, report)
    return report


def _print_gate(report: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    mode_label = {"demo": "演示数据（真数据未接入，数据项跳过）",
                  "real": "实测数据（八项全开）",
                  "strict": "严格模式（演示数据也强制对拍）",
                  "error": "流水线中断"}.get(report.get("mode"), str(report.get("mode")))
    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print("G2 · 闸门｜模式：%s" % mode_label)
    print("-" * 78)
    for item in report.get("targets", []):
        unit = item.get("unit", "")
        print("  %s %-4s %s 目标 %-16s 实测 %s"
              % (_status_icon(item["status"]), item["status"], _pad(item["label"], 24),
                 _fmt(item["expected"], unit) + " " + unit,
                 _fmt(item["actual"], unit) + " " + unit))
    print("-" * 78)
    bad = [x for x in report.get("structural", []) if x["status"] != "PASS"]
    total = len(report.get("structural", []))
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 272 页
    print("  结构项：%d / %d 通过" % (total - len(bad), total))
    for item in bad:
        print("    [FAIL] %s：%s" % (item["label"], item.get("detail", "")))
    route_items = report.get("route_checks", [])
    if route_items:
        print("  路线情景软校验（T7 · 不参与退出码）：")
        for item in route_items:
            print("    %s %-4s %s：%s"
                  % (_status_icon(item["status"]), item["status"],
                     item["label"], item.get("detail", "")))
    counts = report.get("counts", {})
    print("  汇总：PASS %s｜FAIL %s｜SKIP %s"
          % (counts.get("pass", 0), counts.get("fail", 0), counts.get("skip", 0)))
    print("  结论：%s" % ("[OK] 通过" if report.get("passed") else "[FAIL] 未通过（退出码 1）"))
    print("=" * 78)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# G5 · 一键入口
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def run_pipeline(strict: bool = False, simulate_fail: Optional[int] = None,
                with_routes: bool = False) -> int:
    run_log: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "generated_at": _now(),
        "source": "unknown",
        "mode": "strict" if strict else "auto",
        "stages": [],
        "exit_code": None,
        "gate_passed": None,
        "_t0": time.perf_counter(),
    }
    print("=" * 78)
    print("物流企业碳感知智能决策系统 · 一键复算")
    print("时间：%s" % run_log["generated_at"])
    print("=" * 78)

    start_index = simulate_fail or -1
    for i, stage in enumerate(STAGES, start=1):
        rec = _run_stage(i, stage, simulate=(start_index == i))
        run_log["stages"].append(rec)
        if rec["status"] != "ok":
            run_log["source"] = str(_read_json(SOURCE_PATH).get("source", "unknown"))
            return _fail(run_log, "第 %d 段 %s" % (i, stage["name"]), rec["error"])

    # 报表段（G3/G4）也算一段：失败要能报出“报表段”
    print("\n[4/4] ④ 报表段（G3 台账 + G4 披露 + kpi）")
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        source = str(_read_json(SOURCE_PATH).get("source", "unknown"))
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 273 页
        quality = _read_json(QUALITY_PATH)
        wide = _read_parquet(WIDE_PATH)
        seg = _read_parquet(SEG_PATH)
        schemes = _read_parquet(SCHEMES_PATH)
        band = _read_parquet(BAND_PATH)
        if seg.empty:
            raise ValueError("baseline_carbon.parquet 为空")
        if schemes.empty:
            raise ValueError("final_report.parquet 为空")

        total_kg = float(pd.to_numeric(seg["kg_co2"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
        kpi = build_kpi(seg, wide, schemes, band, quality, source)
        ledger = build_ledger(schemes, total_kg, source)
        disclosure = build_disclosure(kpi, quality, source)
        _write_json(KPI_PATH, kpi)
        _save_parquet(ledger, LEDGER_PATH)
        _write_json(DISCLOSURE_PATH, disclosure)
        # 车队真实统计（计算器默认值）：失败不阻断主口径（降级纪律）
        try:
            fleet_stats = build_fleet_stats(seg, source)
            _write_json(FLEET_STATS_PATH, fleet_stats)
        except Exception:
            traceback.print_exc()
            _write_json(FLEET_STATS_PATH, {"source": source, "error": "fleet_stats 聚合失败",
                                           "note": "第 7/8 页将退回口径配置假设值"})
        # E₀ 构成下钻（第 5 页）：失败不阻断主口径（页面退回空态提示）
        try:
            composition = build_composition(seg)
            _write_json(COMPOSITION_PATH, composition)
        except Exception:
            traceback.print_exc()
            _write_json(COMPOSITION_PATH, {"source": source, "error": "composition 聚合失败",
                                           "combos": {}})
        seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
        print("      [OK] %.2f s｜台账 %d 行｜披露 %d 要素｜kpi %d 项｜构成组合 %d 组"
              % (seconds, len(ledger), len(SIX_DISCLOSURE), len(kpi),
                 len(composition.get("combos") or {})))
        run_log["stages"].append({
            "key": "reports", "name": "④ 报表段（G3 台账 + G4 披露）",
            "status": "ok", "seconds": round(seconds, 3), "error": "",
        })
    except Exception as exc:
        seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
        print("      [FAIL] 第 4 段失败：④ 报表段")
        traceback.print_exc()
        run_log["stages"].append({
            "key": "reports", "name": "④ 报表段（G3 台账 + G4 披露）",
            "status": "fail", "seconds": round(seconds, 3),
            "error": "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc),
        })
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 274 页
        run_log["source"] = str(_read_json(SOURCE_PATH).get("source", "unknown"))
        return _fail(run_log, "第 4 段 ④ 报表段", "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc))

    # T7 · 路线情景（可选段）：route_od.parquet 存在才跑，不存在打印 SKIP
    if with_routes:
        print("\n[5/5] ⑤ 路线情景段（T7 · L1/L2 口径）")
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            if not ROUTE_PATH.exists():
                print("      [SKIP] SKIP：未找到 route_od.parquet"
                      "（先跑 python -m tools.route_fetch）——主口径不受影响")
                run_log["stages"].append({
                    "key": "routes", "name": "⑤ 路线情景段（T7）",
                    "status": "skip", "seconds": 0.0,
                    "error": "缺少 route_od.parquet",
                })
            else:
                optimizer.build_routes()
                seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
                print("      [OK] %.2f s｜路线情景表已更新" % seconds)
                run_log["stages"].append({
                    "key": "routes", "name": "⑤ 路线情景段（T7）",
                    "status": "ok", "seconds": round(seconds, 3), "error": "",
                })
        except Exception as exc:
            seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
            print("      [FAIL] 第 5 段失败：⑤ 路线情景段")
            traceback.print_exc()
            run_log["stages"].append({
                "key": "routes", "name": "⑤ 路线情景段（T7）",
                "status": "fail", "seconds": round(seconds, 3),
                "error": "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc),
            })
            # 可选段失败**不阻断**主流程：L0 主口径照常出结果（降级纪律）
            print("      [!] 路线情景段失败，但主口径不受影响——继续出闸门结论")

    # 演示标记：前端据此显示“演示数据”
    if source == "real":
        try:
            MOCK_FLAG.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        except Exception:
            pass
    else:
        MOCK_FLAG.write_text("demo", encoding="utf-8")

    try:
        report = run_gate(strict=strict)
    except Exception as exc:
        traceback.print_exc()
        run_log["source"] = source
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 275 页
        return _fail(run_log, "第 4 段 ④ 报表段（闸门）",
                     "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc))
    _print_gate(report)

    run_log.update({
        "source": source,
        "exit_code": 0 if report.get("passed") else 1,
        "gate_passed": bool(report.get("passed")),
        "gate_counts": report.get("counts", {}),
        "total_seconds": round(time.perf_counter() - run_log.pop("_t0"), 3),
    })
    _write_json(RUN_LOG_PATH, run_log)
    print("\n本次耗时 %.2f s｜结果目录 %s" % (run_log["total_seconds"], PROCESSED))
    if report.get("passed"):
        print("流水线全部跑通 [OK]")
        return 0
    print("流水线跑完，但闸门未通过 [FAIL]（退出码 1）")
    return 1


def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="一键复算：三段调度 + 闸门 + 台账/披露")
    parser.add_argument("--strict", action="store_true",
                        help="严格模式：演示数据也强制对拍七个数（用于验证闸门会红）")
    parser.add_argument("--simulate-fail", type=int, choices=[1, 2, 3], default=None,
                        help="验收自测：模拟第 N 段失败（G1 定位段名）")
    parser.add_argument("--with-routes", action="store_true",
                        help="启用 T7 路线情景段（需先跑 python -m tools.route_fetch）")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    return run_pipeline(strict=args.strict, simulate_fail=args.simulate_fail,
                        with_routes=args.with_routes)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""数据层：原始表 → 宽表（全项目唯一的数据准备入口）。

铁律（按工程师踩坑意见落地）：
1. **脏数据绝不抛 Exception**：里程缺失、车型找不到、载重缺失、发票匹配不上、
   时长异常……全部用「同线历史均值 / 行业均值 / 车型额定值」填补，并打 FLAG；
2. **一次做完**：清洗、匹配、口径换算、标记都在本文件；下游只看宽表；
3. **只产出两张文件**：data/processed/wide_data.parquet + data_quality.json；
   碳因子与碳排属于“口径”，按蓝图 §2.2 修正一留在 optimizer.py；
4. **真数据不在就回退演示数据**：data/raw 为空或列名识别失败时，自动生成
   确定性的合成样本（带脏数据），并把来源写进 _source.json，前端明确标注。

FLAG 约定（全项目统一）：
    flag_dup       1 = 重复行（保留在宽表，基准核算剔除）
    flag_anomaly   1 = 时间戳 / 速度异常（保留计碳，只影响速度统计）
    flag_basis     1 = 载重口径换算（总质量 / 准牵引 → 载质量）
    flag_estimated 1 = 有字段被估算填补（前端黄框，estimate_note 写原因）
"""

class NoRawData(RuntimeError):
    """没有可用的原始数据——由上层回退演示数据，不向外抛。"""


def _as_str(series: Optional[pd.Series], index: pd.Index) -> pd.Series:
    if series is None:
        return pd.Series("", index=index, dtype=object)
    return series.astype("string").fillna("").str.strip().astype(object)


def _num_col(df: pd.DataFrame, name: str) -> pd.Series:
    if name in df.columns:
        return pd.to_numeric(df[name], errors="coerce")
    return pd.Series(np.nan, index=df.index, dtype=float)


def _to_datetime(series: Optional[pd.Series], index: pd.Index) -> pd.Series:
    if series is None:
        return pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    s = series
    if pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(s):
        # Excel 序列日期：1899-12-30 起算
        try:
            return pd.to_datetime(s, unit="D", origin="1899-12-30", errors="coerce")
        except Exception:
            return pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    try:
        return pd.to_datetime(s, errors="coerce")
    except Exception:
        return pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")


def _haversine(lat1: Any, lon1: Any, lat2: Any, lon2: Any) -> np.ndarray:
    def _arr(x: Any) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.asarray(pd.to_numeric(pd.Series(x), errors="coerce"), dtype=float)

    lat1 = np.radians(_arr(lat1))
    lon1 = np.radians(_arr(lon1))
    lat2 = np.radians(_arr(lat2))
    lon2 = np.radians(_arr(lon2))
    dlat = lat2 - lat1
    dlon = lon2 - lon1
    h = np.sin(dlat / 2.0) ** 2 + np.cos(lat1) * np.cos(lat2) * np.sin(dlon / 2.0) ** 2
    return 2.0 * 6371.0 * np.arcsin(np.sqrt(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)))

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 282 页

def _normalize_columns(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """把原始列名尽量映射到宽表契约列名；认不出的列原样保留。"""
    upper = {str(c).strip().lower(): c for c in df.columns}
    out = pd.DataFrame(index=df.index)
    for target, aliases in ALIASES.items():
        for alias in aliases:
            if alias in df.columns:
                out[target] = df[alias]
                break
            key = alias.strip().lower()
            if key in upper:
                out[target] = df[upper[key]]
                break
    return out


def _read_any(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
    if suffix == ".parquet":
        return pd.read_parquet(path)
    if suffix == ".csv":
        for enc in ("utf-8-sig", "gbk", "utf-8"):
            try:
                return pd.read_csv(path, encoding=enc)
            except Exception:
                continue
        raise ValueError("CSV 编码无法识别：" + path.name)
    raise ValueError("不支持的文件类型：" + path.name)


def _read_tables(path: Path) -> List[Tuple[str, pd.DataFrame]]:
    """Excel 逐 Sheet 识别（真实附件有隐藏 Sheet1 和封面页，不能只读第一页）。"""
    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
    if suffix in (".xlsx", ".xls"):
        book = pd.read_excel(path, sheet_name=None)
        return [(str(name), _normalize_columns(sheet)) for name, sheet in book.items()]
    return [(path.name, _normalize_columns(_read_any(path)))]


def _table_score(df: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
    """给每个 Sheet/文件打分：有运单号 + 有里程/起终点 的才可能是运单表。"""
    cols = set(df.columns)
    if "waybill_no" not in cols:
        return -1.0
    score = 4.0
    if "dist_km" in cols:
        score += 4.0
    if {"origin", "dest"} <= cols or {"origin_lat", "dest_lat"} <= cols:
        score += 2.0
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 283 页
    score += min(len(df) / 10000.0, 3.0)
    return score




def _find_col(df: pd.DataFrame, *needles: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """按列名包含关系找列：兼容 'waybill_number（运单号）' 这类带中文后缀的表头。"""
    for c in df.columns:
        name = str(c)
        if all(n in name for n in needles):
            return c
    return None


def _clean_city(series: Optional[pd.Series], index: pd.Index) -> pd.Series:
    s = _as_str(series, index)
    s = s.str.replace(r"[省市]$", "", regex=True)
    return s.str.strip()


def _site_city(site: Any, city_names: List[str]) -> str:
    s = str(site)
    for name in city_names:
        if name and s.startswith(name):
            return name
    return ""


def _gvw_for_type(vtype: Any, load_raw: Any) -> Tuple[float, str, str]:
    """附件2 的 75 车型 → 因子表总质量档位；provisional，待文献表 8 定稿。"""
    t = str(vtype)
    for key, gvw, basis, note in REAL_GVW_RULES:
        if key in t:
            return gvw, basis, note
    try:
        val = float(load_raw)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        val = 12.0
    if not np.isfinite(val) or val <= 0:
        val = 12.0
    return val, "载质量", "按附件2 登记载重"


def _load_competition_workbooks() -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, str, str]:
    """真实竞赛附件1/附件2：订单 + 运单 + 75 车型表 → 运单级原始表。

    数据事实（2026-10-18 实测）：
      - 订单 12,098 行；运单 36,329 行；订单/运单的 waybill_number 集合完全一致（直连 100%）；
      - 附件2 Sheet1 是 eclp↔tms 桥接，本副本里不是必需；仅作备份；
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 284 页
      - 去重键 = 运单+车牌+里程+封/解车时间 → 33,214 段 / 4,690,435.81 km；
      - 车型 68 种全部能在附件2 Sheet2 的 75 条里匹配到。
    """
    files = [p for p in sorted(RAW_DIR.glob("*"))
             if p.suffix.lower() in (".xlsx", ".xls")]
    f1_candidates = [p for p in files if "附件1" in p.name
                     or ("订单" in p.name and "运单" in p.name)]
    f2 = next((p for p in files if "附件2" in p.name or "运输车辆" in p.name), None)
    if not f1_candidates or not f2:
        raise NoRawData("未找到附件1/附件2 两个工作簿")

    # 若同时有原始附件（无“平均速度”列）和团队生成的平均速度副本，
    # 明确优先选择包含真实速度列的附件，避免仅依赖文件名排序导致输入不确定。
    candidates = []
    for candidate in f1_candidates:
        try:
            candidate_book = pd.read_excel(candidate, sheet_name=None)
            candidate_orders = next((df for df in candidate_book.values()
                                     if _find_col(df, "订单号") and _find_col(df, "运单号")), None)
            candidate_waybills = next((df for df in candidate_book.values()
                                       if _find_col(df, "实际行驶里程") and _find_col(df, "运单号")), None)
            if candidate_orders is None or candidate_waybills is None:
                continue
            candidate_speed_col = (_find_col(candidate_waybills, "平均速度")
                                   or _find_col(candidate_waybills, "speed_kmh"))
            score = (10 if candidate_speed_col else 0)
            score += 2 if ("平均数据" in candidate.name or "副本" in candidate.name) else 0
            # 稳定排序：分数优先；同分时按文件名顺序，确保每次选择一致。
            candidates.append((score, candidate.name, candidate, candidate_book,
                               candidate_orders, candidate_waybills, candidate_speed_col))
        except Exception:
            continue
    if not candidates:
        raise NoRawData("附件1 中未识别到同时包含订单号/运单号和里程的工作簿")
    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]), reverse=True)
    _score, _name, f1, book1, orders, waybills, selected_speed_col = candidates[0]

    book2 = pd.read_excel(f2, sheet_name=None)
    vehicles = next((df for df in book2.values()
                     if _find_col(df, "车型种类") and _find_col(df, "车辆载重")), None)
    if vehicles is None:
        raise NoRawData("附件2 的车型 Sheet 结构未识别")

    # ---- 订单：订单号 / 运单号 / 起终点城市 / 重量体积件数 ----
    oi = orders.index
    c_owb = _find_col(orders, "运单号")
    c_ono = _find_col(orders, "订单号")
    c_origin = _find_col(orders, "发货地市") or _find_col(orders, "shipper_city")
    c_dest = (_find_col(orders, "receiver_city") or _find_col(orders, "收货地市")
              or _find_col(orders, "收货地省"))
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 285 页
    c_weight = _find_col(orders, "订单重量")
    c_volume = _find_col(orders, "订单体积")
    c_pieces = _find_col(orders, "总包裹数")
    c_complete = _find_col(orders, "妥投时间")

    o = pd.DataFrame(index=oi)
    o["waybill_no"] = _as_str(orders[c_owb], oi)
    o["order_no"] = _as_str(orders[c_ono], oi) if c_ono else ""
    o["order_no_fallback"] = o["order_no"].eq("").astype(int)
    o["origin"] = _clean_city(orders[c_origin], oi) if c_origin else ""
    o["dest"] = _clean_city(orders[c_dest], oi) if c_dest else ""
    o["origin_site_fallback"] = o["origin"].eq("").astype(int)
    o["dest_site_fallback"] = o["dest"].eq("").astype(int)
    o["cargo_weight_kg"] = pd.to_numeric(orders[c_weight], errors="coerce") if c_weight else np.nan
    o["cargo_volume_cm3"] = pd.to_numeric(orders[c_volume], errors="coerce") if c_volume else np.nan
    o["cargo_pieces"] = pd.to_numeric(orders[c_pieces], errors="coerce") if c_pieces else np.nan
    o["cargo_weight_missing"] = o["cargo_weight_kg"].isna().astype(int)
    o["cargo_volume_missing"] = o["cargo_volume_cm3"].isna().astype(int)
    o["cargo_pieces_missing"] = o["cargo_pieces"].isna().astype(int)
    o["complete_ts"] = _to_datetime(orders[c_complete] if c_complete else None, oi)
    # 运单号只作为缺失订单号的追踪代理；原始缺失状态由 order_no_fallback 保留。
    o["order_no"] = o["order_no"].where(o["order_no"] != "", o["waybill_no"])
    o = o[o["waybill_no"] != ""].drop_duplicates("waybill_no", keep="first")

    # ---- 运单：单号 / 车牌 / 车型 / 里程 / 封解车时间 / 网点 / 速度 ----
    wi = waybills.index
    c_wb = _find_col(waybills, "waybill_number") or _find_col(waybills, "运单号")
    c_plate = _find_col(waybills, "vehicle_number") or _find_col(waybills, "车牌号")
    c_vtype = _find_col(waybills, "车型种类名称") or _find_col(waybills, "vehicle_type_name")
    c_vcode = _find_col(waybills, "车型种类编码")
    c_dist = _find_col(waybills, "实际行驶里程") or _find_col(waybills, "里程")
    c_dep = _find_col(waybills, "封车时间") or _find_col(waybills, "departure_time")
    c_arr = _find_col(waybills, "解封车时间") or _find_col(waybills, "arrival_time")
    c_dep_site = _find_col(waybills, "封车网点")
    c_arr_site = _find_col(waybills, "解封车网点")
    c_speed = (_find_col(waybills, "平均速度")
               or _find_col(waybills, "speed_kmh")
               or _find_col(waybills, "speed"))

    w = pd.DataFrame(index=wi)
    w["source_waybill_row"] = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series(wi, index=wi), errors="coerce").fillna(-1).astype(int) + 2
    w["waybill_no"] = _as_str(waybills[c_wb], wi)
    w["plate"] = _as_str(waybills[c_plate], wi) if c_plate else ""
    w["vehicle_type"] = _as_str(waybills[c_vtype], wi) if c_vtype else "未知车型"
    w["vtype_code"] = (pd.to_numeric(waybills[c_vcode], errors="coerce")
                       if c_vcode else 0)
    w["dist_km"] = pd.to_numeric(waybills[c_dist], errors="coerce") if c_dist else np.nan
    w["depart_ts"] = _to_datetime(waybills[c_dep] if c_dep else None, wi)
    w["arrive_ts"] = _to_datetime(waybills[c_arr] if c_arr else None, wi)
    w["dep_site"] = _as_str(waybills[c_dep_site], wi) if c_dep_site else ""
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 286 页
    w["arr_site"] = _as_str(waybills[c_arr_site], wi) if c_arr_site else ""
    w["speed_kmh"] = (pd.to_numeric(waybills[c_speed], errors="coerce")
                      if c_speed else np.nan)

    # 订单 → 运单：本附件版本的 waybill_number 集合可直接匹配；
    # 关联命中状态必须在回填 order_no 之前记录，不能用非空 order_no 反推匹配成功。
    order_keys = set(o["waybill_no"].astype(str))
    w["order_matched"] = w["waybill_no"].astype(str).isin(order_keys).astype(int)
    order_match = float(w["order_matched"].mean()) if len(w) else 0.0
    w = w.merge(o, on="waybill_no", how="left")

    # ---- 75 车型表：燃油类型 + 登记载重 → 因子表总质量档位 ----
    vi = vehicles.index
    c_vname = _find_col(vehicles, "车型种类")
    c_fuel = _find_col(vehicles, "燃油类型")
    c_load = _find_col(vehicles, "车辆载重")
    v = pd.DataFrame(index=vi)
    v["vehicle_type"] = _as_str(vehicles[c_vname], vi)
    v["fuel_type"] = _as_str(vehicles[c_fuel], vi) if c_fuel else "柴油"
    v["load_raw_t"] = pd.to_numeric(vehicles[c_load], errors="coerce") if c_load else np.nan
    v = v[(v["vehicle_type"] != "")].drop_duplicates("vehicle_type", keep="first")
    vehicle_keys = set(v["vehicle_type"].astype(str))
    w["vehicle_matched"] = w["vehicle_type"].astype(str).isin(vehicle_keys).astype(int)
    w = w.merge(v, on="vehicle_type", how="left")

    gvw: List[float] = []
    basis: List[str] = []
    vcls: List[int] = []
    mapping_method: List[str] = []
    for vtype, raw_load in zip(w["vehicle_type"].astype(str), w["load_raw_t"]):
        profile = VEHICLE_LOAD_CLASS_MAP.get(str(vtype))
        if profile is not None:
            load, cls = profile
            mapping_method.append("baseline_class_map")
            if "7.6" in str(vtype) and "冷藏" not in str(vtype):
                b = "整车总质量"
            elif "牵引" in str(vtype):
                b = "准牵引总质量"
            else:
                b = "载质量"
        else:
            load, b, _note = _gvw_for_type(vtype, raw_load)
            cls = 1 if load <= 5 else (2 if load <= 20 else 3)
            mapping_method.append("fallback_rule")
        gvw.append(load)
        basis.append(b)
        vcls.append(cls)
    w["load_t"] = gvw
    w["mass_basis"] = basis
    w["vclass"] = vcls
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 287 页
    w["load_mapping_method"] = mapping_method
    w["flag_load_mapping_fallback"] = (w["load_mapping_method"] != "baseline_class_map").astype(int)
    # “车型已在 Sheet2 找到”与“载重字段非空”是两件事，不能把后者当车型匹配标志。
    w["vehicle_known"] = w["vehicle_matched"].astype(int)
    w["fuel_type"] = w["fuel_type"].fillna("柴油").replace({"": "柴油"})

    # 订单城市优先；订单缺城市时用网点名前缀兜底。源城市缺失标志保留在宽表中。
    for flag_col in ("order_no_fallback", "cargo_weight_missing", "cargo_volume_missing",
                     "cargo_pieces_missing", "origin_site_fallback", "dest_site_fallback"):
        if flag_col in w.columns:
            w[flag_col] = pd.to_numeric(w[flag_col], errors="coerce").fillna(0).astype(int)
        else:
            w[flag_col] = 0
    city_names = sorted(CITY, key=len, reverse=True)
    for side, site_col in (("origin", "dep_site"), ("dest", "arr_site")):
        w[side] = w[side].fillna("").astype(str)
        miss = w[side] == ""
        if miss.any():
            w.loc[miss, side] = w.loc[miss, site_col].map(
                lambda s: _site_city(s, city_names))

    for col, default in (("order_no", ""), ("cargo_weight_kg", 0.0),
                         ("cargo_volume_cm3", 0.0), ("cargo_pieces", 0.0)):
        if col in w.columns:
            w[col] = w[col].fillna(default)
    w["data_source"] = "real"

    speed_provenance = ("速度取自列“%s”" % selected_speed_col
                        if selected_speed_col else "源文件无平均速度列，按里程/正时长反算速度")
    reason = ("读取 %s / %s（订单 %d 行，运单 %d 行，车型表 %d 条；订单直连率 %.1f%%；%s）"
              % (f1.name, f2.name, len(orders), len(waybills), len(v), order_match * 100,
                 speed_provenance))
    return w.reset_index(drop=True), "real", reason


def _load_raw() -> Tuple[pd.DataFrame, str, str]:
    """读 data/raw：优先按竞赛附件1/附件2 的表结构接，其次通用别名，再回退演示。"""
    if not RAW_DIR.exists():
        raise NoRawData("data/raw 目录不存在")

    try:
        return _load_competition_workbooks()
    except NoRawData:
        pass
    except Exception as exc:
        print("  [P1] 竞赛附件读取失败（%s），尝试通用读取" % type(exc).__name__)

    candidates: List[Path] = []
    for stem in ("waybills", "waybill", "运单", "orders"):
        for ext in (".parquet", ".csv", ".xlsx", ".xls"):
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 288 页
            p = RAW_DIR / (stem + ext)
            if p.exists():
                candidates.append(p)
    if not candidates:
        candidates = [
            p for p in sorted(RAW_DIR.iterdir())
            if p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in (".parquet", ".csv", ".xlsx", ".xls")
        ]
    if not candidates:
        raise NoRawData("data/raw 没有 .parquet/.csv/.xlsx 文件")

    best: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None
    best_score = -1.0
    best_meta = ""
    errors: List[str] = []
    for path in candidates[:3]:
        try:
            for sheet_name, table in _read_tables(path):
                score = _table_score(table)
                if score > best_score:
                    best, best_score = table, score
                    best_meta = path.name if sheet_name == path.name else path.name + " / " + sheet_name
        except Exception as exc:  # 单个文件坏掉不拖垮整条链
            errors.append(path.name + ": " + type(exc).__name__)
            continue

    if best is None or best_score < 0:
        detail = ("；".join(errors) if errors else "未识别到 waybill_no + 里程/起终点 列")
        raise NoRawData("原始文件列名未识别（" + detail + "）")

    raw = best.reset_index(drop=True)
    raw["data_source"] = "real"
    return raw, "real", "读取 %s（%d 行）" % (best_meta, len(raw))
