# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""D6 · 静态预览：一张 OSM 路线 + 道路等级着色图。

任务书 §3 D6 说这是**可选**交付物，且"不改第 6 页"。
本脚本只读已生成的结果表，产出一个自包含 HTML 文件，
双击即可看（不需要起 Streamlit）。

用法
----
    python -m tools.baseline_preview --out outputs/figures/baseline_sh_bj.html

输出
----
* ``outputs/figures/baseline_sh_bj.html`` —— 自包含预览（OpenLayers CDN）
* 同时可选导出 PNG（需要浏览器，--png）

纪律
----
* 只读 ``data/processed/route_baseline_sh_bj_*``，不重算碳排；
* 不改第 6 页；
* 原始 OSRM 响应与瓦片缓存不进 Git。

参考：docs/35_上海北京基线_任务书.md D6。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

APP_DIR = ROOT / "app"
if str(APP_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(APP_DIR))

import pandas as pd  # noqa: E402

from components import baseline_map, basemap_align, olmapview  # noqa: E402

PROCESSED = ROOT / "data" / "processed"
STEPS_PATH = PROCESSED / "route_baseline_sh_bj_steps.parquet"
SUMMARY_PATH = PROCESSED / "route_baseline_sh_bj_summary.json"


碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 346 页
def main(argv: List[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="python -m tools.baseline_preview",
        description="上海—北京 OSM 基线静态预览（D6）")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default=str(ROOT / "outputs" / "figures" / "baseline_sh_bj.html"),
                        help="输出 HTML 路径")
    parser.add_argument("--height", default=820, type=int, help="地图高度（px）")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    if not STEPS_PATH.exists() or not SUMMARY_PATH.exists():
        print("基线表还没生成，先跑 python -m tools.route_baseline", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    steps = pd.read_parquet(STEPS_PATH)
    summary = json.loads(SUMMARY_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    token = olmapview.load_tianditu_token()
    # 底图优先级：天地图（CGCS2000，零偏移，需 token）> 高德栅格（GCJ-02，服务端对齐）。
    # 两者都是国内持牌服务，都不会出现"路线偏在路边"。
    if token:
        layers = olmapview.default_tianditu_layers(token)
        basemap_desc = "天地图 WMTS（CGCS2000，零偏移）"
    else:
        layers = [basemap_align.amap_xyz_layer()]
        basemap_desc = "高德栅格（GCJ-02，已做基准对齐）"
    payload = baseline_map.build_baseline_payload(steps, summary, layers=layers)
    payload["attribution"] = str(summary.get("attribution") or "")

    doc = olmapview.build_doc(
        payload,
        height=int(args.height),
        title="上海—北京 OSM 碳基线 · 道路等级着色",
        fallback_note=("底图：%s" % basemap_desc),
    )
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(doc, encoding="utf-8")

    print("预览已生成: %s" % out)
    print("  feature 数 : %d" % len(payload["features"]))
    print("  底图       : %s" % basemap_desc)
    print("  几何基准   : %s" % payload.get("geomCrs"))
    print("  道路等级   : %s" % payload["road_class_share"])
    print("  署名       : © OpenStreetMap contributors")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build the HYC physical-trip audit layer from D:\\vsp_delivery.

This is an AUDIT layer, not the main E0. It never overwrites baseline_carbon.parquet.
Run:
    python -m tools.build_vsp_audit --src D:\\vsp_delivery
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROCESSED = ROOT / "data" / "processed"
BASELINE = PROCESSED / "baseline_carbon.parquet"
DEFAULT_SRC = Path(r"D:\vsp_delivery")

HYC_COLS = [
    "trip_id", "waybill", "customer", "plate", "vtype", "vtype_name", "km",
    "dep", "arr", "o", "d", "n_assoc", "vtype_mode", "fuel", "group", "ef",
    "e_kg", "hours", "speed", "speed_flag", "band", "month", "e_t",
]


def _physical_key(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = df.copy()
    df["plate_s"] = df["plate"].astype(str).str.strip()
    df["dist_s"] = pd.to_numeric(df["km"], errors="coerce").round(2)
    df["dep_s"] = pd.to_datetime(df["dep"], errors="coerce").astype("string")
    df["arr_s"] = pd.to_datetime(df["arr"], errors="coerce").astype("string")
    df["o_s"] = df["o"].astype(str).str.strip()
    df["d_s"] = df["d"].astype(str).str.strip()
    return df


def _baseline_key(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = df.copy()
    df["plate_s"] = df["plate"].astype(str).str.strip()
    df["dist_s"] = pd.to_numeric(df["dist_km"], errors="coerce").round(2)
    df["dep_s"] = pd.to_datetime(df["depart_ts"], errors="coerce").astype("string")
    df["arr_s"] = pd.to_datetime(df["arrive_ts"], errors="coerce").astype("string")
    df["o_s"] = df["dep_site"].astype(str).str.strip()
    df["d_s"] = df["arr_site"].astype(str).str.strip()
    return df

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 348 页

KEYS = ["plate_s", "dist_s", "dep_s", "arr_s", "o_s", "d_s"]


def build(src: Path = DEFAULT_SRC, out: Path = PROCESSED) -> dict:
    src = Path(src)
    model = src / "improved_model.xlsx"
    if not model.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"missing {model}")
    if not BASELINE.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"missing {BASELINE}")

    b = _baseline_key(pd.read_parquet(BASELINE))
    h = pd.read_excel(model, sheet_name=0)
    if len(h.columns) != len(HYC_COLS):
        raise ValueError(f"unexpected HYC columns: {len(h.columns)}")
    h.columns = HYC_COLS
    h = _physical_key(h)

    # Current waybill-association口径 (gross, before physical allocation)
    gross = b["kg_co2_gross"] if "kg_co2_gross" in b.columns else b["kg_co2"]
    current_rows = int(len(b))
    current_km = float(b["dist_km"].sum())
    current_t = float(gross.sum() / 1000.0)

    # Current physical-trip口径. New baseline is allocated per association,
    # so aggregate by trip_id; old baseline falls back to first-row dedupe.
    if "trip_id" in b.columns:
        trip_legal = b.groupby("trip_id", dropna=False)["kg_co2"].sum()
        trip_vsp = b.groupby("trip_id", dropna=False)["kg_co2_phys"].sum()
        trip_km = b.groupby("trip_id", dropna=False)["trip_km"].first()
        physical_trips = int(b["trip_id"].nunique())
        physical_km = float(trip_km.sum())
        physical_t = float(trip_legal.sum() / 1000.0)
        vsp_t = float(trip_vsp.sum() / 1000.0)
        first = b.drop_duplicates(KEYS, keep="first").copy()
    else:
        first = b.drop_duplicates(KEYS, keep="first").copy()
        physical_trips = int(len(first))
        physical_km = float(first["dist_km"].sum())
        physical_t = float(first["kg_co2"].sum() / 1000.0)
        vsp_t = float(first["kg_co2_phys"].sum() / 1000.0)
    vsp_all_t = float((b["kg_co2_phys_gross"] if "kg_co2_phys_gross" in b.columns else b["kg_co2_phys"]).sum() / 1000.0)

    # HYC physical口径
    hyc_trips = int(len(h))
    hyc_km = float(h["km"].sum())
    hyc_t = float(h["e_kg"].sum() / 1000.0)

    # HYC factors applied to current association rows (auxiliary)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 349 页
    hf = h[KEYS + ["ef"]].copy()
    aux = b.merge(hf, on=KEYS, how="left")
    if aux["ef"].isna().any():
        raise ValueError(f"unmatched HYC factors: {int(aux['ef'].isna().sum())}")
    hyc_aux_t = float((pd.to_numeric(aux["dist_km"], errors="coerce") * pd.to_numeric(aux["ef"], errors="coerce") / 1_000_000.0).sum())

    duplicate_effect = current_t - physical_t
    factor_effect = physical_t - hyc_t
    net_delta = current_t - hyc_t
    vsp_effect = vsp_t - physical_t

    # group summary from HYC physical trips
    g = h.groupby("group", dropna=False).agg(
        trips=("trip_id", "count"),
        km=("km", "sum"),

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""坐标转换的**裁判实验**：哪套 GCJ-02→WGS-84 转换是地理正确的？

背景
----
docs/35 §2 写死的"预期 WGS"（上海 ``121.317487, 31.011123``）是作者用
``_scratch/osrm_spike.py`` 里**一个把两个多项式接反**的转换算出来的。
本工具用**客观证据**判定哪套转换对，而不是靠"我觉得"。

裁判原理
--------
项目 ``data/processed/route_od.parquet`` 里存着 **AMap 的真实路线折线**
（GCJ-02）；OSRM 给的是同一条 OD 的 **WGS-84 路线**。
两者描述的是**同一批真实公路**，所以：

1. 取一条 **短 OD**（20–200 km）—— 长 OD 上 AMap 与 OSRM 可能选不同高速，
   路线本身就不同，偏差会被误判成坐标误差；
2. 把 AMap 折线用候选逆变换转到 WGS-84；
3. 把 OSRM 路线按候选正变换映射回 GCJ-02；
4. 逐点算 OSRM 点到 AMap 折线 **线段** 的垂距（点到点不行：折线被 RDP
   抽稀，点到点距离会被抽样间距污染）；
5. **垂距中位数最小的候选 = 地理正确的转换**。

实测结果（2026-10-22）
----------------------
=============================== ==================================
候选                            中位垂距
=============================== ==================================
**标准实现（本项目采用）**       **1.6 / 2.6 / 10.1 / 2.9 / 6.8 m** ✅
作者 spike 版（tx/ty 接反）      415 – 929 m ❌
完全不转换（GCJ 当 WGS 用）      201 – 460 m ❌
=============================== ==================================

米级重合 vs 近 1 km 偏差，结论没有争议。

用法
----
    python -m tools.coord_truth            # 跑裁判实验（需要网络）
    python -m tools.coord_truth --offline   # 只跑本地几何校验（不需要网络）

参考：docs/36_上海北京基线_交付说明.md §3.1。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import math
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 353 页
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from src.routing import coord  # noqa: E402

PI = math.pi
A = 6378245.0
EE = 0.00669342162296594323


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 候选变换


def _tx(x: float, y: float) -> float:
    """纬度变换多项式（国测局算法）。"""
    r = (-100.0 + 2.0 * x + 3.0 * y + 0.2 * y * y + 0.1 * x * y
         + 0.2 * math.sqrt(abs(x)))
    r += (20.0 * math.sin(6.0 * x * PI) + 20.0 * math.sin(2.0 * x * PI)) * 2.0 / 3.0
    r += (20.0 * math.sin(y * PI) + 40.0 * math.sin(y / 3.0 * PI)) * 2.0 / 3.0
    r += (160.0 * math.sin(y / 12.0 * PI) + 320 * math.sin(y * PI / 30.0)) * 2.0 / 3.0
    return r


def _ty(x: float, y: float) -> float:
    """经度变换多项式（国测局算法）。"""
    r = (300.0 + x + 2.0 * y + 0.1 * x * x + 0.1 * x * y
         + 0.1 * math.sqrt(abs(x)))
    r += (20.0 * math.sin(6.0 * x * PI) + 20.0 * math.sin(2.0 * x * PI)) * 2.0 / 3.0
    r += (20.0 * math.sin(x * PI) + 40.0 * math.sin(x / 3.0 * PI)) * 2.0 / 3.0
    r += (150.0 * math.sin(x / 12.0 * PI) + 300.0 * math.sin(x / 30.0 * PI)) * 2.0 / 3.0
    return r


def _scale(lng: float, lat: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    radlat = lat / 180.0 * PI
    magic = math.sin(radlat)
    magic = 1 - EE * magic * magic
    return math.sqrt(magic), radlat, magic


def standard_forward(lng: float, lat: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    """标准：WGS→GCJ，dlat=tx，dlng=ty。**这是权威方向**。"""
    dlat = _tx(lng - 105.0, lat - 35.0)
    dlng = _ty(lng - 105.0, lat - 35.0)
    sm, radlat, magic = _scale(lng, lat)
    dlat = (dlat * 180.0) / ((A * (1 - EE)) / (magic * sm) * PI)
    dlng = (dlng * 180.0) / (A / sm * math.cos(radlat) * PI)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 354 页
    return lng + dlng, lat + dlat


def swapped_forward(lng: float, lat: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    """作者 spike 版：dlat=ty，dlng=tx（**接反**）。仅作对照用。"""
    dlat = _ty(lng - 105.0, lat - 35.0)
    dlng = _tx(lng - 105.0, lat - 35.0)
    sm, radlat, magic = _scale(lng, lat)
    dlat = (dlat * 180.0) / ((A * (1 - EE)) / (magic * sm) * PI)
    dlng = (dlng * 180.0) / (A / sm * math.cos(radlat) * PI)
    return lng + dlng, lat + dlat


def invert(lng: float, lat: float, forward, iters: int = 10) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    """用正变换迭代反解 WGS-84。"""
    wlng, wlat = float(lng), float(lat)
    for _ in range(iters):
        glng, glat = forward(wlng, wlat)
        d = (glng - lng, glat - lat)
        wlng -= d[0]
        wlat -= d[1]
        if abs(d[0]) < 1e-11 and abs(d[1]) < 1e-11:
            break
    return wlng, wlat


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 几何


def seg_dist_m(p, a, b) -> float:
    """点到线段的近似垂距（米）。"""
    lat0 = math.radians((a[1] + b[1]) * 0.5)
    kx = 111320.0 * math.cos(lat0)
    ky = 110574.0
    px, py = (p[0] - a[0]) * kx, (p[1] - a[1]) * ky
    bx, by = (b[0] - a[0]) * kx, (b[1] - a[1]) * ky
    dx, dy = bx, by
    L2 = dx * dx + dy * dy
    if L2 <= 0:
        return math.hypot(px, py)
    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, (px * dx + py * dy) / L2))
    return math.hypot(px - t * dx, py - t * dy)


def polyline_dist_m(p, pts) -> float:
    """点到整条折线的最小垂距。"""
    best = 1e18
    for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
        d = seg_dist_m(p, pts[i], pts[i + 1])
        if d < best:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 355 页
            best = d
    return best


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 实验


def pick_short_ods(limit: int = 6) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """挑若干条 20–200 km 的 AMap 真路线（短 OD，路线本身不容易分叉）。"""
    df = pd.read_parquet(ROOT / "data" / "processed" / "route_od.parquet")
    mask = ((df["source"] == "amap_rest")
            & (df["distance_km"].astype(float) > 20)
            & (df["distance_km"].astype(float) < 200))
    return df[mask].head(limit)


def run_offline_check() -> int:
    """不需要网络的本地校验：确认三个端点的转换结果。"""
    print("=== 三个端点的转换结果（标准实现）===")
    cases = [
        ("上海车墩", 121.315087, 31.014995),
        ("北京通州", 116.664264, 39.909698),
    ]
    bad = 0
    for name, lng, lat in cases:
        w = coord.gcj02_to_wgs84(lng, lat)
        swapped = invert(lng, lat, swapped_forward)
        err = coord.haversine_km(w[0], w[1], swapped[0], swapped[1]) * 1000.0
        print("  %-8s GCJ(%.6f, %.6f) → 标准 WGS(%.6f, %.6f)"
              % (name, lng, lat, w[0], w[1]))
        print("           接反版 WGS(%.6f, %.6f)  两者相差 %.0f m"
              % (swapped[0], swapped[1], err))
        if err < 100.0:
            bad += 1
            print("           ⚠️ 与接反版几乎一致 —— 实现可能被改坏了")
    print("")
    print("=== 往返精度 ===")
    for name, lng, lat in cases:
        w = coord.gcj02_to_wgs84(lng, lat)
        back = coord.wgs84_to_gcj02(w[0], w[1])
        err = coord.haversine_km(lng, lat, back[0], back[1]) * 1000.0
        print("  %-8s 往返误差 %.4f m %s" % (name, err, "✅" if err < 1.0 else "❌"))
        if err >= 1.0:
            bad += 1
    return 1 if bad else 0


def run_judge(limit: int = 6) -> int:
    """裁判实验（需要网络拉 OSRM）。"""
    try:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 356 页
        import requests
    except ImportError:
        print("需要 requests", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    cand = pick_short_ods(limit)
    if cand.empty:
        print("没有可用的 AMap 路线做对照", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    print("选取 OD：")
    for _, r in cand.iterrows():
        print("  - %s（%.0f km）" % (r["od_key"], float(r["distance_km"])))

    agg: Dict[str, List[float]] = {"standard": [], "spike_bug": [], "no_convert": []}
    skipped: List[str] = []
    for _, row in cand.iterrows():
        pts = [tuple(map(float, c.split(",")))
               for c in str(row["polyline"]).split(";") if "," in c]
        if len(pts) < 5:
            continue
        o = invert(float(row["o_lng"]), float(row["o_lat"]), standard_forward)
        d = invert(float(row["d_lng"]), float(row["d_lat"]), standard_forward)
        url = ("https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/driving/"
               "%f,%f;%f,%f?overview=full&geometries=geojson&steps=false"
               % (o[0], o[1], d[0], d[1]))
        try:
            j = requests.get(url, timeout=90,
                             headers={"User-Agent": "carbon-tide-baseline/1.0"}).json()
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            print("  OSRM 失败：%s" % exc)
            continue
        if j.get("code") != "Ok":
            print("  OSRM %s：%s" % (j.get("code"), row["od_key"]))
            continue
        coords = [(c[0], c[1]) for c in j["routes"][0]["geometry"]["coordinates"]]
        samples = coords[::max(1, len(coords) // 120)]

        medians = {}
        for name, forward in (("standard", standard_forward),
                              ("spike_bug", swapped_forward)):
            conv = [invert(x, y, forward) for (x, y) in pts]
            ds = sorted(polyline_dist_m(p, conv) for p in samples)
            medians[name] = ds[len(ds) // 2]
        ds = sorted(polyline_dist_m(p, pts) for p in samples)
        medians["no_convert"] = ds[len(ds) // 2]

        # ---- 分叉剔除 ----
        # 如果连"标准实现"的垂距都很大（>100 m），说明这条 OD 上
        # AMap 与 OSRM **选了不同的路**（不同高速/不同绕行），
        # 偏差来自路线分叉而不是坐标误差 —— 这条样本对判定没有贡献，
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 357 页
        # 应当剔除，否则会用噪声稀释结论。
        if medians["standard"] > 100.0:
            skipped.append("%s（两源路线分叉，标准实现垂距 %.0f m）"
                           % (row["od_key"], medians["standard"]))
            continue

        print("  %s" % row["od_key"])
        for name in ("standard", "spike_bug", "no_convert"):
            agg[name].append(medians[name])
            print("      %-10s 中位垂距 %8.1f m" % (name, medians[name]))

    if skipped:
        print("")
        print("剔除的样本（两源路线分叉，与坐标误差无关）：")
        for item in skipped:
            print("  - %s" % item)

    print("")
    print("=== 汇总 ===")
    for name, values in agg.items():
        if values:
            print("  %-12s 平均中位垂距 = %8.1f m" % (name, sum(values) / len(values)))
    best = min(((sum(v) / len(v), k) for k, v in agg.items() if v), default=(0, ""))
    print("")
    print("裁判结论：%s 垂距最小 → 地理正确" % (best[1] if best else "无"))
    return 0


def main(argv: List[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="python -m tools.coord_truth",
        description="坐标转换的裁判实验（docs/36 §3.1）")
    parser.add_argument("--offline", action="store_true",
                        help="只跑本地校验，不联网")
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=6, help="参与判定的 OD 条数")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    rc = run_offline_check()
    if rc:
        return rc
    if args.offline:
        return 0
    print("")
    return run_judge(args.limit)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Git Data API 推送器：github.com 主站不通时，走 api.github.com 提交。

为什么需要这个：本机到 github.com:443 的链路时通时不通（DNS 解析到 IPv6 地址后连不上），
但 api.github.com 一直可用。git push 走的是 github.com，推不动；
这个脚本用 GitHub REST 的 Git Data API 完成同样的事——把本地某个 commit 的内容
原样推到远端分支。

它**不重新打包**：
  - 直接取本地 commit 的 tree SHA；
  - 若远端已有该 commit 及其祖先（tree 可达），则无需动作；
  - 否则用「ref → tree」的等价路径重建：用远端 base tree + 本地 commit 的 diff
    生成新 tree，再创建 commit，最后快进更新 ref。

**安全**：token 只从 git credential manager 取，不落盘、不打印。
用法：
    python -m tools.git_push_api --branch integration/da-zg-ui
    python -m tools.git_push_api --branch integration/da-zg-ui --dry-run
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import base64
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
API = "https://api.github.com"
REPO = "Zhouyx-0123/2027-challenge-cup"


def _git(*args: str, input_text: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, input=input_text,
                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("git %s 失败：%s" % (" ".join(args), proc.stderr.strip()[:300]))
    return proc.stdout


def get_credentials() -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """从 git credential manager 取用户名与 token（不落盘、不打印）。"""
    out = _git("credential", "fill", input_text="protocol=https\nhost=github.com\n\n")
    username, password = "", ""
    for line in out.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("username="):
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 359 页
            username = line.split("=", 1)[1]
        elif line.startswith("password="):
            password = line.split("=", 1)[1]
    if not password:
        raise RuntimeError("取不到 GitHub 凭据")
    return username, password


class GitHubAPI:
    def __init__(self, token: str):
        self.token = token

    def _request(self, method: str, path: str, payload: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Any:
        url = API + path
        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8") if payload is not None else None
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method)
        req.add_header("Authorization", "Bearer " + self.token)
        req.add_header("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json")
        req.add_header("X-GitHub-Api-Version", "2022-11-28")
        req.add_header("User-Agent", "workbuddy-git-push")
        if data is not None:
            req.add_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as resp:
                body = resp.read().decode("utf-8")
                return json.loads(body) if body else {}
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            raise RuntimeError("HTTP %s %s %s → %s"
                               % (exc.code, method, path, detail[:600])) from exc

    def get_ref(self, branch: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return self._request("GET", "/repos/%s/git/ref/heads/%s" % (REPO, branch))

    def get_commit(self, sha: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return self._request("GET", "/repos/%s/git/commits/%s" % (REPO, sha))

    def get_tree(self, sha: str, recursive: str = "1") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return self._request("GET", "/repos/%s/git/trees/%s?recursive=%s" % (REPO, sha, recursive))

    def create_blob(self, content: bytes) -> str:
        payload = {"content": base64.b64encode(content).decode("ascii"),
                   "encoding": "base64"}
        return self._request("POST", "/repos/%s/git/blobs" % REPO, payload)["sha"]

    def create_tree(self, base_tree: str, entries: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
        payload = {"base_tree": base_tree, "tree": entries}
        return self._request("POST", "/repos/%s/git/trees" % REPO, payload)["sha"]

    def create_commit(self, message: str, tree: str, parents: List[str],
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 360 页
                      author: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> str:
        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "message": message, "tree": tree, "parents": parents,
        }
        if author:
            payload["author"] = author
        return self._request("POST", "/repos/%s/git/commits" % REPO, payload)["sha"]

    def update_ref(self, branch: str, sha: str, force: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        payload = {"sha": sha, "force": force}
        return self._request("PATCH", "/repos/%s/git/refs/heads/%s" % (REPO, branch), payload)


def local_commit_info(ref: str) -> Tuple[str, str, str, str]:
    """返回 (commit_sha, tree_sha, parent_sha, message)。"""
    sha = _git("rev-parse", ref).strip()
    tree = _git("rev-parse", ref + "^{tree}").strip()
    parents = _git("rev-list", "--parents", "-n", "1", ref).strip().split()[1:]
    message = _git("log", "-1", "--pretty=%B", ref)
    return sha, tree, (parents[0] if parents else ""), message.strip()


def file_list(tree_sha: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """本地 tree → {path: blob_sha}。

    **必须加 -z**：git 默认把非 ASCII 路径转义成 "\\346\\241\\243..." 的八进制形式
    （core.quotepath），与远端的原始 UTF-8 路径对不上，会导致所有中文文件
    被误判成"远端有、本地无"→ 全被删掉。用 -z 拿 NUL 分隔的原始字节路径。
    """
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "-z", tree_sha], cwd=ROOT,
                          capture_output=True, timeout=120)
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for record in proc.stdout.split(b"\x00"):
        if not record:
            continue
        meta, _, raw_path = record.partition(b"\t")
        parts = meta.split()
        if len(parts) >= 3 and parts[1] == b"blob":
            path = raw_path.decode("utf-8")
            mapping[path] = parts[2].decode("ascii")
    return mapping


def _file_mode(path: str) -> str:
    """取文件在 git tree 里的 mode：普通文件 100644 / 可执行 100755 / symlink 120000。"""
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", "ls-files", "-s", "-z", "--", path], cwd=ROOT,
                          capture_output=True, timeout=60)
    for record in proc.stdout.split(b"\x00"):
        if not record:
            continue
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 361 页
        meta, _, _ = record.partition(b"\t")
        parts = meta.split()
        if parts and parts[0].isdigit():
            return parts[0].decode("ascii")
    return "100644"


def _align_local_ref(branch: str, remote_sha: str) -> None:
    """把本地 origin/<branch> 指到远端实际的新 commit（尽力而为）。

    两个已知限制，如实说明，不粉饰：
      1. 走 API 建的 commit 与本地 commit 是**不同的 SHA 对象**（内容一致，但
         author/committer 时间戳不同），所以本地分支与远端分支永远不可能 0/0；
      2. 远端 commit 对象不在本地 git 对象库里，`git update-ref` 指向它会被 git 拒绝
         （exit 128），本地 ref 对齐大概率失败——这是预期内的，不影响远端内容。

    结论：**远端文件内容已与本地完全一致**，这才是"推送成功"的实质。
    下次 github.com 通了，用 `git push` 可把本地这条 commit 正式推上去并让 SHA 对齐。
    """
    ref = "refs/remotes/origin/%s" % branch
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", "update-ref", ref, remote_sha], cwd=ROOT,
                          capture_output=True, timeout=60)
    if proc.returncode == 0:
        print("  · 本地 origin/%s 已指向远端新 commit" % branch)
    else:
        print("  · 本地 ref 未对齐（预期内：远端 commit 对象不在本地库，git 拒绝指向它）")
    print("  · 注：走 API 建的 commit 与本地 commit SHA 不同（时间戳不同）但内容一致，")
    print("    故本地分支会显示领先 1 个等价提交。**远端文件内容已完全同步**；")
    print("    下次 github.com 通了跑一次 `git push` 即可让 SHA 也对齐。")


def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="用 Git Data API 推送（github.com 不通时的替代路径）")
    parser.add_argument("--branch", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    print("=" * 78)
    print("Git Data API 推送器 → %s @ %s" % (REPO, args.branch))
    print("=" * 78)

    username, token = get_credentials()
    api = GitHubAPI(token)

    try:
        ref = api.get_ref(args.branch)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        print("  [FAIL] 取远端分支失败：HTTP %s" % exc.code)
        return 1
    remote_sha = ref["object"]["sha"]
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 362 页
    print("  远端 HEAD ：%s" % remote_sha[:10])

    local_sha, local_tree, local_parent, message = local_commit_info("HEAD")
    print("  本地 HEAD ：%s" % local_sha[:10])
    print("  提交信息 ：%s" % (message.splitlines()[0] if message else "(空)"))

    if local_sha == remote_sha:
        print("  [OK] 已是最新，无需推送")
        return 0

    # --- 两边的文件清单（快进/重建两条路都要用）---
    remote_commit = api.get_commit(remote_sha)
    remote_tree = remote_commit["tree"]["sha"]
    remote_files = {e["path"]: e["sha"] for e in api.get_tree(remote_tree).get("tree", [])
                    if e.get("type") == "blob"}
    local_files = file_list(local_tree)
    print("  远端文件 %d 个 / 本地文件 %d 个" % (len(remote_files), len(local_files)))

    if local_parent and local_parent == remote_sha:
        print("  关系：本地是远端的直接子提交（快进）")
        if args.dry_run:
            print("  [dry-run] 快进推送将创建 commit（tree 与本地内容一致）")
            return 0
        # ---- 上传 blob → 在远端建 tree → 建 commit → 快进 ref ----
        # 注意：不能直接引用本地 tree SHA——GitHub 不认本地算出的 tree
        # （子模块/symlink 的 mode 处理不同），必须在远端用 create_tree 重建。
        print("  上传 blob……")
        entries: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        uploaded = 0
        for path, blob_sha in local_files.items():
            if remote_files.get(path) == blob_sha:
                continue
            raw = subprocess.run(["git", "cat-file", "blob", blob_sha], cwd=ROOT,
                                 capture_output=True, timeout=120).stdout
            new_blob = api.create_blob(raw)
            mode = _file_mode(path)
            entries.append({"path": path, "mode": mode, "type": "blob", "sha": new_blob})
            uploaded += 1
        print("  上传 %d 个 blob" % uploaded)

        for path in remote_files:
            if path not in local_files:
                entries.append({"path": path, "mode": "100644", "type": "blob",
                                "sha": "0000000000000000000000000000000000000000"})
                print("  删除 %s" % path)

        if not entries:
            print("  文件无差异——远端内容已是最新")
            return 0

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 363 页
        print("  在远端构建 tree……")
        new_tree = api.create_tree(remote_tree, entries)
        new_commit = api.create_commit(message or "sync", new_tree, [remote_sha],
                                       {"name": "WorkBuddy", "email": "workbuddy@local"})
        print("  新建 commit：%s" % new_commit[:10])
        api.update_ref(args.branch, new_commit, force=False)
        print("  [OK] 远端分支已快进 → %s" % new_commit[:10])
        _align_local_ref(args.branch, new_commit)
        print("=" * 78)
        return 0
    elif local_parent:
        print("  关系：本地父提交 %s ≠ 远端 %s" % (local_parent[:10], remote_sha[:10]))
        print("        → 走重建路径（以远端为基底叠加本地差异）")
    else:
        print("  关系：本地无父提交（首次提交）")

    if args.dry_run:
        print("  [dry-run] 到此为止")
        return 0

    # --- 重建：base = 远端 tree，把本地改动的文件叠加上去 ---
    changed = {p: s for p, s in local_files.items() if remote_files.get(p) != s}
    removed = [p for p in remote_files if p not in local_files]
    print("  需更新 %d 个 / 需删除 %d 个" % (len(changed), len(removed)))

    if not changed and not removed:
        print("  [OK] 文件内容与远端**完全一致**——远端已是最新，无需再推")
        _align_local_ref(args.branch, remote_sha)
        print("=" * 78)
        return 0

    entries: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for path, blob_sha in changed.items():
        # 用 git cat-file 取出字节内容（保持二进制安全）
        raw = subprocess.run(["git", "cat-file", "blob", blob_sha], cwd=ROOT,
                             capture_output=True, timeout=120).stdout
        new_blob = api.create_blob(raw)
        entries.append({"path": path, "mode": _file_mode(path), "type": "blob",
                        "sha": new_blob})
    for path in removed:
        entries.append({"path": path, "mode": "100644", "type": "blob",
                        "sha": "0000000000000000000000000000000000000000"})

    print("  上传 %d 个 blob" % len(changed))
    new_tree = api.create_tree(remote_tree, entries)
    author = {"name": "WorkBuddy", "email": "workbuddy@local"}
    new_commit = api.create_commit(message or "sync", new_tree, [remote_sha], author)
    print("  新建 commit：%s" % new_commit[:10])

    api.update_ref(args.branch, new_commit, force=False)

    print("  [OK] 远端分支已更新 → %s" % new_commit[:10])
    _align_local_ref(args.branch, new_commit)
    print("=" * 78)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""一键启动：环境自检 → 一键复算 → 打开五页。

给两类人用：
  - **团队自己**：日常开发，一条命令看全项目健康度；
  - **答辩现场**：陌生电脑、可能断网、可能没有高德 key——也要能开。

设计原则（工程师建议 #1/#9：静态兜底、不许红屏）：
  - 任何一步失败都**不阻断启动**，只打印警告并继续；
  - 高德 key 缺失 → 自动走离线降级（五页照常演）；
  - 路线表缺失 → 跳过路线段，主口径照常；
  - 永远不出现"启动脚本报错、演示机打不开"。

用法：
    python -m tools.launch              # 自检 + 复算 + 启动 Streamlit
    python -m tools.launch --check      # 只自检，不启动
    python -m tools.launch --no-recalc  # 不复算，直接启动（改前端时用）
    python -m tools.launch --port 8502  # 换端口
"""

def check_python() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    if sys.version_info < (3, 8):
        return False, "Python %s 过低，需要 3.8+" % ".".join(map(str, sys.version_info[:3]))
    return True, "Python %s" % ".".join(map(str, sys.version_info[:3]))


def check_deps() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    missing: List[str] = []
    versions: List[str] = []
    for mod, name in (("streamlit", "streamlit"), ("pandas", "pandas"),
                      ("numpy", "numpy"), ("pyarrow", "pyarrow"),
                      ("plotly", "plotly")):
        try:
            m = __import__(mod)
            versions.append("%s %s" % (name, getattr(m, "__version__", "?")))
        except ImportError:
            missing.append(name)
    if missing:
        return False, "缺依赖：%s（pip install -r requirements.txt）" % ", ".join(missing)
    return True, " · ".join(versions)


def check_data() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    required = ["wide_data.parquet", "baseline_carbon.parquet", "final_report.parquet",
                "od_band.parquet", "ledger.parquet"]
    missing = [f for f in required if not (PROCESSED / f).exists()]
    if missing:
        return False, "缺结果表 %d 个：%s（跑 python -m src.pipeline）" % (
            len(missing), ", ".join(missing))
    return True, "%d 个核心结果表就绪" % len(required)


def check_gate() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """闸门：passed 为真且 FAIL=0 才算健康（当前 26 项：8 目标 + 18 结构）。"""
    import json
    path = PROCESSED / "gate_report.json"
    if not path.exists():
        return False, "还没有 gate_report.json（跑 python -m src.pipeline）"
    try:
        report = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as exc:
        return False, "gate_report.json 读不了：%s" % exc
    counts = report.get("counts", {})
    passed = report.get("passed")
    detail = "PASS %s｜FAIL %s｜SKIP %s" % (
        counts.get("pass", "?"), counts.get("fail", "?"), counts.get("skip", "?"))
    if passed:
        return True, detail
    return False, detail + "（闸门未通过）"

def check_amap() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """高德 key 状态：齐了就提示能开在线地图，没齐就说会走离线兜底。"""
    files = {
        "Web服务key": CONFIG / "amap_key.txt",
        "JS API key": CONFIG / "amap_js_key.txt",
        "安全密钥": CONFIG / "amap_js_code.txt",
    }
    ready, missing = [], []
    for label, path in files.items():
        if not path.exists():
            missing.append(label)
            continue
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        if text and not text.startswith("请至高德"):
            ready.append(label)
        else:
            missing.append(label)
    if not missing:
        return True, "三把 key 齐备 → 在线底图 + 真路线可用"
    return True, "缺 %s → 自动走离线兜底（Plotly 底图 + haversine 路线）" % "、".join(missing)


def check_routes() -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    path = PROCESSED / "route_od.parquet"
    if not path.exists():
        return True, "路线表未生成 → 第 3 页动态路线区块自动跳过"
    try:
        import pandas as pd
        df = pd.read_parquet(path)
        if "source" in df.columns:
            online = int((df["source"] == "amap_rest").sum())
            return True, "路线表 %d 条（高德真路线 %d / 离线兜底 %d）" % (
                len(df), online, len(df) - online)
        return True, "路线表 %d 条" % len(df)
    except Exception as exc:
        return True, "路线表读不了（不影响启动）：%s" % exc


def _raw_ready() -> bool:
    """data/raw 是否有可读的附件（xlsx/xls/csv）。"""
    raw = ROOT / "data" / "raw"
    if not raw.exists():
        return False
    return any(p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in (".xlsx", ".xls", ".csv")
               for p in raw.iterdir())


def run_recalc(with_routes: bool = True, force: bool = False) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 368 页
    """一键复算。失败返回 False，但**不阻断启动**。

    data/raw 为空且已有结果表时默认跳过：否则 preprocess 会回退合成演示样本，
    把真实的 data/processed 结果表覆盖掉。要强制复算（例如故意演练演示模式），
    用 --force-recalc 或直接运行 python -m src.pipeline。
    """
    if not force and not _raw_ready() and (PROCESSED / "baseline_carbon.parquet").exists():
        return True, "data/raw 为空：跳过复算，保留现有结果表（避免被演示样本覆盖）"
    cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "src.pipeline"]
    if with_routes:
        cmd.append("--with-routes")
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
    tail = (proc.stdout or "").strip().splitlines()[-3:]
    summary = " / ".join(line.strip() for line in tail if line.strip())
    return proc.returncode == 0, summary or "退出码 %d" % proc.returncode


def start_streamlit(port: int, open_browser: bool = False) -> int:
    cmd = [
        sys.executable, "-m", "streamlit", "run", str(ROOT / "app" / "app.py"),
        "--server.port", str(port),
        "--server.headless", "true" if not open_browser else "false",
        "--browser.gatherUsageStats", "false",
    ]
    print("  启动命令：%s" % " ".join(cmd))
    print("  浏览器打开：http://localhost:%d" % port)
    print("  停止：Ctrl+C")
    print("-" * 78)
    try:
        return subprocess.call(cmd, cwd=ROOT)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n  已停止")
        return 0


def main(argv: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="一键启动：自检 + 复算 + 五页")
    parser.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="只自检，不复算不启动")
    parser.add_argument("--no-recalc", action="store_true", help="不复算，直接启动")
    parser.add_argument("--no-routes", action="store_true", help="复算时不带路线段")
    parser.add_argument("--force-recalc", action="store_true",
                        help="data/raw 为空时也强制复算（会回退演示数据，慎用）")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8501)
    parser.add_argument("--open", action="store_true", help="自动打开浏览器")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    _print_header("环境自检（任何一项不通过都不会阻断启动）")
    checks: List[Tuple[str, Any]] = [
        ("Python 版本", check_python()),
        ("依赖包", check_deps()),

        ("核心数据表", check_data()),
        ("对拍闸门", check_gate()),
        ("高德 key", check_amap()),
        ("路线表", check_routes()),
    ]
    hard_fail = 0
    for label, (ok, detail) in checks:
        icon = OK if ok else (WARN if label in ("高德 key", "路线表") else BAD)
        if not ok and label in ("高德 key", "路线表"):
            icon = WARN
        elif not ok:

        print("  %s %-14s %s" % (icon, label, detail))

    if hard_fail:

        for label, (ok, detail) in checks:

                print("     · %s → %s" % (label, detail))

    if not args.no_recalc:
        _print_header("一键复算（python -m src.pipeline）")
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        ok, summary = run_recalc(with_routes=not args.no_routes, force=args.force_recalc)
        print("  %s 耗时 %.1fs｜%s" % (OK if ok else WARN, time.perf_counter() - t0, summary))
        if not ok:
            print("  [!] 复算未通过，但仍然启动——演示用上一轮结果表")

    _print_header("启动应用")
    return start_streamlit(args.port, open_browser=args.open)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

纪律

* 不读 ``config/amap_*``，不调 AMap，不爬瓦片；

STEP_COLUMNS = [
    "od_key", "provider", "traffic_mode", "step_index", "distance_km",

def parse_point(text: str) -> tuple:
    """解析 "lng,lat"。"""
    parts = str(text).replace("，", ",").split(",")
    if len(parts) < 2:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("坐标格式应为 'lng,lat'，收到: %r" % text)
    try:
        return float(parts[0]), float(parts[1])
    except ValueError:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("坐标必须是数字，收到: %r" % text)


def build_steps_frame(result: osrm_client.RouteResult, od_key: str,
                      vehicle: str, load_t: float) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """RouteResult → steps.parquet 的 DataFrame（严格按 STEP_COLUMNS）。"""
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for step in result.steps:
        rows.append({
            "od_key": od_key,
            "provider": result.provider,
            "traffic_mode": result.traffic_mode,
            "step_index": int(step.step_index),
            "distance_km": round(float(step.distance_km), 6),
            "duration_min": round(float(step.duration_min), 6),
            "speed_kmh": round(float(step.speed_kmh), 4),
            "road_name": step.road_name,
            "road_ref": step.road_ref,
            "road_class": step.road_class,
            "road_class_source": step.road_class_source,
            "road_class_confidence": step.road_class_confidence,
            # OSRM 无过路费金额与信号灯数据 —— 留空，不编（docs/35 §9 第 4/5 条）
            "toll_flag": step.toll_flag,
            "toll_yuan": step.toll_yuan,
            "traffic_lights": step.traffic_lights,
            "carbon_kg": round(carbon_baseline.step_carbon_kg(step.distance_km, vehicle, load_t), 6),
            "factor_version": carbon_baseline.FACTOR_VERSION,
            "factor_mode": carbon_baseline.FACTOR_MODE,
            "vehicle": vehicle,
            "load_t": float(load_t),
            "geometry_geojson": step.geometry_geojson,
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 373 页
        })
    df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=STEP_COLUMNS)
    if not df.empty:
        df["toll_flag"] = df["toll_flag"].astype("Int64")
        df["toll_yuan"] = df["toll_yuan"].astype("Float64")
        df["traffic_lights"] = df["traffic_lights"].astype("Int64")
    return df


def build_summary(result: osrm_client.RouteResult, steps: pd.DataFrame,
                  od_key: str, vehicle: str, load_t: float,
                  origin_in: tuple, dest_in: tuple, coord_in: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """RouteResult → summary.json（docs/35 §5.2 字段）。"""
    step_km = float(steps["distance_km"].sum()) if not steps.empty else 0.0
    step_min = float(steps["duration_min"].sum()) if not steps.empty else 0.0
    carbon_kg = float(steps["carbon_kg"].sum()) if not steps.empty else 0.0
    road = summarize_road_class(result.steps)

    unknown_share = float(road["share_pct"].get("unknown", 0.0))
    return {
        "od_key": od_key,
        "distance_km": round(float(result.distance_km), 3),
        "duration_min": round(float(result.duration_min), 1),
        "carbon_kg": round(carbon_kg, 3),
        "carbon_t": round(carbon_kg / 1000.0, 6),
        "road_class_share": road["share_pct"],
        "road_class_count": road["count"],
        "road_class_distance_km": road["distance_km"],
        "road_class_unknown_pct": round(unknown_share, 3),
        "step_count": int(len(steps)),
        "provider": result.provider,
        "traffic_mode": result.traffic_mode,
        "factor_version": carbon_baseline.FACTOR_VERSION,
        "factor_mode": carbon_baseline.FACTOR_MODE,
        "vehicle": vehicle,
        "load_t": float(load_t),
        "ef_base_gkm": carbon_baseline.EF_BASE_GKM,
        "base_speed_kmh": carbon_baseline.BASE_SPEED_KMH,
        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "points": int(result.points),
        # ---- 坐标审计信息（复核用；记录基准，避免"基准不明"） ----
        "input_coord": coord_in,
        "input_origin": {"lng": origin_in[0], "lat": origin_in[1]},
        "input_dest": {"lng": dest_in[0], "lat": dest_in[1]},
        "wgs_origin": {"lng": round(result.origin_wgs[0], 6), "lat": round(result.origin_wgs[1], 6)},
        "wgs_dest": {"lng": round(result.dest_wgs[0], 6), "lat": round(result.dest_wgs[1], 6)},
        "snap_distance_m": {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in result.snap_distance_m.items()},
        # ---- 求和自检（docs/35 §8：求和偏差 < 0.5%） ----
        "sum_check": {
            "step_distance_km": round(step_km, 3),
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 374 页
            "route_distance_km": round(float(result.distance_km), 3),
            "distance_delta_pct": round(
                (step_km - float(result.distance_km)) / float(result.distance_km) * 100.0, 4)
            if result.distance_km else 0.0,
            "step_duration_min": round(step_min, 1),
            "route_duration_min": round(float(result.duration_min), 1),
            "duration_delta_pct": round(
                (step_min - float(result.duration_min)) / float(result.duration_min) * 100.0, 4)
            if result.duration_min else 0.0,
        },
        # ---- 非实时/固定因子声明（页面与文档必须照抄） ----
        "disclaimer": {
            "real_time": False,
            "fixed_factor": True,
            "road_class_is_proxy": True,
            "note": "非实时 · 固定因子（fixed_50）· 道路等级为 proxy，不代表实测碳账。",
        },
        "attribution": "路线几何与道路等级来自 OpenStreetMap（© OpenStreetMap contributors），"
                       "经 OSRM 公共服务计算。",
    }


def main(argv: List[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="python -m tools.route_baseline",

                        help="OD 标识，写进表里做键")
    parser.add_argument("--alternatives", default=2, type=int,
                        help="候选路线数（公共实例最多 3）")
    parser.add_argument("--steps-out", default=str(PROCESSED / STEPS_NAME),
                        help="逐 step 表输出路径")
    parser.add_argument("--summary-out", default=str(PROCESSED / SUMMARY_NAME),
                        help="summary 输出路径")
    parser.add_argument("--raw-out", default=str(RAW_DIR / "raw_osrm.json"),
                        help="原始响应落盘路径（不进 Git）")
    parser.add_argument("--base-url", default=osrm_client.OSRM_PUBLIC_URL,
                        help="OSRM 实例地址（正式版换本地实例）")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    # ---- 坐标转 WGS-84 ----

    if args.coord == "gcj02":

        dest_wgs = coord.gcj02_to_wgs84(*args.dest)
    else:
        origin_wgs = tuple(args.origin)
        dest_wgs = tuple(args.dest)

    osrm_client.save_raw(result.raw, Path(args.raw_out))
    print("原始响应   : %s（不进 Git）" % args.raw_out)

    steps = build_steps_frame(result, args.od_key, args.vehicle, args.load)
    summary = build_summary(result, steps, args.od_key, args.vehicle, args.load,
                            args.origin, args.dest, args.coord)

    steps_path = Path(args.steps_out)
    steps_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    steps.to_parquet(steps_path, index=False)

    summary_path = Path(args.summary_out)
    summary_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    summary_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),

    # ---- 控制台摘要（可直接抄进交接材料） ----
    print("")
    print("=== %s ===" % args.od_key)
    print("provider     : %s（traffic_mode=%s，非实时）" % (summary["provider"], summary["traffic_mode"]))
    print("距离         : %.2f km（step 求和 %.2f km，偏差 %+.3f%%）"
          % (summary["distance_km"], summary["sum_check"]["step_distance_km"],
             summary["sum_check"]["distance_delta_pct"]))
    print("时长         : %.1f min（step 求和 %.1f min，偏差 %+.3f%%）"
          % (summary["duration_min"], summary["sum_check"]["step_duration_min"],
             summary["sum_check"]["duration_delta_pct"]))
    print("碳排         : %.1f kg（%.4f t，%s / %s）"
          % (summary["carbon_kg"], summary["carbon_t"],
             summary["factor_version"], summary["factor_mode"]))
    print("step 数      : %d" % summary["step_count"])
    print("端点吸附     : 起点 %.1f m / 终点 %.1f m（容差 %.0f m）"
          % (summary["snap_distance_m"]["origin"], summary["snap_distance_m"]["dest"],

             osrm_client.SNAP_TOLERANCE_M))
    print("道路等级占比 :")
    for klass, pct in summary["road_class_share"].items():
        print("    %-10s %6.2f%%  (%d steps)"
              % (klass, pct, summary["road_class_count"].get(klass, 0)))

    print("")
    print("输出         : %s" % steps_path)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""候选路线预取：310 个热点 OD × 4 条高德策略 → route_candidates.parquet。

定位（docs/28 §8 下一步）：让第 6 页切换策略时不只换几何，还能换同一策略的
碳账/成本/推荐车型。本脚本只做**路网 I/O**，不写任何业务口径：

    高德 REST（strategy 0/4/1/6）→ route_candidates.parquet
        ↓
    src/optimizer.route_candidate_select()（L2 展示口径）
        ↓
    route_candidate_carbon.parquet
        ↓
    第 6 页只读；浏览器不算碳

四条策略（与前端 pills 的近似映射）：

    0 综合  ↔ JS policy 0
    4 避堵  ↔ JS policy 4
    1 避费  ↔ JS policy 1
    6 不走高速 ↔ JS policy 6

REST 的 strategy 与 JS 的 policy 使用同一套 0/1/4/6 语义；两边分别请求，
距离/时长可能有几个百分点的差异；页面同时展示“实时几何”和“同策略预计算
碳账”，并标注口径。

用法：
    python -m tools.route_candidates --limit 5     # 小样本
    python -m tools.route_candidates                # 全量，可断点续跑
    python -m tools.route_candidates --refresh      # 忽略缓存
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence

import pandas as pd

from tools import route_fetch as rf

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROCESSED = ROOT / "data" / "processed"
BASE_PATH = PROCESSED / "route_od.parquet"
CAND_PATH = PROCESSED / "route_candidates.parquet"

STRATEGIES = [(0, "综合"), (4, "躲避拥堵"), (1, "避开收费"), (6, "不走高速")]

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 378 页
CAND_COLUMNS = [
    "od_key", "od_site", "od_pair", "origin", "dest",
    "o_lng", "o_lat", "d_lng", "d_lat",
    "strategy", "strategy_name",
    "distance_km", "duration_min", "toll_yuan", "traffic_lights",
    "source", "flag_route_est", "fetched_at",
]


def load_base(limit: Optional[int] = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if not BASE_PATH.exists():
        return pd.DataFrame()
    df = pd.read_parquet(BASE_PATH)
    if df.empty:
        return df
    need = ["od_key", "origin", "dest", "o_lng", "o_lat", "d_lng", "d_lat"]
    if any(col not in df.columns for col in need):
        return pd.DataFrame()
    if limit:
        df = df.head(int(limit)).copy()
    return df


def _load_cache(refresh: bool) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    if refresh or not CAND_PATH.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        old = pd.read_parquet(CAND_PATH)
    except Exception:
        return {}
    cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for _, row in old.iterrows():
        try:
            key = "%s|%d" % (str(row["od_key"]), int(row["strategy"]))
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        cache[key] = row.to_dict()
    return cache


def _save(cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if not cache:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=CAND_COLUMNS)
    out = pd.DataFrame(list(cache.values()))
    for col in CAND_COLUMNS:
        if col not in out.columns:
            out[col] = None
    out = out[CAND_COLUMNS].sort_values(["od_key", "strategy"]).reset_index(drop=True)
    PROCESSED.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.to_parquet(CAND_PATH, index=False)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 379 页
    return out


def _record(row: Any, strategy: int, name: str,
            result: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not result:
        return None
    try:
        o_lng, o_lat = float(row["o_lng"]), float(row["o_lat"])
        d_lng, d_lat = float(row["d_lng"]), float(row["d_lat"])
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return {
        "od_key": str(row["od_key"]),
        "od_site": str(row.get("od_site") or row["od_key"]),
        "od_pair": str(row.get("od_pair") or (str(row["origin"]) + " → " + str(row["dest"]))),
        "origin": str(row["origin"]),
        "dest": str(row["dest"]),
        "o_lng": o_lng, "o_lat": o_lat,
        "d_lng": d_lng, "d_lat": d_lat,
        "strategy": int(strategy), "strategy_name": name,
        "distance_km": result.get("distance_km"),
        "duration_min": result.get("duration_min"),
        "toll_yuan": result.get("toll_yuan"),
        "traffic_lights": result.get("traffic_lights"),
        "source": result.get("source") or "amap_rest",
        "flag_route_est": int(result.get("flag_route_est") or 0),
        "fetched_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
    }


def build(limit: Optional[int] = None, refresh: bool = False,
          workers: int = 3) -> pd.DataFrame:
    base = load_base(limit)
    if base.empty:
        print("  [C1] route_od.parquet 缺失或没有坐标 — 跳过候选路线预取")
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=CAND_COLUMNS)

    cache = _load_cache(refresh)
    todo: List[Any] = []
    for _, row in base.iterrows():
        for strategy, _name in STRATEGIES:
            key = "%s|%d" % (str(row["od_key"]), strategy)
            if key not in cache:
                todo.append((row, strategy))

    print("  [C1] 候选路线：待抓 %d 条 / 缓存命中 %d 条（%d 个 OD × %d 策略，%d 并发）"
          % (len(todo), len(cache), len(base), len(STRATEGIES), max(1, workers)))
    if todo and not rf.key_available():
        print("       [!] 未检测到 Web 服务 key，候选路线需要真实 key 才能生成")
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 380 页
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=CAND_COLUMNS)

    def work(item: Any):
        row, strategy = item
        name = dict(STRATEGIES)[strategy]
        try:
            result = rf.fetch_driving(
                (float(row["o_lng"]), float(row["o_lat"])),
                (float(row["d_lng"]), float(row["d_lat"])),
                strategy=strategy, with_polyline=False,
            )
        except Exception:
            result = None
        return row, strategy, _record(row, strategy, name, result)

    done = 0
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max(1, int(workers))) as pool:
        for row, strategy, record in pool.map(work, todo):
            if record is None and strategy == 0:
                # 综合策略失败时，至少用 route_od 的既有指标兜底，保证默认策略有账可看
                record = {
                    "od_key": str(row["od_key"]),
                    "od_site": str(row.get("od_site") or row["od_key"]),
                    "od_pair": str(row.get("od_pair") or ""),
                    "origin": str(row["origin"]), "dest": str(row["dest"]),
                    "o_lng": float(row["o_lng"]), "o_lat": float(row["o_lat"]),
                    "d_lng": float(row["d_lng"]), "d_lat": float(row["d_lat"]),
                    "strategy": 0, "strategy_name": "综合",
                    "distance_km": row.get("distance_km"),
                    "duration_min": row.get("duration_min"),
                    "toll_yuan": row.get("toll_yuan"),
                    "traffic_lights": row.get("traffic_lights"),
                    "source": "route_od_fallback",
                    "flag_route_est": int(row.get("flag_route_est") or 1),
                    "fetched_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                }
            if record is not None:
                cache["%s|%d" % (str(row["od_key"]), strategy)] = record
            done += 1
            if done % 20 == 0 or done == len(todo):
                _save(cache)
                print("       %d/%d（候选 %d 条）" % (done, len(todo), len(cache)))

    out = _save(cache)
    n_ok = int((out["source"] == "amap_rest").sum()) if not out.empty else 0
    print("  [C1] 候选路线落盘：%s（%d 行，真路线 %d）" % (CAND_PATH.name, len(out), n_ok))
    return out

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""路线预取层：网点级 OD → 高德驾车路线（T7 前置件）。

定位（docs/23 §5.1）：**网络 I/O 是工具行为，不是业务口径**，所以放 tools/，不进 src/。
它只做一件事——把"这条 OD 走哪条路、多远、多久、过路费多少、折线怎么画"预取成
``data/processed/route_od.parquet``，供引擎层（O8）和前端（只读）消费。

四条纪律（与 docs/23 §1.2 一致）：
    1. 失败不抛异常——单 OD 失败 → offline_route() 兜底并打 flag_route_est；
    2. key 只在服务端读，永不进 git、永不进前端；
    3. 幂等——已缓存的 od_key 直接跳过，第二次运行零 API 调用；
    4. 坐标系单一——高德返回即 GCJ-02，直上高德底图；本地坐标仅离线兜底并标注近似。

用法：
    python -m tools.route_fetch                     # 全量（310 条热点 OD）
    python -m tools.route_fetch --limit 20          # 先小样本试跑
    python -m tools.route_fetch --refresh           # 忽略缓存重抓
    python -m tools.route_fetch --offline           # 强制离线兜底（无 key 演练用）

离线兜底口径：haversine 直线距离 × 1.28 绕行系数，与 docs/20 §2「距离缺失→绕行系数 1.28」
完全同口径，保证"有网/无网"两套数字可比。
"""

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 383 页

# 离线兜底参数（与 docs/20 §2 同口径）
DETOUR_FACTOR = 1.28
OFFLINE_SPEED_KMH = 50.0

# 路线表契约（前端与引擎层共用，docs/24 §4）
ROUTE_COLUMNS: List[str] = [
    "od_key", "od_site", "od_pair", "origin", "dest",

_HTTP_TIMEOUT = 10.0
_QPS = 3.0
_MAX_RETRY = 3

# 高德对**并发**请求限流：实测同一批 24 个地址，
#   串行 24/24 成功（13.5s）｜并发 6 无节流 10/24｜并发 6 + QPS4 15/20
#   **并发 2 + QPS 2.0 → 20/20 全成功**（10.4s）
# 所以并发必须配一个**全局令牌桶**——不是每线程一个，是所有线程共用一个。
# QPS 取 2.0：官方个人 key 基础服务 QPS 通常 3~5，但并发会额外触发限流，
# 留足余量比抢那几秒划算——地理编码失败会导致该网点退化成城市级近似坐标。
_GLOBAL_QPS = 2.0
_RATE_WORKERS = 2
_rate_lock = threading.Lock()
_last_call = [0.0]


def _throttle() -> None:
    """全局令牌桶：保证所有线程合起来不超过 _GLOBAL_QPS 次/秒。"""
    interval = 1.0 / _GLOBAL_QPS
    with _rate_lock:
        now = time.monotonic()
        wait = interval - (now - _last_call[0])
        if wait > 0:
            time.sleep(wait)
            now = time.monotonic()
        _last_call[0] = now

def load_key() -> str:
    """env → config/amap_key.txt → 占位符。占位符 = 演示回退模式，不报错。"""
    env = os.environ.get("AMAP_WEB_SERVICE_KEY", "").strip()
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 384 页
    if env and not env.startswith(PLACEHOLDER):
        return env
    if KEY_PATH.exists():
        text = KEY_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        if text and not text.startswith(PLACEHOLDER):
            return text
    return PLACEHOLDER


def key_available() -> bool:
    return load_key() != PLACEHOLDER


def _request(url: str, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """一次带重试的 GET。高德限流/网络抖动不抛异常，失败返回 None。

    **只有"临时性"错误才重试**（网络异常、5xx、10003 配额超限、10004 访问过频）。
    "确定性"错误（key 无效、参数错、引擎解析不了）**立刻放弃**——
    实测 246 个网点如果每个都重试 3 次 + sleep，流程会卡死十几分钟。
    """
    key = load_key()
    if key == PLACEHOLDER:
        return None
    query = dict(params)
    query["key"] = key
    query["output"] = "json"

    # 这些错误重试也没用：key 不对/没权限/参数错/引擎解析不了
    _FATAL_CODES = {"10001", "10002", "10005", "10006", "10007", "10009",
                    "10020", "10021", "30001", "30002", "30003"}
    # 这些是临时性的，值得重试
    _RETRY_CODES = {"10003", "10004"}

    delay = 0.6
    for attempt in range(_MAX_RETRY):
        try:
            import requests
            _throttle()
            resp = requests.get(url, params=query, timeout=_HTTP_TIMEOUT)
            if resp.status_code != 200:
                if resp.status_code >= 500:
                    time.sleep(delay)
                    delay *= 2
                    continue
                return None
            payload = resp.json()
            if payload.get("status") == "1":
                return payload
            code = str(payload.get("infocode", ""))
            if code in _FATAL_CODES:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 385 页
                return None
            if code not in _RETRY_CODES:
                return None
            time.sleep(delay)
            delay *= 2
        except Exception:
            time.sleep(delay)
            delay *= 2
    return None


def _haversine_km(lng1: float, lat1: float, lng2: float, lat2: float) -> float:
    """大圆距离（km）。"""
    radius = 6371.0088
    p1, p2 = math.radians(lat1), math.radians(lat2)
    dp = math.radians(lat2 - lat1)
    dl = math.radians(lng2 - lng1)
    a = math.sin(dp / 2) ** 2 + math.cos(p1) * math.cos(p2) * math.sin(dl / 2) ** 2
    return 2 * radius * math.asin(math.sqrt(a))


def offline_route(origin: Tuple[float, float], dest: Tuple[float, float]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """无网兜底：haversine × 1.28 绕行系数，时长按 50 km/h 反推。

    与 docs/20 §2「距离缺失→绕行系数 1.28」完全同口径，保证有网/无网数字可比。

    **坐标缺失时返回 None**——调用方必须回退到宽表实测里程。
    不能拿 (0,0) 当坐标算 haversine：那会算出上万公里的荒谬距离，
    比"没有路线"糟糕得多。宁可标为缺失，也不给错数字。
    """
    o_lng, o_lat = float(origin[0]), float(origin[1])
    d_lng, d_lat = float(dest[0]), float(dest[1])
    if (o_lng, o_lat) == (0.0, 0.0) or (d_lng, d_lat) == (0.0, 0.0):
        return {}          # 坐标缺失 → 调用方回退
    straight = _haversine_km(o_lng, o_lat, d_lng, d_lat)
    distance = round(straight * DETOUR_FACTOR, 3)
    duration = round(distance / OFFLINE_SPEED_KMH * 60.0, 2) if distance > 0 else 0.0
    return {
        "distance_km": distance,
        "duration_min": duration,
        "toll_yuan": 0.0,
        "traffic_lights": None,
        "strategy": None,
        "polyline": "",
        "source": "offline_haversine",
        "flag_route_est": 1,
    }


def geocode(name: str, city_hint: str = "") -> Optional[Tuple[float, float]]:
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 386 页
    """地名 → GCJ-02 坐标。失败返回 None（调用方回退离线坐标），不抛异常。

    **不传 city 参数**：实测高德对"苏州昆山集货分拣中心"这类
    「城市 + 区县 + 业务后缀」的网点名，带 city 参数反而报 ENGINE_RESPONSE_DATA_ERROR(30001)，
    不带则正常返回。网点名前两字已经是城市名，city 参数是冗余的。

    **必须按城市校验候选**（关键）：高德对虚构网点名会"字面匹配"到别的城市——
    实测"北京光华散货分拣中心"第 1 条候选是**惠州市博罗县分拣中心**（"分拣中心"字面命中），
    若直接取第 1 条，北京网点会被安到广东坐标上，路线算出 2100 km，闸门 S1 直接报红。
    所以：只接受 `city`/`province` 字段与网点名所属城市一致的候选；
    都不一致就判为不可信（返回 None，由调用方回退城市级近似坐标——
    **宁可粗一点，也不给错坐标**）。

    只做一次，不缓存——缓存由 route_geo.parquet 在批处理层做。
    """
    if not name:
        return None
    payload = _request(GEO_URL, {"address": name})
    if not payload:
        return None
    pois = payload.get("geocodes") or []
    if not pois:
        return None

    want_city = city_hint or _site_to_city(name, _short_city_names(_city_coordinate_table().keys()))
    fallback: Optional[Tuple[float, float]] = None
    for item in pois:
        location = str(item.get("location", ""))
        if "," not in location:
            continue
        lng_text, lat_text = location.split(",", 1)
        try:
            coord = (float(lng_text), float(lat_text))
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if coord[0] == 0.0 and coord[1] == 0.0:
            continue
        got_city = str(item.get("city", "") or "")
        got_province = str(item.get("province", "") or "")
        # 城市一致 → 可信；省级一致（市不同）也算可信（北京乡镇常被归到廊坊）
        if want_city and (got_city.startswith(want_city) or got_province.startswith(want_city)):
            return coord
        if fallback is None:
            fallback = coord
    # 候选城市全不匹配：判为不可信（宁可回退也不给错坐标）
    return None if want_city else fallback


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 驾车路径规划
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 387 页
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def geocode_batch(names: List[str], city_hint_of: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
                  workers: int = _RATE_WORKERS) -> Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]:
    """批量地理编码：**并发单条请求**（每个网点一个请求，带 city 参数）。

    为什么不用高德的 batch=true：
        batch 模式返回的 geocodes 里**没有原始地址**，只有 city + formatted_address，
        只能靠"网点名前两字 == 城市名"猜回去——猜错就把**别人的坐标**安到这个网点上，
        路线全错，比慢糟糕得多。宁可多花几秒，不给错坐标。

    并发 6 路（高德个人 key 的 QPS 完全够）：246 个网点从 ~4 分钟压到 ~30 秒。
    返回 {网点名: (lng, lat)}，失败的条目不出现（调用方回退城市级近似坐标）。
    """
    if not names:
        return {}
    city_hint_of = city_hint_of or {}
    result: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]] = {}
    lock = threading.Lock()
    done = [0]

    def work(site: str) -> None:
        coord = geocode(site, city_hint_of.get(site, ""))
        with lock:
            done[0] += 1
            if coord:
                result[site] = coord
            if done[0] % 40 == 0 or done[0] == len(names):
                print("       %d/%d（成功 %d）" % (done[0], len(names), len(result)))

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max(1, workers)) as pool:
        list(pool.map(work, names))
    return result


def fetch_driving(origin: Tuple[float, float], dest: Tuple[float, float],
                  strategy: int = 32,
                  with_polyline: bool = True) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """单 OD 驾车规划 /v3/direction/driving。

    strategy：32=默认（综合）、33=躲避拥堵、34=避开收费、35=不走高速。
    with_polyline=False 时不解析/抽稀几何，供候选路线批量预取省 CPU。
    失败/超时/配额耗尽 → None（调用方走 offline_route）。
    """
    payload = _request(DRIVING_URL, {
        "origin": "%.6f,%.6f" % (origin[0], origin[1]),
        "destination": "%.6f,%.6f" % (dest[0], dest[1]),
        "strategy": strategy,
        "extensions": "base",
    })
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 388 页
    if not payload:
        return None
    routes = payload.get("route") or {}
    paths = routes.get("paths") or []
    if not paths:
        return None
    path = paths[0]
    try:
        distance_km = round(float(path.get("distance", 0)) / 1000.0, 3)
        duration_min = round(float(path.get("duration", 0)) / 60.0, 2)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None

    except (TypeError, ValueError):

    lights = path.get("traffic_lights")
    try:
        traffic_lights = int(lights) if lights not in (None, "") else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        traffic_lights = None
    # 高德 /v3/direction/driving 的路径几何在 path.steps[*].polyline，
    # path 顶层通常没有 polyline；之前只读顶层导致 295 条“真路线”全是空线。
    polyline = ""
    if with_polyline:
        polyline = str(path.get("polyline", "") or "")
        if not polyline:
            steps = path.get("steps") or []
            polyline = ";".join(
                str(step.get("polyline", "") or "")
                for step in steps
                if step.get("polyline")
            )
        polyline = _simplify_polyline(polyline, max_points=1500)
    return {
        "distance_km": distance_km,
        "duration_min": duration_min,
        "toll_yuan": toll_yuan,
        "traffic_lights": traffic_lights,
        "strategy": strategy,
        "polyline": polyline,
        "source": "amap_rest",
        "flag_route_est": 0,
    }


def _simplify_polyline(polyline: str, max_points: int = 1500) -> str:
    """抽稀高德 polyline（RDP，保形），保留首尾点。

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 389 页
    为什么不再按固定步长抽样：旧实现 chunks[::step] 在 1,375 km 路线上
    只留 400 个点（约 3.4 km 一个点），城市/山区路段会直接跨过道路拐弯，
    渲染出来就是“抽象折线”。现在改用 Douglas–Peucker：
    先按 20 m 容差保形抽稀，点仍太多再逐步放大容差，直到不超过 max_points。
    这样同样点数下能保留弯道与互通立交的走向。

    注意：在线页面用 AMap.Driving 实时几何（约 12,000 点），本函数只服务
    route_od.parquet 的离线缓存；缓存几何再密也不可能替代实时路网。
    """
    chunks = [c for c in str(polyline or "").split(";") if c]
    if len(chunks) <= max_points:
        return ";".join(chunks)
    points = _polyline_to_points(polyline)
    if len(points) <= max_points:
        return polyline

    # 以路线中心纬度做局部等距投影，RDP 的距离单位用米。
    mean_lat = sum(p[1] for p in points) / len(points)
    kx = 111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(mean_lat))
    ky = 110540.0
    projected = [(p[0] * kx, p[1] * ky) for p in points]

    tolerance = 20.0
    simplified = _rdp(projected, tolerance)
    while len(simplified) > max_points and tolerance < 5000.0:
        tolerance *= 1.8
        simplified = _rdp(projected, tolerance)
    if len(simplified) > max_points:
        step = max(1, len(simplified) // max_points)
        simplified = simplified[::step]
        if simplified[-1] != projected[-1]:
            simplified.append(projected[-1])
    out = []
    for x, y in simplified:
        out.append("%.6f,%.6f" % (x / kx, y / ky))
    return ";".join(out)


def _rdp(points: List[Tuple[float, float]], tolerance: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
    """Douglas–Peucker 折线抽稀（迭代版，避免长路线递归过深）。"""
    n = len(points)
    if n <= 2:
        return list(points)
    keep = [False] * n
    keep[0] = keep[-1] = True
    stack = [(0, n - 1)]
    while stack:
        start, end = stack.pop()
        if end <= start + 1:
            continue
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 390 页
        ax, ay = points[start]
        bx, by = points[end]
        dx, dy = bx - ax, by - ay
        denom = dx * dx + dy * dy
        max_dist = -1.0
        max_idx = -1
        for i in range(start + 1, end):
            px, py = points[i]
            if denom <= 1e-12:
                dist = math.hypot(px - ax, py - ay)
            else:
                t = ((px - ax) * dx + (py - ay) * dy) / denom
                t = max(0.0, min(1.0, t))
                dist = math.hypot(px - (ax + t * dx), py - (ay + t * dy))
            if dist > max_dist:
                max_dist = dist
                max_idx = i
        if max_dist > tolerance and max_idx > start:
            keep[max_idx] = True
            stack.append((start, max_idx))
            stack.append((max_idx, end))
    return [p for i, p in enumerate(points) if keep[i]]


def _polyline_to_points(polyline: str) -> List[List[float]]:
    """高德 polyline（'lng,lat;lng,lat'）→ [[lng, lat], ...]。"""
    if not polyline:
        return []
    points: List[List[float]] = []
    for chunk in str(polyline).split(";"):
        if "," not in chunk:
            continue
        lng_text, lat_text = chunk.split(",", 1)
        try:
            points.append([float(lng_text), float(lat_text)])
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return points

    band = pd.read_parquet(OD_BAND_PATH)

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 391 页

            "od_pair": str(r["od_pair"]),
            "origin": sites[0],
            "dest": sites[1],
            "kg_t": float(r.get("kg_t") or 0.0),
        })
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    if out.empty:
        return out
    out = out.sort_values("kg_t", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)
    if limit:
        out = out.head(int(limit)).copy()
    return out


def resolve_coordinates(od_df: pd.DataFrame, city_hint_of: Dict[str, str],
                        offline: bool = False) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """补全网点级 GCJ-02 坐标。

    优先级：① 地理编码缓存 route_geo.parquet → ② 高德地理编码 → ③ 城市级近似（离线兜底）。
    城市级近似来自宽表 origin_lat/origin_lon（docs/24 §8 标注为"离线近似坐标"）。
    """
    sites = sorted({s for pair in od_df["origin"].tolist() + od_df["dest"].tolist()
                    for s in [pair]})
    cache = _load_geo_cache()
    city_coords = _load_site_coords()

    todo = [s for s in sites if s not in cache]
    if todo and not offline and key_available():
        print("  [R2] 地理编码 %d 个网点（高德 /v3/geocode/geo，批量 10 个/次）…" % len(todo))
        resolved = geocode_batch(todo, city_hint_of)
        cache.update(resolved)
        print("       成功 %d / %d" % (len(resolved), len(todo)))
        _save_geo_cache(cache)
    elif todo and offline:
        print("  [R2] 离线模式：跳过地理编码，%d 个网点用城市级近似坐标" % len(todo))

碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 392 页
    out = od_df.copy()
    o_lng, o_lat, d_lng, d_lat = [], [], [], []
    o_precise, d_precise = [], []
    for _, r in out.iterrows():
        for site, lng_c, lat_c, prec_c in (
            (str(r["origin"]), o_lng, o_lat, o_precise),
            (str(r["dest"]), d_lng, d_lat, d_precise),
        ):
            coord = cache.get(site)
            if coord:
                lng_c.append(coord[0])
                lat_c.append(coord[1])
                prec_c.append("exact")
                continue
            approx = city_coords.get(site)
            if approx:
                lng_c.append(approx[0])
                lat_c.append(approx[1])
                prec_c.append("city_approx")
            else:
                lng_c.append(0.0)
                lat_c.append(0.0)
                prec_c.append("missing")
    out["o_lng"], out["o_lat"] = o_lng, o_lat
    out["d_lng"], out["d_lat"] = d_lng, d_lat
    out["o_precise"], out["d_precise"] = o_precise, d_precise
    return out


def _load_geo_cache() -> Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]:
    if not GEO_CACHE_PATH.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        df = pd.read_parquet(GEO_CACHE_PATH)
    except Exception:
        return {}
    cache: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]] = {}
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        try:
            cache[str(r["site"])] = (float(r["lng"]), float(r["lat"]))
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
    return cache


def _save_geo_cache(cache: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]) -> None:
    if not cache:
        return
    df = pd.DataFrame(
        [{"site": k, "lng": v[0], "lat": v[1]} for k, v in sorted(cache.items())]

    )
    PROCESSED.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    df.to_parquet(GEO_CACHE_PATH, index=False)


_city_table_cache: Optional[Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]] = None


def _city_coordinate_table() -> Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]:
    """城市名 → 坐标（离线兜底用）。来源：宽表 origin/dest + 城市级近似坐标。

    带进程内缓存——这个函数被频繁调用（每次城市归属判断都要用），
    每次重读 parquet 会把 310 条路线的处理拖慢几十秒。
    """
    global _city_table_cache
    if _city_table_cache is not None:
        return _city_table_cache
    wide_path = PROCESSED / "wide_data.parquet"
    if not wide_path.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        wide = pd.read_parquet(wide_path)
    except Exception:
        return {}
    table: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]] = {}
    pairs = (
        ("origin", "origin_lat", "origin_lon"),
        ("dest", "dest_lat", "dest_lon"),
    )
    for name_col, lat_col, lon_col in pairs:
        if name_col not in wide.columns:
            continue
        for name, lat, lon in zip(wide[name_col], wide.get(lat_col, []),
                                  wide.get(lon_col, [])):
            try:
                if name is None or str(name) in ("", "nan", "None"):
                    continue
                lat_v, lon_v = float(lat), float(lon)
                # NaN 或 0 都不是有效坐标（宽表里有缺失填充值）
                if math.isnan(lat_v) or math.isnan(lon_v):
                    continue
                if lat_v == 0.0 and lon_v == 0.0:
                    continue
                # 注意：**不能**用 setdefault —— 城市首行可能是 NaN 坐标，
                # setdefault 会占位后再也不更新，导致该城市永远"缺失"。
                # 同一城市的坐标本应唯一，重复出现时保留首个有效值即可。
                table.setdefault(str(name), (lon_v, lat_v))
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                continue
    _city_table_cache = table
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 394 页
    return table


def _short_city_names(cities: Iterable[str]) -> List[str]:
    """城市短名列表：用于网点名前缀匹配。

    只剥离**真正的行政后缀**（"地区/特别行政区/自治州"），**不能剥"市/州"**——
    "苏州""广州""兰州""杭州"里的"州/广"都是城市名的一部分，剥掉会退化成"苏""广"，
    前缀匹配随即失效。剥不动的名字保持原样即可。

    长名优先（"和田地区" 必须在 "和田" 之前匹配）。
    """
    suffixes = ("特别行政区", "自治州", "地区")
    shorts: List[str] = []
    for city in cities:
        name = str(city)
        for suffix in suffixes:
            if name.endswith(suffix) and len(name) > len(suffix):
                name = name[: -len(suffix)]
                break
        if name and name not in shorts:
            shorts.append(name)
    shorts.sort(key=len, reverse=True)
    return shorts


def _site_to_city(site: str, city_names: List[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    """网点名 → 所属城市（按前缀匹配，如"哈尔滨分拣中心" → "哈尔滨"）。"""
    if not site:
        return None
    for city in city_names:
        if site.startswith(city):
            return city
    return None


def _load_site_coords() -> Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]:
    """网点名 → 城市级近似坐标（离线兜底用）。

    做法：**按网点名前缀解析城市**，再查城市坐标表。
    比"按宽表行配对 dep_site→origin"稳健——同一网点名在不同运单里可能被挂到不同城市，
    按行配对会随机取到错误坐标；前缀解析则是确定性的。
    """
    wide_path = PROCESSED / "wide_data.parquet"
    if not wide_path.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        wide = pd.read_parquet(wide_path)
    except Exception:
        return {}
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 395 页
    if "dep_site" not in wide.columns or "arr_site" not in wide.columns:
        return {}

    city_table = _city_coordinate_table()
    city_names = _short_city_names(city_table.keys())

    coords: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]] = {}
    sites = set(wide["dep_site"].astype(str)) | set(wide["arr_site"].astype(str))
    for site in sites:
        if site in ("", "nan", "None"):
            continue
        city = _site_to_city(site, city_names)
        if city and city in city_table:
            coords[site] = city_table[city]
    return coords


def _city_hint_map(od_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """网点名 → 所属城市（用于地理编码的 city 参数，提高命中率）。

    与 _load_site_coords 同源：**按网点名前缀解析城市**，不按宽表行配对。
    """
    city_table = _city_coordinate_table()
    city_names = _short_city_names(city_table.keys())
    hints: Dict[str, str] = {}
    sites = set(od_df["origin"].astype(str)) | set(od_df["dest"].astype(str))
    for site in sites:
        city = _site_to_city(site, city_names)
        if city:
            hints[site] = city
    return hints

# 批量预取
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _base_distances() -> pd.Series:
    """OD（od_site）→ 历史实测里程中位（km）。

    来自 baseline_carbon.parquet 的 dist_km —— 运单回传的真实里程，
    是判断"高德路线是否匹配错了城市"的锚：路线距离超过实测里程 6 倍就是错配。
    """
    path = PROCESSED / "baseline_carbon.parquet"
    if not path.exists():
        return pd.Series(dtype=float)
    try:
        seg = pd.read_parquet(path, columns=["od_site", "dist_km"])
    except Exception:
        return pd.Series(dtype=float)
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 396 页
    if seg.empty or "od_site" not in seg.columns:
        return pd.Series(dtype=float)
    dist = pd.to_numeric(seg["dist_km"], errors="coerce")
    median = dist.groupby(seg["od_site"].astype(str)).median()
    # 必须显式命名：Series 自带名字（dist_km），merge 后列名会跟着变，
    # 后面 row.get("base_km") 就取不到了
    return median.rename("base_km")


def _same_city(row: Any) -> bool:
    """判断这条 OD 的起点和终点是否属于同一城市（按网点名前缀解析）。"""
    origin = str(row.get("origin", ""))
    dest = str(row.get("dest", ""))
    if not origin or not dest:
        return False
    city_names = _short_city_names(_city_coordinate_table().keys())
    o_city = _site_to_city(origin, city_names)
    d_city = _site_to_city(dest, city_names)
    return bool(o_city) and o_city == d_city


def _implausible(result: Dict[str, Any], row: Any) -> bool:
    """路线是否明显错配（True = 不可信，应作废）。

    两条判据，任一命中即判为错配：
      ① **同城 + 距离过大**：同城市的 OD，路线不可能超过 300 km；
      ② **倍数异常**：路线距离 > 实测里程 × 6（实测里程取该 OD 的历史中位）。
         实测里程是运单回传的真实里程，路线距离是其 6 倍以上只能是匹配错了城市。

    判据 ② 是主力：它不依赖"网点名以城市开头"这个假设——
    实测"嘉兴分拣中心 → 京东南湖城南银河营业部"（终点名不含城市），
    同城判断失效但倍数判断照样能抓住（路线 1222 km vs 实测 23 km）。
    """
    distance = float(result.get("distance_km") or 0.0)
    if distance <= 0:
        return False
    if _same_city(row) and distance > 300.0:
        return True
    base_km = row.get("base_km")
    if base_km is None:
        return False
    try:
        base = float(base_km)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False
    if base <= 0:
        return False
    return distance > base * 6.0


碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 397 页
def batch_fetch(od_df: pd.DataFrame, qps: float = _QPS,
                refresh: bool = False, offline: bool = False,
                cache_path: Path = ROUTE_PATH, workers: int = _RATE_WORKERS) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """批量算路 + 并发限流 + 退避重试 + parquet 缓存。

    - 已有缓存的 od_key 直接跳过（第二次运行零 API 调用）；
    - 任何单点失败只跳过该 OD，不影响整批（走 offline_route 兜底）；
    - 并发受全局令牌桶约束（workers=2, QPS=2.0，实测 100% 成功率）；
    - 不抛异常：函数一定返回一张表。
    """
    if od_df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=ROUTE_COLUMNS)

    existing: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    if cache_path.exists() and not refresh:
        try:
            cached = pd.read_parquet(cache_path)
            for _, r in cached.iterrows():
                existing[str(r["od_key"])] = r.to_dict()
        except Exception:
            existing = {}

    todo = [row for _, row in od_df.iterrows() if str(row["od_key"]) not in existing]
    print("  [R2] 批量算路：待抓 %d 条 / 缓存命中 %d 条"
          % (len(todo), len(existing)))
    if todo and not offline and not key_available():
        print("       [!] 未检测到可用 Web 服务 key → 全部走离线兜底（haversine×%.2f）"
              % DETOUR_FACTOR)
        offline = True

    interval = 1.0 / max(qps, 0.1)
    fetched = 0
    lock = threading.Lock()
    counter = [0]

    def work(row: Any) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        od_key = str(row["od_key"])
        origin = (float(row["o_lng"]), float(row["o_lat"]))
        dest = (float(row["d_lng"]), float(row["d_lat"]))

        result: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        if not offline and origin != (0.0, 0.0) and dest != (0.0, 0.0):
            result = fetch_driving(origin, dest)
            # 合理性校验（双保险）：高德对虚构网点名会字面匹配到别的城市，
            # 实测"北京光华散货分拣中心"被安到惠州市，路线算出 2100 km。
            #   ① 同城校验：同城市的 OD 不可能是几百上千公里；
            #   ② 倍数校验：路线距离超过实测里程的 6 倍 → 明显错配。
            # 命中的路线作废，回退离线估算——**宁可粗一点，也不给错路线**。
            if result is not None and _implausible(result, row):
                result = None
碳慧智算物流碳感知智能决策系统 V1.0    第 398 页
        if result is None:
            result = offline_route(origin, dest)
        if not result:
            # 坐标缺失 → 不编数字：距离留空，交由引擎层回退宽表实测里程
            result = {
                "distance_km": None,
                "duration_min": None,
                "toll_yuan": 0.0,
                "traffic_lights": None,
                "strategy": None,
                "polyline": "",
                "source": "missing_coord",
                "flag_route_est": 1,
            }

        record = {
            "od_key": od_key,
            "od_site": str(row.get("od_site", od_key)),
            "od_pair": str(row["od_pair"]),
            "origin": str(row["origin"]),
            "dest": str(row["dest"]),
            "o_lng": origin[0], "o_lat": origin[1],
            "d_lng": dest[0], "d_lat": dest[1],
            "o_precise": str(row.get("o_precise", "")),
            "d_precise": str(row.get("d_precise", "")),
            "distance_km": result["distance_km"],
            "duration_min": result["duration_min"],
            "toll_yuan": result["toll_yuan"],
            "traffic_lights": result["traffic_lights"],
            "strategy": result["strategy"],
            "polyline": result["polyline"],
            "source": result["source"],
            "flag_route_est": result["flag_route_est"],
            "fetched_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        }
        with lock:
            counter[0] += 1
            n = counter[0]
            if n % 40 == 0 or n == len(todo):
                online = sum(1 for v in existing.values() if v["source"] == "amap_rest")
                print("       %d/%d（在线 %d）" % (n, len(todo), online))
        return od_key, record

    # 并发抓路：高德个人 key 的 QPS 远够用，6 路并发把 310 条从 ~5 分钟压到 ~40 秒
    if not offline:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max(1, workers)) as pool:
            for od_key, record in pool.map(work, todo):
                existing[od_key] = record
    else:
        for row in todo:
